<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037555' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037555' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.digitalfoundry.net/news/2026/05/valve-releases-steam-controller-cad-files-under-creative-commons-license' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valve releases Steam Controller CAD files under Creative Commons license</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valve 将 Steam 控制器 CAD 文件以 Creative Commons 许可发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1717</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 584</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037555' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve released the Steam Controller&#x27;s CAD files under a Creative Commons license, enabling makers to study, modify, or 3D print its components. This broadens access to hardware designs and could spur community-driven mods, repairs, and derivative hardware. The takeaway is that open hardware files lower barriers to tinkering and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 将 Steam 控制器的 CAD 文件以 Creative Commons 授权发布，允许开发者研究、修改乃至3D打印相关部件。此举扩大了硬件设计的获取渠道，可能促成社区驱动的改造、维修与衍生硬件。核心意义在于开源硬件设计降低了再加工的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.digitalfoundry.net/19e3ca4a25c2c/large.jpg' data-full='https://images.digitalfoundry.net/19e3ca4a25c2c/large.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48019219' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/chrome-silent-nano-install/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Chrome silently installs a 4 GB AI model on your device without consent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌 Chrome 未经同意在设备上静默下载 4GB AI 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1691</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48019219' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome quietly downloads a 4GB AI model to devices without user consent, raising storage and privacy risks as silent payloads become more common. This underscores the need for transparent controls and clear opt-out options to protect user autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 未经用户同意在设备上静默下载一个 4GB 的 AI 模型，增添了存储和隐私方面的潜在风险。此举凸显对静默下载的透明度、可控性以及用户撤销的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/assets/2026-05-04-001.png' data-full='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/assets/2026-05-04-001.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48038001' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48038001' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nooneshappy.com/article/appearing-productive-in-the-workplace/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Appearing productive in the workplace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在职场中营造高效形象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1570</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 636</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038001' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People can project productivity through visible habits and communication signals at work. The piece argues that appearance can influence collaboration and advancement, but genuine impact remains essential. For engineers and tech teams, the takeaway is to align presentation with real value by delivering tangible results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论通过可见的工作习惯和沟通信号来塑造高效形象。它认为外观的专业性会影响协作与晋升，但真正的成效仍然关键。对工程师而言，核心在于让自我认知与实际产出保持一致。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48067119' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48067119' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/google-broke-recaptcha-for-de-googled-android-users' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google broke reCAPTCHA for de-googled Android users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌对去谷歌化 Android 用户的 reCAPTCHA 失效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1491</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 552</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48067119' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report claims reCAPTCHA no longer functions on de-Googled Android devices, highlighting how privacy-focused setups can clash with anti-bot protections. The piece raises questions about platform control, user privacy, and the trade-offs of relying on third-party verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称去谷歌化 Android 设备上的 reCAPTCHA 功能不再工作，凸显隐私保护设置与反机器人机制之间的冲突。文章提出对平台控制、用户隐私以及依赖第三方验证所带来的权衡的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://reclaimthenet.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/BAPiBKSUNDTJ-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://reclaimthenet.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/BAPiBKSUNDTJ-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48007438' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48007438' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thienantran.com/talking-to-35-strangers-at-the-gym/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Talking to strangers at the gym</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在健身房与35位陌生人交流</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48007438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1442</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48007438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 702</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48007438' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece recounts the author&#x27;s experiment of striking up conversations with 35 strangers at the gym. It distills practical tips for networking, improvisation, and building social capital in everyday spaces. The takeaway is that meaningful connections can emerge from intentional, real-world small-talk moments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在健身房与35位陌生人交谈的个人实验，记录了公共场合交流的规律。文章总结了在日常场景中提升社交能力的实用要点，如主动开场、倾听与建立人脉的价值，同时也提醒在边界设定与自我保护方面的注意。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thienantran.com/_astro/30-gym-reddit.BhCuREGi.png' data-full='https://thienantran.com/_astro/30-gym-reddit.BhCuREGi.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48054423' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/world-at-work/cloudflare-cut-over-1100-jobs-2026-05-07/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare to cut about 20% of its workforce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 将裁员约 20%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48054423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48054423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 946</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48054423' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare plans to cut about 1,100 jobs, roughly 20% of its staff, as part of a strategic restructuring amid macro pressures. The move reflects broader tech layoff trends and the tension between growth, profitability, and the resilience of critical internet infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 将裁员约 20%，涉及约 1100 名员工，作为战略重组的一部分。此举反映出科技行业的裁员潮以及在维持关键互联网基础设施的同时寻求盈利与增长的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48086190' data-tags='Hardware,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48086190' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116550899908879585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardware Attestation as Monopoly Enabler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硬件认证成垄断推动者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48086190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48086190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 398</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48086190' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hardware attestation mechanisms verify device state to software and remote services, enabling trusted-environment features. When the attestation is controlled by a single vendor, it can lock users into that ecosystem and hinder competition. The takeaway is a push for interoperable, transparent standards to prevent security from becoming a monopoly lever.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>硬件认证机制用于向软件和远程服务验证设备状态，支持可信执行环境等功能。若认证机制被单一厂商掌控，可能将用户锁定在该生态中，削弱竞争。核心信息是推动互操作、透明的认证标准，以防安全被用于垄断工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48062117' data-tags='Business,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48062117' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/poland-economy-growth-g20-gdp-26fe06e120398410f8d773ba5661e7aa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Poland is now among the 20 largest economies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>波兰现已跻身全球前二十大经济体：原因何在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1034</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 837</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48062117' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Poland has entered the global top 20 economies by GDP, driven by resilient domestic demand, investment, and productivity gains from reforms and EU funding. This shift reshapes European dynamics and could influence policy in energy, labor, and technology sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>波兰凭借国内需求韧性、投资增长以及改革与欧盟资金带来的生产力提升，GDP规模跃入全球前二十。此举重新塑造了欧洲格局，未来在能源、劳动力与科技领域的政策走向值得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/909dda7/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4923x3280+3+0/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fe4%2Fdf%2F057b32831d24160d51408147c477%2F88cbd023c7c44085abb11287ab911ccf' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/909dda7/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4923x3280+3+0/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fe4%2Fdf%2F057b32831d24160d51408147c477%2F88cbd023c7c44085abb11287ab911ccf' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48055913' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48055913' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/926458/canvas-shinyhunters-breach' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canvas online again as ShinyHunters threatens to leak schools’ data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Canvas 在线再次上线，ShinyHunters 威胁泄露学校数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48055913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48055913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 615</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48055913' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canvas LMS came back online after a disruption tied to a ShinyHunters threat to leak school data. The incident highlights how education platforms remain high-value extortion targets, underscoring the need for rapid containment and stronger data protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canvas 在 ShinyHunters 的勒索威胁下重新上线。此事凸显教育平台成为高价值勒索目标，强调快速遏制和加强数据保护的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25332725/STK419_DEEPFAKE_CVIRGINIA_L.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25332725/STK419_DEEPFAKE_CVIRGINIA_L.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48085821' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48085821' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unix.foo/posts/local-ai-needs-to-be-norm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local AI needs to be the norm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地 AI 应成为常态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 849</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48085821' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The push toward on-device, local AI is moving from novelty to necessity as privacy, latency, and data governance become real constraints for organizations. This shift demands tooling and standards for model updates, secure storage, and offline operation across devices and endpoints. Expect more investment in edge hardware, federated learning, and open models that respect user data sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向本地化 AI 迁移正在从新鲜事变成必要趋势，隐私、延迟和数据治理成为现实约束，促使在各设备端部署和离线运行模型。需要完善的更新、安全存储和跨设备协同的工具与标准。这将推动对边缘硬件、联邦学习以及尊重数据主权的开源模型的投入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unix.foo/images/brutalist_ios_main.png' data-full='https://unix.foo/images/brutalist_ios_main.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cp3pww9g0p5o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>David Attenborough&#x27;s 100th Birthday</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 837</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48061884' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>David Attenborough&#x27;s centennial marks a landmark career in nature documentary, blending storytelling with scientific communication. The milestone underscores the enduring role of nature programming in science literacy and global environmental awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大卫·艾登堡百岁生日标志着他在自然纪录片领域的里程碑式职业生涯，将叙事与科普结合，持续推动科学素养与全球环境意识。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48056227' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xeiaso.net/blog/2026/abstain-from-install/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maybe you shouldn&#x27;t install new software for a bit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>也许你暂时不该安装新软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 830</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 446</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056227' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues against rushing to install new software, urging readers to pause updates while evaluating risk and security implications. Practical tips include staged deployments, prioritizing critical fixes, and verifying supply-chain integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张暂缓安装新软件以降低风险，敦促在评估风险与安全影响后再决策更新。文中给出阶段性部署、优先修复关键问题以及核验供应链完整性的做法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48053203' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48053203' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rmoff.net/2026/05/06/ai-slop-is-killing-online-communities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI slop is killing online communities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 低质内容正在侵蚀在线社区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 816</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 707</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48053203' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated content is flooding forums and comment sections, diluting signal and user trust. Moderation becomes harder as automated posts mimic human behavior, prompting calls for better guidelines, tooling, and community norms. The piece argues for proactive design choices to preserve healthy discourse online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成的内容大量涌入论坛和评论区，削弱了信息质量和社区信任。随着自动化发帖越来越像真人，社区治理更具挑战性，呼吁更好的准则、工具和治理机制来维护健康讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48053623' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48053623' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/05/07/8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dirty Frag: Universal Linux LPE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dirty Frag：通用 Linux 本地提权漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 803</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 315</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48053623' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dirty Frag reveals a broad local privilege escalation path affecting many Linux systems. The exploit enables an unprivileged user to gain elevated rights, increasing the risk of container escapes and system compromise; applying the latest kernel and userland patches is urgent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dirty Frag 揭示了影响大量 Linux 系统的广泛本地提权路径。该漏洞让普通用户能够提升权限，增加容器逃逸和系统被攻破的风险，赶紧应用最新内核及相关组件的补丁势在必行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' data-full='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.drive.com.au/news/mercedes-benz-commits-to-bringing-back-phycial-buttons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mercedes-Benz commits to bringing back physical buttons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>梅赛德斯-奔驰承诺重新回归物理按钮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 802</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 469</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47997418' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercedes-Benz plans to reintroduce physical knobs and tactile controls, signaling a shift away from touchscreen-dominated interiors toward familiar, tactile interaction. The move could influence UX standards in premium cars and inform future cabin design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梅赛德斯-奔驰计划重新引入物理旋钮和实体按钮，强调从以触控为主的内饰向更直觉、可触感交互回归。此举可能影响高端车的用户体验标准，并引导未来座舱设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037128' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037128' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/May/6/vibe-coding-and-agentic-engineering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vibe coding and agentic engineering are getting closer than I&#x27;d like</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vibe 编程与代理工程比我想象的更接近</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 772</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 865</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037128' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vibe coding and agentic engineering are narrowing the gap between intent and autonomous AI action. The piece argues that small UX choices and prompt engineering can push AI toward more capable, goal-driven behavior, raising safety and control concerns for developers. Takeaway: balancing usability with robust safeguards is becoming harder as agents get more capable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vibe 编程和代理工程让人类意图更易转化为自主AI行动，作者认为微小的用户体验设计和提示工程可能推动 AI 走向更强的目标驱动行为，对开发者的安全与控制提出挑战。要点是：在追求易用性的同时，保持强健的防护越来越困难。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://red-squares.cian.lol/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Red Squares – GitHub outages as contributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Red Squares 将 GitHub 故障变成贡献</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48034587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 748</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48034587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48034587' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Red Squares turns outage data into contributions, offering a playful, data-driven way to visualize GitHub reliability events in a public project. It sparks ideas for incident-aware dashboards and community-driven reliability metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Red Squares 将故障数据转化为贡献，以数据驱动的方式在公开项目中可视化 GitHub 可靠性事件，激发对基于事件的仪表板和社区驱动的可靠性指标的探索。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48027897' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48027897' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.denic.de/pages/incident/592577eab611ce1e0d00046f/69fa60ef9d12f5057a974f38' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DNSSEC disruption affecting .de domains – Resolved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.de 域名的 DNSSEC 中断已解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48027897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 740</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48027897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 405</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48027897' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DNSSEC validation disruption briefly interrupted secure resolution for .de domains, affecting trust chain verification across German sites. The incident has been resolved; operators are advised to re-validate DNSSEC records and monitor for lingering validation issues. This underscores how crypto-based DNS protections, while powerful, require careful operational hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段 DNSSEC 验证中断曾影响 .de 域名的安全解析，现已解决。事件凸显了基于加密的 DNS 保护需要严格的运维与监控以确保信任链的稳定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.status.io/HtSLcvSAq94M.jpg' data-full='https://image.status.io/HtSLcvSAq94M.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48073201' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48073201' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://fourlightyears.blogspot.com/2026/05/i-returned-to-aws-and-was-reminded-hard.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I returned to AWS and was reminded why I left</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我回到 AWS，被提醒了为何离开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 707</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 510</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073201' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A first-hand account of returning to AWS exposure to cost, vendor lock-in, or outages, highlighting the tradeoffs of cloud choices. The post contrasts AWS with other cloud approaches and stresses the importance of cost management and operational simplicity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回到 AWS 的体验再次提醒他为何离开，涉及成本、锁定和运维复杂性等问题。文章对比了 AWS 与其他云方案，强调成本控制与简化运维的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://resources.blogblog.com/img/icon18_edit_allbkg.gif' data-full='https://resources.blogblog.com/img/icon18_edit_allbkg.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48016880' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48016880' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/oven-sh/bun/commit/46d3bc29f270fa881dd5730ef1549e88407701a5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig → Rust porting guide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig 到 Rust 的端口移植指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48016880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48016880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 532</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48016880' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines the approach for porting Bun from Zig to Rust, highlighting architectural decisions and milestones to preserve performance. It signals a broader interest in rewriting critical runtimes in Rust to improve safety and maintainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文概述将 Bun 的核心从 Zig 迁移到 Rust 的方法论，强调架构决策、里程碑与在保持高性能的同时提升安全性的要点。它体现了在关键运行时中以 Rust 提高可维护性与稳定性的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/71b057188fb96b3e204004bda82c56574fc44a5f6c7958772ae6d55f18d93afb/oven-sh/bun/commit/46d3bc29f270fa881dd5730ef1549e88407701a5' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/71b057188fb96b3e204004bda82c56574fc44a5f6c7958772ae6d55f18d93afb/oven-sh/bun/commit/46d3bc29f270fa881dd5730ef1549e88407701a5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48063199' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48063199' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://privatecaptcha.com/blog/google-cloud-fraud-defence-wei/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Cloud Fraud Defence is just WEI repackaged</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Cloud Fraud Defence 实为 WEI 的再包装</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 695</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 354</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48063199' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud Fraud Defence appears to be a repackaged Web Environment Integrity solution. It signals a push to apply browser-attestation concepts to cloud fraud, but raises questions about privacy, cross-platform effectiveness, and how it will be validated in real-world workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud Fraud Defence 看似把 Web Environment Integrity 技术打包成云端反欺诈服务。这一动向将浏览器端的认证思想带入云环境，但也引发隐私、跨设备有效性和实际场景落地的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://privatecaptcha.com/images/pc-logo-dark.svg' data-full='https://privatecaptcha.com/images/pc-logo-dark.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48073680' data-tags='Programming,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48073680' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jarredsumner/status/2053047748191232310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bun&#x27;s experimental Rust rewrite hits 99.8% test compatibility on Linux x64 glibc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bun 的 Rust 重写在 Linux x64 glibc 上实现 99.8% 的测试兼容性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 690</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 670</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073680' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun&#x27;s experimental Rust rewrite aims to improve startup and performance for the JavaScript runtime. Hitting 99.8% test compatibility on Linux x64 glibc signals strong parity with the current build, though a small gap remains. If realized, the rewrite could influence Bun&#x27;s future architecture and ecosystem adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 的实验性 Rust 重写旨在提升 JavaScript 运行时的启动速度与性能。在 Linux x64 glibc 上达到 99.8% 的测试兼容性，表明与现有实现的对齐性很高，但仍存在小幅差距。这一进展可能影响 Bun 的未来架构选择和生态系统的采用路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HH3kDB4b0AA6w5C.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HH3kDB4b0AA6w5C.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48006402' data-tags='Business,Finance,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48006402' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cn0p8yled1do' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GameStop makes $55.5B takeover offer for eBay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GameStop 对 eBay 提出 555 亿美元的收购要约</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 688</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 656</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48006402' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GameStop has reportedly offered to acquire eBay for about $55.5 billion, a bid that would dramatically reshape a major online marketplace and expand GameStop’s diversification beyond physical stores. The proposal highlights an appetite for large-scale consolidation in tech-enabled retail, potentially drawing antitrust scrutiny and signaling investor interest in combining community-driven marketplaces with scale. If pursued, it could redefine how marketplace ecosystems co-evolve around collectibles and broader commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 GameStop 向 eBay 提出约 555 亿美元的收购要约，这一交易将大幅改变全球大型线上市场格局，并扩展 GameStop 的多元化布局。此举显示了对科技驱动零售领域进行大规模并购的热情，可能引发反垄断审查，并揭示投资者对将社区型市场与规模效应结合的兴趣。若成行，或将重塑收藏品与更广泛商业生态的协同发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/0759/live/6f3aea00-474a-11f1-b55d-0f258dce1735.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/0759/live/6f3aea00-474a-11f1-b55d-0f258dce1735.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48071262' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48071262' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gowers.wordpress.com/2026/05/08/a-recent-experience-with-chatgpt-5-5-pro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A recent experience with ChatGPT 5.5 Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最近使用 ChatGPT 5.5 Pro 的体验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48071262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48071262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 519</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48071262' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firsthand take on ChatGPT 5.5 Pro, noting noticeable speed gains and steadier responses, plus improved handling of long conversations. Highlights advantages for developers and power users, but cautions that accuracy can still slip and prompts require careful framing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分享了对 ChatGPT 5.5 Pro 的最新使用感受，指出响应更快、对话更连贯，长上下文处理也有所改善。强调这对开发者和高阶用户有实用价值，但仍需警惕输出的不准确性，并在使用时保持批判性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48024540' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48024540' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/multi-token-prediction-gemma-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Accelerating Gemma 4: faster inference with multi-token prediction drafters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加速 Gemma 4：通过多令牌预测提升推理速度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48024540' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 unlocks faster inference by enabling multi-token prediction drafts. The approach lets the model draft several tokens in parallel, reducing latency on long-context tasks. It signals a shift toward token-level parallelism in scalable AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌的 Gemma 4 通过实现多令牌预测草拟来提升推理速度。该方法允许同时预测若干令牌，从而降低长上下文任务的延迟。标志着在可扩展的 AI 部署中向令牌级并行性的转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Share_Image_7bOA183.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Share_Image_7bOA183.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.stvn.sh/writing/programming-still-sucks-fqffhyp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Programming Still Sucks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编程仍然很糟糕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 664</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48040269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative essay argues that programming remains challenging due to fragmentation, tool churn, and misaligned incentives, urging improvements in tooling, processes, and collaboration to ease developers&#x27; pain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇挑衅性文章认为编程仍然困难，原因包括工具生态碎片化、工具更新换代频繁以及激励机制错配。作者提出通过改进工具链、流程与协作来缓解开发者痛苦的建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48031261' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48031261' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://us.starlabs.systems/pages/starfighter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>StarFighter 16-Inch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>StarFighter 16英寸笔记本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 660</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 371</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031261' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StarFighter 16-Inch marks Star Labs&#x27; new 16-inch Linux-friendly laptop lineup, offering improved display, thermals, and battery life for developers and power users. The device continues Star Labs’ focus on open hardware and Linux-first design, appealing to enthusiasts who want a ready-to-use Linux machine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StarFighter 16英寸笔记本展示了 Star Labs 面向 Linux 的新旗舰。新机在显示、散热与续航方面有显著提升，符合开放硬件与 Linux 优先的定位，适合开发者与高阶用户。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://us.starlabs.systems/cdn/shop/files/Star_6c731e1d-b2c1-4de1-a872-7081e0b5d878.png?v=1762248375' data-full='http://us.starlabs.systems/cdn/shop/files/Star_6c731e1d-b2c1-4de1-a872-7081e0b5d878.png?v=1762248375' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48031684' data-tags='DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48031684' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/agents-stripe-projects/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agents can now create Cloudflare accounts, buy domains, and deploy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理现在可创建 Cloudflare 账户、购买域名并完成部署</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 640</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031684' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare&#x27;s Agents can now perform end-to-end provisioning: create accounts, buy domains, and deploy projects automatically. This accelerates onboarding for developers and MSP-like workflows, but also raises security and governance questions around automated provisioning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 的 Agents 现在能够实现端到端的自动化：自动创建账户、购买域名并部署项目。这将加速开发者与托管工作流的上线速度，但也带来自动化权限与域名管理的安全与治理挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/1z87ypuPVuagcw4apgVWEz/32925c589f5c49ea212c2b12359c3efc/BLOG-3288-OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/1z87ypuPVuagcw4apgVWEz/32925c589f5c49ea212c2b12359c3efc/BLOG-3288-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48042434' data-tags='Open Source,Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48042434' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sqlite.org/locrsf.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SQLite Is a Library of Congress Recommended Storage Format</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQLite 成为美国国会图书馆推荐的存储格式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48042434' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SQLite is now recommended by the Library of Congress as a storage format for digital preservation. The format is lightweight, self-contained, and portable, making it attractive for archiving, but it may not scale or handle concurrent workloads like larger DB systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SQLite 获得美国国会图书馆数字保存推荐的存储格式称号，因其体积小、文件自包含、跨平台且易于迁移。此举使 SQLite 成为长期档案的一种实用且易于访问的格式，但并非适用于所有工作负载，尤其在并发和大规模扩展方面仍有局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sqlite.org/images/sqlite370_banner.svg' data-full='https://sqlite.org/images/sqlite370_banner.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47977026' data-tags='Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977026' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/24/deepseek-v4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek V4 – almost on the frontier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek V4：几近前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 635</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 374</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 pushes closer to the frontier of data-search tooling, with improvements in indexing speed and user workflow. It frames how this version stacks up against peers and what the next leap in AI-assisted data discovery might look like.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 靠近数据检索工具的前沿，在索引速度和工作流方面有显著改进。它评估了该版本与同类产品的差异以及AI辅助数据发现的下一步可能走向。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48002136' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/aattaran/deepclaude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepClaude – Claude Code agent loop with DeepSeek V4 Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepClaude：结合 DeepSeek V4 Pro 的 Claude 代码代理循环，成本低至原来的 1/17</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 625</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002136' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents DeepClaude, a Claude-based code agent loop powered by DeepSeek V4 Pro, claiming a 17x reduction in cost. Highlights potential gains for automated coding tasks and iterative AI-assisted development with cheaper inference and faster cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍基于 Claude 的代码代理循环 DeepClaude，搭载 DeepSeek V4 Pro，成本降低约 17 倍。强调在自动编码任务和 AI 辅助开发中的实际收益，如更低的推理成本和更快的迭代速度。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48050964' data-tags='Privacy,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48050964' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/chrome/comments/1t5qayz/chrome_removes_claim_of_ondevice_al_not_sending/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome removes claim of On-device Al not sending data to Google Servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 撤回‘本地 AI 不发送数据给 Google 服务器’的声明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050964' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome has updated its messaging around on-device AI privacy, removing the claim that on-device processing never transmits data to Google servers. The change underscores ongoing tensions between user privacy, telemetry, and model updates. For users and developers, it highlights the importance of transparent data practices in browser-hosted AI features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 更新了关于本地化 AI 隐私的表述，撤回了“本地处理不向 Google 服务器发送数据”的说法。这一变动凸显了用户隐私、遥测和模型更新之间的持续博弈，并提醒开发者和用户关注浏览器 AI 功能中的数据透明度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48012735' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48012735' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/L1v1ng0ffTh3L4N/status/2051308329880719730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Edge stores all passwords in memory in clear text, even when unused</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软 Edge 将所有密码明文存储在内存中，即使未使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012735' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This exposes a security risk: Edge stores passwords in memory as plaintext, even when not in use. That design choice increases the risk of credential leaks via memory dumps or malicious processes. It underscores why secure by design and minimizing in-memory secrets matters for browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这暴露了浏览器设计中的安全隐患：Edge 将凭证以明文存储在内存中，即使未被使用时也可能暴露。此设计增加了通过内存转储或恶意进程获取凭据的风险。再次强调从设计层面减少内存中敏感信息的重要性，以及对浏览器安全的长期关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2051308082500681729/img/txKW5u6WctKrftAv.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2051308082500681729/img/txKW5u6WctKrftAv.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48062178' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48062178' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sinceyouarrived.world/taken' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A web page that shows you everything the browser told it without asking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个网页在未征询许可的情况下透露浏览器所有信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48062178' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web page demonstrates exactly what data a site can read from your browser without explicit permission. It highlights passive data leakage and fingerprinting risks, reinforcing the case for stricter sandboxing, fewer default disclosures, and privacy-focused browsing defaults.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个网页演示网站在未获得明确同意的情况下能够读取的浏览器信息全貌，凸显被动数据泄露和指纹识别风险。此事强调需要更严格的沙箱、降低默认披露、以及更隐私导向的浏览器设置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sinceyouarrived.world/api/og-taken' data-full='https://sinceyouarrived.world/api/og-taken' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48072190' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cyberinsider.com/eu-calls-vpns-a-loophole-that-needs-closing-in-age-verification-push/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Parliamentary Research Service calls VPNs &quot;a loophole that needs closing&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国推动破解端对端加密通讯</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48072190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 599</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48072190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 411</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48072190' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France considers measures that could weaken or undermine encrypted messaging as part of a broader digital regulation push, signaling renewed regulatory scrutiny of end-to-end encryption and its privacy vs. safety implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国在打击犯罪和国家安全背景下，拟采取措施削弱或破坏端对端加密通讯，显示出对加密的再度监管压力。这引发对隐私、公共安全和创新的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48051562' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48051562' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/agents-need-control-flow/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agents need control flow, not more prompts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理需要控制流，而非更多提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48051562' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prompt-based thinking has plateaued for coordinating multi-step AI agents. The piece argues that explicit control flow, state machines, and orchestration offer reliability and debuggability that prompts can&#x27;t. As AI agents tackle more complex workflows, control-flow design becomes essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用提示词来指挥多步代理的局限性逐渐显现。文章主张通过显式控制流、状态机和任务编排来提升可靠性与可调试性；当代理处理更复杂的工作流时，控制流设计显得至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48009697' data-tags='Hardware,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48009697' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ecopv-eu.com/en/blog-en/replaceable-smartphone-batteries-2027-eu-regulation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Removable batteries in smartphones will be mandatory in the EU starting in 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟自 2027 年起强制智能手机使用可拆卸电池</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 526</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48009697' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU rules will require removable batteries in smartphones starting in 2027 to improve repairability and reduce e-waste. Regulators push for standardization and accessible spare parts, likely impacting manufacturer design choices and supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟自 2027 年起强制智能手机使用可拆卸电池，以提升可修复性并降低电子垃圾。规定推动标准化和易于采购的备用电池，将影响厂商的设计与供应链策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ecopv-eu.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/akkus-smartphone_en.jpg' data-full='https://www.ecopv-eu.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/akkus-smartphone_en.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48006445' data-tags='Legal,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48006445' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notepad-plus-plus.org/news/npp-trademark-infringement/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trademark violation: Fake Notepad++ for Mac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>商标侵权：伪装成 Mac 版的 Notepad++</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 568</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48006445' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notepad++&#x27;s team warns of a counterfeit Mac app that infringes the project’s trademark, potentially confusing users and exposing them to risk. This highlights how trademark enforcement matters in software distribution and the fragility of brand trust in app stores and unofficial channels. Developers should monitor forks and counterfeit bundles that piggyback on popular open-source projects to protect their ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Notepad++ 团队警告有一款伪 Mac 应用侵犯其商标，可能混淆用户并带来安全风险。这凸显了软件分发中商标保护的重要性，尤其在应用商店之外的生态中品牌信任容易受损。开发者应警惕山寨版本对开源项目生态的侵蚀并采取监控措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://notepad-plus-plus.org/images/logo.svg' data-full='https://notepad-plus-plus.org/images/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsbuyspiritair.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s Buy Spirit Air</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let&#x27;s Buy Spirit Air</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 539</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个宣传性页面，促使读者购买 Spirit Air，但具体产品背景尚不清晰。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48006967' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48006967' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thetypicalset.com/blog/thoughts-on-coding-agents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bottleneck was never the code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瓶颈从来不是代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 363</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48006967' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that the real bottleneck in building effective AI agents isn’t code but the orchestration, data quality, and decision-making loops that feed them. It stresses that tooling, memory, and integration with real-world data often limit performance more than algorithms. Takeaway: invest in systems design and data pipelines, not just faster code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为构建高效 AI 代理的瓶颈并非代码本身，而是编排、数据质量与决策循环等要素。强调工具链、内存管理以及与真实数据的集成往往比算法本身更能决定成效。要点是：应更多投资于系统设计和数据管线，而非单纯追求更快的代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thetypicalset.com/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://thetypicalset.com/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48022742' data-tags='Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48022742' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idiallo.com/blog/ai-didnt-delete-your-database-you-did' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI didn&#x27;t delete your database, you did</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 没有删除你的数据库，是你删的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48022742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48022742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48022742' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that data loss often results from human error or misconfigurations rather than AI mishaps, calling for stronger backups, robust data governance, and clearer ownership of data workflows. It emphasizes responsibility on operators and teams rather than blaming tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为数据丢失往往源自人为错误或配置失误，而非 AI 的失误，呼吁加强备份、健全的数据治理以及对数据工作流的明确责任。强调将责任落到运营团队而非单纯指责工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.idiallo.com/images/assets/652/hero.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.idiallo.com/images/assets/652/hero.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48023861' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48023861' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://susam.net/inverse-laws-of-robotics.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three Inverse Laws of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的三条逆向律</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48023861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48023861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48023861' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three inverse laws of AI propose that as AI grows more capable, governance should tighten; interpretability remains essential for trust; and expectations should be tempered by practical limits. The piece uses humorous framing to underscore real-world constraints in safety and deployment. It invites readers to rethink AI governance alongside capability gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出三条 AI 的逆向法则：随着能力提升，治理需要加紧；可解释性对建立信任至关重要；对现实边界应持保留态度。以诙谐的笔法强调了现实世界在安全与落地中的约束，鼓励在能力增长的同时重新审视治理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48084432' data-tags='Open Source,Culture,Hardware,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>路易斯·罗斯曼愿为遭威胁的 OrcaSlicer 开发者支付诉讼费</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48084432' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Louis Rossmann offers to cover legal fees for a threatened OrcaSlicer developer, signaling high-profile support for open hardware and repair activism. The gesture highlights ongoing tensions between manufacturers pursuing lawsuits and the enthusiast community defending rights to repair and independent tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>路易斯·罗斯曼愿为遭遇威胁的 OrcaSlicer 开发者支付诉讼费，显示对开放硬件与修理倡议的强力支持。这一举动凸显了厂商诉讼与热衷社区捍卫修理权和独立工具之间的持续紧张关系。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/04/us-healthcare-marketplaces-shared-citizenship-and-race-data-with-ad-tech-giants/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US healthcare marketplaces shared citizenship and race data with ad tech giants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国医疗保健市场将公民身份和种族等数据分享给广告科技巨头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48011689' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report reveals that US healthcare marketplaces shared sensitive citizenship and race data with ad tech giants, raising privacy and regulatory concerns. The finding underscores the need for stricter data governance and transparency in how health data is used outside medical contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示美国医疗保健市场将公民身份与种族等敏感数据分享给广告科技巨头，引发隐私与监管担忧。这凸显了在健康数据用于非医疗场景时对数据治理与透明度的严格要求。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48011184' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post questions Bun&#x27;s maturity and ecosystem maturity, highlighting concerns about stability, long-term viability, and ecosystem readiness compared to more established runtimes. It urges readers to assess risk before deep adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文质疑 Bun 的成熟度和生态系统的稳定性，关注相对于更成熟的运行时其长期可行性与生态就绪度。建议读者在深度采用前评估风险。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48076173' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chrismorgan.info/no-query-strings' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I’ve banned query strings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我已禁止查询字符串</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48076173' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer bans query strings to improve cacheability and determinism in URLs. This approach reduces cache fragmentation and enables more aggressive CDN caching, though it requires rethinking how parameters and analytics are handled. The post shares practical steps and trade-offs from their experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者宣布禁用查询字符串以提升 URL 的缓存性和确定性。这一做法减少了缓存碎片，使 CDN 能更有效地缓存资源，但需要重新考虑参数和分析信息的处理方式。文中分享了基于其经验的实践要点与取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48047981' data-tags='Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48047981' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paulkrugman.substack.com/p/grand-theft-oil-futures' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grand Theft Oil Futures: Insider traders keep making a killing at our expense</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巨额油价期货内幕交易者继续坐收利润</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 499</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 322</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48047981' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Krugman argues that insiders keep profiting from oil futures, extracting wealth at the public&#x27;s expense due to market structure and regulatory gaps. The essay highlights how information advantages and opaque pricing enable rent extraction, and it calls for policy remedies to curb excess profits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，油价期货市场的内幕交易者持续从中获利，市场结构和监管漏洞使公众承受成本。文章揭示信息优势与定价不透明如何促成利润攫取，并呼吁通过政策改革来遏制这类过度获利。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/delivering-low-latency-voice-ai-at-scale/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How OpenAI delivers low-latency voice AI at scale</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 介绍在大规模场景下实现低延迟语音 AI 的设计思路，涵盖流式推理、优化策略及跨规模部署的要点，为构建可扩展的实时语音应用提供实用洞见。</p>
