<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48061566' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48061566' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://meshtastic.org/docs/introduction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Introduction to Meshtastic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meshtastic 入门指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48061566' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meshtastic is an open-source mesh-networking project that enables off-grid text messaging using low-power radios like LoRa. This introduction covers core concepts, components, and practical use cases, plus trade-offs in latency and range compared with cellular networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meshtastic 是一个开源网状通信项目，利用低功耗无线电（如 LoRa）在无蜂窝网络的情况下实现短讯传输。本文概述了核心概念、组成部分和典型用例，并讨论了与蜂窝网络相比的延迟和覆盖范围权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://meshtastic.org/img/logo.svg' data-full='https://meshtastic.org/img/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48071940' data-tags='AI,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48071940' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/trq212/status/2052809885763747935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using Claude Code: The unreasonable effectiveness of HTML</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Claude Code：HTML 的非同寻常高效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48071940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 489</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48071940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48071940' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code demonstrates that HTML&#x27;s simple, deterministic structure can yield reliable, quick wins for AI-assisted frontend tasks. HTML&#x27;s straightforward nature makes it a practical starting point for AI-driven UI prototyping and code generation. This highlights HTML as a durable substrate for AI copilots in frontend work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 展示了 HTML 的简单、确定性结构在 AI 辅助前端任务中能带来可靠且快速的收益。HTML 的直接性使其成为 AI 驱动的 UI 原型和代码生成的实际起点。此现象凸显了 HTML 在前端工作中作为 AI 协作工具的持久价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHz_ftzaIAAwkQs.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHz_ftzaIAAwkQs.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47991981' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47991981' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/30/ai-outperforms-doctors-in-harvard-trial-of-emergency-triage-diagnoses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients vs. 50-55% by triage doctors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 o1 在急诊诊断中正确率达 67%，超出分诊医生的 50–55%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 437</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47991981' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a Harvard trial, OpenAI&#x27;s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients, compared with 50-55% by triage doctors. This demonstrates AI&#x27;s potential to augment emergency triage, but real-world deployment must address safety, workflow integration, and clinician oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在哈佛的一项试验中，o1 在急诊患者诊断中的正确率达到 67%，明显高于分诊医生的 50–55%。这显示人工智能有潜力提升急诊分诊的效率与准确性，但将其落地需解决安全性、工作流整合以及医生监督等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7f4b1062db43fb053f4d68c08b8e92dd39f49107/0_62_4362_3487/master/4362.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ceb8f56ddb65e715896d86461e95819f' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7f4b1062db43fb053f4d68c08b8e92dd39f49107/0_62_4362_3487/master/4362.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ceb8f56ddb65e715896d86461e95819f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48051951' data-tags='Web,AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48051951' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moq.dev/blog/webrtc-is-the-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s WebRTC problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 WebRTC 问题与挑战</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48051951' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI’s WebRTC challenges highlight the friction between real-time media needs and browser/network realities in AI-powered web apps. The issues point to where WebRTC adoption stalls and what developers might optimize—latency, security, and compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的 WebRTC 问题揭示 AI 驱动网页应用中实时媒体需求与浏览器/网络现实之间的摩擦。相关问题指向需要优化的领域，如延迟、安全性与兼容性，以及在实际应用中的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://moq.dev/blog/webrtc-is-the-problem/rtt.png' data-full='https://moq.dev/blog/webrtc-is-the-problem/rtt.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48050751' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48050751' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/antirez/ds4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek 4 Flash local inference engine for Metal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 4：面向 Metal 的本地推理引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050751' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 4 Flash runs as a local inference engine optimized for Apple&#x27;s Metal, enabling on-device ML workloads without cloud dependencies. It aims for fast, low-footprint inference suitable for edge apps and privacy-conscious users. The project highlights ongoing efforts to bring efficient ML at the user device level.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 4 作为面向 Apple Metal 的本地推理引擎，支持在设备上运行 ML 推理，避免云端依赖。目标是为边缘应用提供快速、低开销的推理，并强调对隐私的考虑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81cfc4881ef4ad3653f34ee37858e28367d14efb79212067e4aac6c3e9ea5ff5/antirez/ds4' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81cfc4881ef4ad3653f34ee37858e28367d14efb79212067e4aac6c3e9ea5ff5/antirez/ds4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48024859' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48024859' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reflex.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computer Use is 45x more expensive than structured APIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计算使用成本比结构化 API 高出 45 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48024859' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Computing for unstructured tasks can be 45x more expensive than using structured APIs. This highlights the cost benefits of API-based tooling and disciplined data orchestration for AI workloads. It nudges engineers to design pipelines around reliable interfaces rather than ad-hoc compute.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项分析称，处理非结构化任务的计算成本大约比使用结构化 API 高出 45 倍。这凸显了基于 API 的工作流在 AI 负载中的成本优势，促使开发者以稳定接口而非随意计算来设计数据处理管线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://web.reflex-assets.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis.webp' data-full='https://web.reflex-assets.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An incident report details a vulnerability tagged CVE-2024-YIKES and its exploitation path, affecting multiple systems before a patch was released. It highlights how attackers leveraged the flaw and the importance of rapid patching and layered defenses. The takeaway: keep dependencies current and strengthen incident response to reduce blast radius.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份事件报告披露了被标记为 CVE-2024-YIKES 的漏洞及其利用路径，影响了多套系统，直至修补程序发布。攻击者利用该漏洞进行入侵，凸显了快速打补丁和分层防御的重要性。关键点在于保持依赖项更新与强化事件响应以降低影响范围。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CARA 2.0 details improvements over the previous version, focusing on more capable locomotion, perception, and autonomous behaviors. It covers the hardware-software stack and practical lessons from building and testing a robot dog, highlighting real-world tradeoffs in power, reliability, and safety. <div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48021368' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/brian_armstrong/status/2051616759145185723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Today I&#x27;ve made the difficult decision to reduce the size of Coinbase by ~14%</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48021368' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CEO announces a roughly 14% workforce reduction to streamline operations and refocus on core products. The move signals ongoing consolidation in crypto infrastructure and underscores the cost discipline shaping tech startups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CEO 宣布将 Coinbase 的规模缩减约14%，以精简运营并聚焦核心产品。此举反映了加密基础设施领域持续整合的趋势，以及成本管控在科技初创公司中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47999636' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47999636' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://partyon.xyz/@nullagent/116499715071759135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BYOMesh – New LoRa mesh radio offers 100x the bandwidth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BYOMesh：新一代 LoRa 网状无线电带来 100 倍带宽</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 451</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999636' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BYOMesh launches a LoRa mesh radio claiming 100x the bandwidth of typical LoRa devices, enabling more data-rich IoT, firmware updates, and real-time telemetry over long ranges. The move highlights how higher-throughput needs are pushing mesh/LPWAN technology beyond traditional use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BYOMesh 推出声称比常规 LoRa 设备快 100 倍带宽的网状无线电，可能支持更高数据量的物联网、固件更新和远程遥测等。此举反映了在大范围低功耗网络里，对更高吞吐的需求正在推动新应用场景的产生。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://partyon.xyz/packs/media/images/logo-d4b5dc90fd3e117d141ae7053b157f58.svg' data-full='https://partyon.xyz/packs/media/images/logo-d4b5dc90fd3e117d141ae7053b157f58.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48073246' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48073246' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs corrupt your documents when you delegate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当你让大语言模型处理文档时，文档可能被污染</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 444</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073246' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper demonstrates that delegating document editing to LLMs can introduce subtle, hard-to-detect changes to content and structure. Such corruptions can affect citations, formatting, and data provenance, undermining automated workflows. It argues for stronger verification, provenance tooling, and robust evaluation when integrating LLMs into document pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文展示在将文档处理任务委托给大语言模型时，内容和结构可能出现细微且难以察觉的改变。这类污染会影响引用、格式和数据可追溯性，削弱自动化工作流的可信度。文章提出加强验证、溯源工具和在文档管线中引入鲁棒评估的建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48019163' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48019163' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tweedegolf.nl/en/blog/237/async-rust-never-left-the-mvp-state' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Async Rust never left the MVP state</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>异步 Rust 仍处于 MVP 阶段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 437</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48019163' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the async Rust ecosystem remains MVP-level, with immature tooling and stability for production use. It cautions developers to weigh risk and consider maturer alternatives or wait for ecosystem stabilization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为异步 Rust 的生态仍处于 MVP 水平，开发生产环境时工具链与稳定性尚不成熟。提醒开发者权衡风险，考虑更成熟的替代方案或等待生态稳定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.tweedegolf.nl/images/logo-1024.png' data-full='https://media.tweedegolf.nl/images/logo-1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48010301' aria-label='Show summary and image'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Issues 与 Webhooks 事件事件已解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 425</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48010301' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Status update confirms the incident affecting Issues and Webhooks has been resolved, detailing the root cause and remediation steps. It underscores the importance of rapid incident response and transparent communication with users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>状态更新确认 Issues 与 Webhooks 的故障已解决，给出根本原因与修复步骤。强调快速响应与对用户的透明沟通的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48066524' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48066524' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jefftk.com/p/ai-is-breaking-two-vulnerability-cultures' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is breaking two vulnerability cultures</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在打破两种漏洞文化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 419</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48066524' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is accelerating vulnerability research and patching, collapsing the old divide between offensive and defensive security cultures. The post argues AI tools change how bugs are discovered, validated, and fixed, urging teams to rethink disclosure, testing, and risk modeling in software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 正在加速漏洞研究与修复，打破了旧有的攻击者与防御者漏洞文化之分。AI 工具改变了漏洞的发现、验证与修复方式，促使团队重新审视披露、测试和风险建模等软件开发实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jefftk.com/jefftk-glyph-framed.jpg' data-full='https://www.jefftk.com/jefftk-glyph-framed.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48065360' data-tags='Web,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48065360' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.webdesignmuseum.org/flash-game-exhibitions/cartoon-network-flash-games' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cartoon Network Flash Games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卡通网络的 Flash 游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065360' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065360' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48065360' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on Cartoon Network&#x27;s Flash-era games, many of which are now part of digital heritage as browsers retire Flash.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾卡通网络在 Flash 时代推出的网页游戏，如今随着浏览器逐步淘汰 Flash，这些游戏成为数字遗产的一部分。文中强调游戏的艺术性和玩法对早期网络游戏文化的影响，以及为未来保存这些作品所进行的努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.webdesignmuseum.org/uploaded/exhibitions/cover-flash-games/cartoon-network-flash-games-preview.png' data-full='https://www.webdesignmuseum.org/uploaded/exhibitions/cover-flash-games/cartoon-network-flash-games-preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48077126' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/08/technology/meta-ai-employees-miserable.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta&#x27;s embrace of AI is making its employees miserable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 对 AI 的拥抱让员工感到痛苦</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48077126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48077126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 459</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48077126' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NYT reports that Meta&#x27;s push into AI is harming morale, with employees facing heightened expectations, reshuffles, and surveillance-like monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约时报报道指出，Meta 大力推进 AI 策略正在侵蚀员工士气，导致高压工作环境、职责调整和对绩效的强化监控。文章强调 AI 为先的路线图对员工福祉的代价，并可能带来留任挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 的生产工程团队在生产环境中管理着数百万行 Haskell 代码，展示了如何将 Haskell 规模化。文中讨论了工具链、可靠性实践，以及强类型语言在实际系统中的应用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48021561' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://walletwallet.alen.ro/blog/ios-27-wallet-create-pass/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iOS 27 is adding a &#x27;Create a Pass&#x27; button to Apple Wallet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>iOS 27 将在 Apple Wallet 增设“创建凭证”按钮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48021561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48021561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48021561' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple Wallet&#x27;s new Create a Pass button streamlines programmatic pass creation for tickets, loyalty cards, and IDs. Developers can automate credential generation at scale, reducing manual steps. The change raises questions about security, offline validity, and data integrity across pass ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apple Wallet 新增“创建凭证”按钮，使凭证的批量生成和管理更为自动化，覆盖票务、积分卡与身份凭证等场景。开发者可借此规模化生成凭证，显著减少人工步骤。此变动同时带来安全、离线有效性与跨生态数据完整性等考量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isene.org/2026/05/Audience-of-One.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A desktop made for one</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为一个人定制的桌面</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A desktop built for a single user emphasizes bespoke configurations, streamlined workflows, and software choices tailored to one person&#x27;s needs. The piece argues for systems optimized around individual requirements rather than generic, mass-market setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述为单一用户定制的桌面系统，强调定制化的配置、简化的工作流和专属的软件选择。观点在于以个人需求为中心的系统比大众化设置更高效。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48074952' data-tags='Privacy,Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48074952' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matduggan.com/the-intolerable-hypocrisy-of-cyberlibertarianism/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The hypocrisy of cyberlibertarianism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网络自由主义的虚伪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48074952' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique argues that cyberlibertarianism&#x27;s rhetoric about freedom often clashes with real-world power dynamics, surveillance, and corporate control. The piece calls for a more nuanced view that reconciles privacy with accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文指出网络自由主义者的自由论调往往与现实中的权力结构、监控与平台控制发生冲突，显露出虚伪之处。作者呼吁以更务实的视角看待隐私、自由与责任之间的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://matduggan.com/content/images/2026/05/image.png' data-full='https://matduggan.com/content/images/2026/05/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48060054' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48060054' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: We just had an actual UUID v4 collision...</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：我们刚刚遇到一次真正的 UUID v4 冲突</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48060054' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A real UUID v4 collision occurred, a surprising reminder that even with 122 random bits, edge cases happen. The incident prompts developers to consider UUID generation strategies, namespace-based or versioned schemes, and robust collision handling in distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次真正的 UUID v4 冲突发生，提醒即使有 122 位随机性也会出现极端情况。此事件促使开发者重新考虑 UUID 生成策略、命名空间或版本化方案，以及在分布式系统中对冲突的鲁棒处理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48039362' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48039362' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cloud.google.com/blog/products/identity-security/introducing-google-cloud-fraud-defense-the-next-evolution-of-recaptcha/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Cloud fraud defense, the next evolution of reCAPTCHA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Cloud Fraud Defense：reCAPTCHA 的新进化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48039362' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud unveils Fraud Defense, the next evolution of anti-fraud tools beyond reCAPTCHA, leveraging ML signals to better distinguish humans from bots across apps and APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud 推出 Fraud Defense，成为对抗网络欺诈的下一代工具，超越传统的 reCAPTCHA，利用机器学习信号在应用与 API 级别更精准地区分真人与机器人。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-cloudblog-publish/images/GCN26_102_BlogHeader_2436x1200_Opt_1_Dark.max-2500x2500.jpg' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-cloudblog-publish/images/GCN26_102_BlogHeader_2436x1200_Opt_1_Dark.max-2500x2500.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47962032' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47962032' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://distr.sh/blog/running-docker-in-production/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Should I run plain Docker Compose in production in 2026?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 2026 年，应该在生产环境直接运行普通 Docker Compose 吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47962032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47962032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47962032' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plain docker-compose lacks orchestration, resilience, and predictable scaling for production workloads. The article argues for more robust deployment patterns, including Kubernetes or other orchestrators, and cautions against relying on ad-hoc Compose setups. It suggests using Compose in development or tightly controlled production contexts with strong tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纯 Docker Compose 在生产环境缺乏编排、弹性与可预测的扩展能力。建议采用更健壮的部署模式，如 Kubernetes 或其他编排工具，避免以随意的 Compose 设置执勤生产。可在开发或受控的生产场景中配合强工具链使用 Compose。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://distr.sh/_astro/thumb.C3Sy6lvp_Z19EmG1.webp' data-full='https://distr.sh/_astro/thumb.C3Sy6lvp_Z19EmG1.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48052371' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.elciudadano.com/en/brazils-pix-payment-system-faces-pressure-from-visa-and-mastercard/04/04/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brazil&#x27;s Pix payment system faces pressure from Visa and Mastercard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴西 Pix 支付体系面临 Visa 与 Mastercard 的压力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48052371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48052371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 346</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48052371' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Brazil&#x27;s Pix system is under pressure from card networks seeking interoperability or competition, raising questions about payment dynamics, fees, and regulatory responses in Latin America. The development could reshape how instant payments coexist with card rails and banks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴西的 Pix 即时支付体系正面临 Visa 与 Mastercard 的压力，涉及互操作性和竞争格局，可能影响费率、支付生态与监管走向。该动向将影响即时支付与信用卡通道、银行系统的共存方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.elciudadano.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/el-ciudadano-img-1.png' data-full='https://www.elciudadano.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/el-ciudadano-img-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wiisfi.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wi is Fi: Understanding Wi-Fi 4/5/6/6E/7/8 (802.11 n/AC/ax/be/bn)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wi is Fi：理解 Wi-Fi 4/5/6/6E/7/8（802.11n/AC/AX/BE/BN）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037760' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide walking through Wi-Fi generations 4 through 8, highlighting key differences in throughput, spectrum usage, and efficiency. It includes tips on when to deploy newer standards and how client compatibility shapes real-world performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本指南对 Wi-Fi 4/5/6/6E/7/8 的差异进行梳理，聚焦吞吐量、频谱利用率和效率的提升，并给出在实际部署中应何时采用新标准以及客户端兼容性的考量。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wiki.alcidesfonseca.com/blog/why-tuis-are-back/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why TUIs are back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TUI（可触知用户界面）为何回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 398</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48000028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tangible user interfaces are making a comeback as advances in fabrication, sensing, and materials enable new physical-digital interactions. The trend favors embodied learning, collaboration, and accessible design through rapid prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>可触知用户界面正重新兴起，得益于制材、传感与材料领域的进步，使物理数字互动更直观。该潮流推动以身临其境的学习、协作和可及性为目标的快速原型设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wiki.alcidesfonseca.com/static/images/alcides_small.jpg' data-full='https://wiki.alcidesfonseca.com/static/images/alcides_small.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260505-00/?p=112298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IBM didn&#x27;t want Microsoft to use the Tab key to move between dialog fields</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IBM 不希望微软在对话框字段间用 Tab 键切换</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48025687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48025687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48025687' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retro UI disagreement reveals how tab order decisions shaped early Windows product design. The anecdote highlights how tiny UX decisions influence developer workflows and product behavior. It reminds us that keyboard navigation remains a subtle but powerful UI lever.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一则关于 UI 设计的往事揭示 IBM 不愿微软在对话框字段之间通过 Tab 键切换焦点的历史观点。该趣闻凸显了微小设计决策对开发者工作流和产品行为的影响，提醒人们键盘导航仍是重要的 UI 驱动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48049208' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48049208' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-00720-8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Child marriages plunged when girls stayed in school in Nigeria</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尼日利亚女孩留在学校导致童婚大幅下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48049208' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large-scale study finds that keeping girls in school dramatically reduces child marriage in Nigeria. Each additional year of schooling correlates with lower odds of marrying as a child, highlighting education as a powerful lever for gender equality and development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，教育持续时间与童婚率呈显著负相关。让女孩上学不仅提升个人未来，还显著降低童婚风险，凸显教育在推动性别平等和社会发展的关键作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-00720-8/d41586-026-00720-8_52162394.png' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-00720-8/d41586-026-00720-8_52162394.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful site tracks days without GitHub incidents, serving as a lighthearted barometer of platform reliability. It invites discussions on resilience practices and what it takes to sustain outage-free operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的网站记录没有 GitHub 事件的日子，作为平台可靠性的轻量式晴雨表，促使人们讨论韧性实践与维持无故障运行所需的条件。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47940789' data-tags='Security,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47940789' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/pc-gaming/denuvo-has-been-bypassed-in-all-single-player-games-it-previously-protected-2k-games-and-denuvo-reportedly-retaliate-with-mandatory-14-day-online-checks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Denuvo has been cracked in all single-player games it previously protected</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Denuvo 已被破解，覆盖同类单人游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 379</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47940789' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Denuvo’s anti-tamper protections have been cracked across all single-player titles it previously protected, signaling a continuing piracy vulnerability in boxed and digitally distributed PC games. Publishers may respond with countermeasures and new DRM strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Denuvo 的防篡改保护已被破解，波及此前所保护的所有单人游戏，显示 PC 游戏的盗版防护依然脆弱。发行方可能采纳新的对策与 DRM 策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/NgNprVVnWz46KEcoC6U6V8-2048-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/NgNprVVnWz46KEcoC6U6V8-2048-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48080587' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48080587' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/imtomt/ymawky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Building a web server in assembly to give my life (a lack of) meaning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用汇编语言构建网络服务器以寻求生活意义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 379</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48080587' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A programmer builds a web server entirely in assembly, using the project as a personal experiment to explore the extremes of low-level computing and to find a sense of meaning. The effort highlights the practical challenges of implementing networking purely in assembly and offers reflections on abstraction costs and developer discipline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者用汇编语言从零实现一个网络服务器，将其作为自我探索的一种方式。该项目揭示了使用汇编实现网络功能的实际挑战，以及对抽象层成本与开发自律的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8bf79907f2a3d50813c871a692131dd5132ce31a07baf38b3be42d8aeac0be20/imtomt/ymawky' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8bf79907f2a3d50813c871a692131dd5132ce31a07baf38b3be42d8aeac0be20/imtomt/ymawky' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48026349' data-tags='Business,Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48026349' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sfgate.com/centralcoast/article/usda-aid-california-farmers-22240694.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California farmers to destroy 420k peach trees following Del Monte bankruptcy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Del Monte 破产后，加州农民将毁掉42万棵桃树</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 446</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48026349' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Following Del Monte&#x27;s bankruptcy, California growers must destroy around 420,000 peach trees due to supply chain realignments and replanting costs. The move underscores how corporate finance can ripple through farming and local economies, often necessitating government support or replanting plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Del Monte 破产后，加州农户需销毁约 420,000 棵桃树，原因包括供给链重组与再栽成本。这反映了企业金融风暴对农业的连锁影响，并可能促成政府援助或再植计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sfgate.com/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://www.sfgate.com/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48046436' data-tags='Career,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48046436' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/bliki/MythicalManMonth.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mythical Man Month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《人月神话》：现代解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48046436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 376</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48046436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48046436' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisits the core idea of the Mythical Man-Month and applies it to modern software teams, arguing that adding manpower to late-stage projects often backfires due to coordination overhead. Presents pragmatic takeaways for how to structure teams, estimate work, and preserve throughput in fast-moving environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>重新审视《人月神话》在当下软件团队中的含义，指出把人力加到后期项目往往适得其反，增加的沟通成本会拖慢进度。给出在团队组织、工作量估算和保持产出方面的实用经验教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/logo-sq.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/logo-sq.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48016534' data-tags='Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48016534' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/05/y_combinators_stake_in_openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Y Combinator&#x27;s Stake in OpenAI (0.6%?)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YC 在 OpenAI 的持股（约 0.6%？）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48016534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 375</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48016534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48016534' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of YC&#x27;s stake in OpenAI suggests early investor exposure to AI breakthroughs and questions about governance, ROI, and the evolving AI funding landscape. It reflects the high-stakes dynamics of AI startups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 YC 在 OpenAI 的持股进行分析，显示早期投资者对 AI 突破的潜在收益与治理、回报率以及 AI 创业融资格局的演变，折射出 AI 初创领域的高风险高回报。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' data-full='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48075366' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48075366' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.kronis.dev/blog/apple-is-increasing-my-cortisol-levels' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Distributing Mac software is increasing my cortisol levels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在分发 Mac 软件时，我的压力越来越大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48075366' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sharing the emotional burden of distributing Mac software, from signing and notarization to dealing with Gatekeeper prompts and new Apple requirements. The piece details the friction points developers face and how these policies affect release cycles and automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享在将 Mac 软件分发到用户手中时感受到的压力，覆盖签名、Notarization、Gatekeeper 等流程以及 Apple 日益严格的要求。文章指出这些政策对发布周期和自动化工作流带来的阻力，并给出应对建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.kronis.dev/user/pages/blog/apple-is-increasing-my-cortisol-levels/01-quarantine.jpg' data-full='https://blog.kronis.dev/user/pages/blog/apple-is-increasing-my-cortisol-levels/01-quarantine.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48020063' data-tags='AI,Business,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48020063' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.robert-glaser.de/when-everyone-has-ai-and-the-company-still-learns-nothing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When everyone has AI and the company still learns nothing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当人人都在用 AI，而公司却没有学到任何东西</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48020063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48020063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48020063' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI adoption alone doesn&#x27;t guarantee organizational learning; without feedback loops, governance, and value-driven KPIs, AI programs stagnate. The piece urges embedding learning culture into AI initiatives, with measurable outcomes and user-centered design. It argues for treating AI as a process improvement tool, not a magic fix.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>单靠 AI 的普及并不能带来组织学习；若缺乏反馈机制、治理与以价值为导向的指标，AI 项目会停滞。应把学习文化嵌入 AI 计划，设定可衡量的目标并以用户为中心设计。把 AI 视为流程改进工具，而非灵丹妙药。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/55/c6/55c640b8-7cdc-45a4-965d-3d691ea2071f/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/monolith-leaking-tokens.jpeg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/55/c6/55c640b8-7cdc-45a4-965d-3d691ea2071f/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/monolith-leaking-tokens.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48015397' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48015397' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://addyosmani.com/blog/agent-skills/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agent Skills</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理技能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48015397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 370</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48015397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48015397' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Skills outlines a modular approach to building AI agents, defining reusable capabilities that can be mixed and matched to create more capable automation. It offers practical guidance for designing scalable agent systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代理技能提出了一种将 AI 代理能力模块化的设计思路，定义可重用的技能并灵活组合，以打造更强的自动化能力。为构建可扩展的代理系统提供实用指引。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://addyosmani.com/assets/images/addys-agent-skills.jpg' data-full='https://addyosmani.com/assets/images/addys-agent-skills.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48028842' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48028842' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonogra.ph/write-some-software-give-it-away-for-free-05-05-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Write some software, give it away for free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>写点软件，免费开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48028842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 367</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48028842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48028842' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Publishing software for free can accelerate adoption, attract contributors, and create long-term value through ecosystem effects. The piece weighs licensing choices and strategies for sustaining open-source work while still deriving value from the effort.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>免费发布软件有助于加速采用、吸引贡献者，并通过生态效应创造长期价值。文章讨论许可选择以及在保持开放性的同时实现可持续收益的策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48052537' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48052537' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/natural-language-autoencoders' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Natural Language Autoencoders: Turning Claude&#x27;s Thoughts into Text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自然语言自动编码器：把 Claude 的想法转化为文本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48052537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 364</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48052537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48052537' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers explore natural language autoencoders as a way to compress model thoughts into textual representations. The approach aims to store, retrieve, and manipulate internal reasoning as editable text, potentially improving interpretability and memory for large AI systems. It also raises questions about fidelity, control, and how to use such latent text in downstream tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者将自然语言自动编码器用于把模型内部思维压缩成文本表示，以便存储、检索和编辑。该思路可能提升大型模型的可解释性与记忆能力，但也带来保真度、控制以及在下游任务中的应用挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/645f8c90db35a5d392c816a90c806b2904ccad21-1280x720.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/645f8c90db35a5d392c816a90c806b2904ccad21-1280x720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48032968' data-tags='Politics,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48032968' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/may/06/singapore-caning-school-bullies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Singapore introduces caning for boys who bully others at school</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新加坡对校园霸凌的男生实施鞭笞处罚</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 360</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 572</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48032968' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Singapore approves corporal punishment for school bullies, sparking debate over effectiveness, ethics, and student welfare. The policy signals a hard line on bullying but raises concerns about proportionality and long-term impacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新加坡宣布对校园霸凌者实施鞭笞等体罚措施，引发关于效果、伦理与学生福祉的讨论。此举硬性打击校园欺凌，但也引发对处罚比例与长期影响的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/86ec731f883bf7323f947a4361cd8e6a97e55906/501_0_5000_4000/master/5000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=79aaafa1086b2db25f6bef3718c1989a' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/86ec731f883bf7323f947a4361cd8e6a97e55906/501_0_5000_4000/master/5000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=79aaafa1086b2db25f6bef3718c1989a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48057901' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48057901' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mojolang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mojo 1.0 Beta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mojo 1.0 测试版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48057901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48057901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48057901' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mojo 1.0 Beta marks a milestone for the Mojo language, blending Python-like ergonomics with systems-level performance via LLVM-backed compilation. The beta signals growing interest in a Python-friendly, high-performance language for AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mojo 1.0 测试版标志着这门语言的一个里程碑，将 Python 风格的易用性与 LLVM 支撑的高性能系统级能力结合起来。测试版显示了对面向 AI 工作负载的高性能、易用语言的日益增长兴趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mojolang.org/img/mojo-social-card.png' data-full='https://mojolang.org/img/mojo-social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47977990' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://samcollins.blog/underdrawings/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using “underdrawings” for accurate text and numbers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 underdrawings 实现文本与数字的精确对齐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how underdrawings help align annotations with final drawings and diagrams, outlining workflows for overlaying text and numbers to improve documentation accuracy, revision control, and QA.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 how underdrawings 如何帮助将注释与最终图纸对齐，概述在图解上叠加文本与数字的工作流程，从而提升文档准确性、版本控制和质量检查。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/05/behind-the-scenes-hardening-firefox/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardening Firefox with Claude Mythos Preview</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Claude Mythos Preview 强化 Firefox 安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48051079' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox gains hardening features via Claude Mythos Preview, illustrating how AI-assisted tooling can tighten browser security and developer workflows. The post shares concrete steps and rationale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 通过 Claude Mythos Preview 引入安全增强功能，展示了人工智能辅助工具在提升浏览器安全与开发流程方面的潜力。文中给出具体实现步骤与背后的原因。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48032265' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48032265' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yuanchuan.dev/multi-stroke-text-effect-in-css' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Multi-stroke text effect in CSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS 的多描边文本效果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 352</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48032265' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Layering multiple text-shadow effects or using -webkit-text-stroke creates multi-stroke outlines for text, enabling bold, decorative headings. It boosts visual impact but requires attention to accessibility, rendering quality, and cross-browser consistency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过在 CSS 中叠加多层 text-shadow 或使用 -webkit-text-stroke，可以实现文本的多描边效果，提升标题的视觉冲击力。此做法在可访问性、渲染质量和跨浏览器一致性方面需要权衡，方能在实际 UI 中稳妥使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yuanchuan.dev/assets/images/kofi.png' data-full='https://yuanchuan.dev/assets/images/kofi.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48002442' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48002442' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://larsfaye.com/articles/agentic-coding-is-a-trap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic Coding Is a Trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理编码是一个陷阱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002442' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that agency-focused coding approaches, where AI agents take control, risk brittle behavior and hidden dependencies; recommends robust design, monitoring, and fallback strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出让 AI 代理参与编码的做法可能导致易崩溃的行为与隐藏的依赖，建议采用坚固的设计、监控与回退策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://larsfaye.com/opengraph-agents.jpg' data-full='https://larsfaye.com/opengraph-agents.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inkscape.org/doc/release_notes/1.4.4/Inkscape_1.4.4.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inkscape 1.4.4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Inkscape 1.4.4</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 347</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48040622' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inkscape 1.4.4 ships with bug fixes, stability improvements, and small feature tweaks. The release keeps pace with ongoing open-source SVG editor development, smoothing workflows for designers and hobbyists alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inkscape 1.4.4 版本修復多項漏洞，提升穩定性，並帶來若干小幅功能調整。這次更新延續開源向圖形編輯工具社群的發展步伐，為設計師與愛好者提供更順暢的工作流程。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48031769' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48031769' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.stripes.com/opinion/2026-04-23/stripes-former-ombudsman-pentagon-trying-to-silence-21465037.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ombudsman column: The Pentagon is trying to silence me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>监察专员专栏：五角大楼试图让我保持沉默</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 343</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031769' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Pentagon ombudsman column alleges the department is pressuring him to stay silent, underscoring tensions around whistleblowing, transparency, and oversight in the defense apparatus. The piece adds to ongoing questions about accountability in government.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前五角大楼监察专员的专栏声称政府正施压他保持沉默，凸显国防机构的告密、透明度与监督之间的紧张关系。这一说法引发对政府问责制的关注与讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.stripes.com/incoming/7rcmey-260326dealsphoto01.jpg/alternates/LANDSCAPE_480/260326DEALSphoto01.jpg' data-full='https://www.stripes.com/incoming/7rcmey-260326dealsphoto01.jpg/alternates/LANDSCAPE_480/260326DEALSphoto01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48009983' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48009983' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nber.org/papers/w35117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does Employment Slow Cognitive Decline? Evidence from Labor Market Shocks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>就业是否能减缓认知衰退？来自劳动力市场冲击的证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 370</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48009983' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using labor market shocks as natural experiments, the study examines whether employment slows cognitive decline. Findings hint at protective effects of work, though interpretation depends on causal assumptions and study design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将劳动力市场冲击作为自然实验，研究检验就业是否能减缓认知衰退。结果提示就业可能具有保护作用，但需结合因果推断的前提来解读。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nber.org/sites/default/files/2022-06/NBER-FB-Share-Tile-1200.jpg' data-full='https://www.nber.org/sites/default/files/2022-06/NBER-FB-Share-Tile-1200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.amusingplanet.com/2026/05/emerich-juettner-one-dollar.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The One Dollar Counterfeiter</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48048684' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical profile of Emerich Juettner, the infamous counterfeiter who produced large numbers of $1 bills, using relatively simple equipment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一美元钞票伪造者的历史画像，介绍了使用简易设备大量伪造的一位罪犯及其影响。文章分析了小面额伪钞对社会与经济的冲击，以及安全特征如何随之演化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48050499' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48050499' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tastecooking.com/i-want-to-live-like-costco-people/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I want to live like Costco people</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我想过像 Costco 那样生活</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 712</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050499' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay exploring the Costco lifestyle—bulk buying, long memberships, and frugal routines—as a model for efficiency and minimalism. It argues that adopting Costco-like habits can simplify decision fatigue, cut waste, and emphasize value over novelty in everyday consumption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨 Costco 式的生活方式——囤货、长期会员、简洁的日常习惯——作为提升效率与简约的范式。作者认为采用 Costco 风格的做法可以降低决策疲劳、减少浪费，并让日常消费更注重性价比。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tastecooking.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/COSTCO_SOCIAL-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://tastecooking.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/COSTCO_SOCIAL-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48030964' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrss.org/blog/youtube-your-feeds-are-broken' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTube, your RSS feeds are broken</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YouTube 的 RSS 订阅源出了问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48030964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48030964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48030964' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YouTube RSS feeds are experiencing issues that break third-party readers, forcing users to seek workarounds or alternative feeds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YouTube 的 RSS 订阅源出现问题，导致第三方订阅工具无法正常工作。文章分析了现代视频网站对 RSS 的依赖脆弱性，并提出可能的解决思路与替代方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
