<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48024129' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48024129' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6JhK8iCQuqI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EEVblog: The 555 Timer is 55 years old [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EEVblog：555定时器诞生55周年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48024129' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At 55 years old, the 555 timer remains a staple in analog electronics. Its simplicity and versatility power countless hobbyist builds and teaching labs, influencing modern timing and waveform generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>555 定时器已诞生55周年，仍是模拟电子学的基石。它的简单性和灵活性支撑着无数爱好者项目与教学实验，至今也影响着现代定时与波形生成的设计思路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48069192' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48069192' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/news/meta-shuts-down-end-to-end-encryption-for-instagram-dms-messaging' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Shuts Down End-to-End Encryption for Instagram Messaging</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 关闭 Instagram 消息端到端加密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48069192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48069192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48069192' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta has disabled end-to-end encryption for Instagram direct messages, citing policy enforcement and safety considerations. The change enables company access to message content for law enforcement and platform moderation, sparking debate over user privacy and the trade-offs of security versus safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 已对 Instagram 私信关闭端到端加密，表示出于合规执法和安全考量需要对信息进行访问。此举引发对隐私与安全的权衡辩论，质疑用户隐私性是否应让位于平台监控和执法需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/01VCs6SZdta2WftLSUptXTG-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1778247916.jpg' data-full='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/01VCs6SZdta2WftLSUptXTG-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1778247916.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48081245' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48081245' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.debian.org/debian-devel-announce/2026/05/msg00001.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Debian must ship reproducible packages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Debian 必须提供可重复构建的软件包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48081245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48081245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48081245' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Debian is pushing to ship reproducible packages, enabling anyone to rebuild binaries and verify their integrity. Reproducible builds help catch tampering and improve supply-chain transparency across the Debian ecosystem. The push will require changes to build processes and tooling, with implications for maintainers and CI pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Debian 正在推动发布可重复构建的软件包，使任何人都能重建二进制文件并验证完整性。可重复构建有助于发现篡改并提升供应链透明度，覆盖整个 Debian 生态。此举将要求构建流程和工具链的变更，影响维护者与持续集成管线。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48075144' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48075144' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cyberinsider.com/grapheneos-fixes-android-vpn-leak-google-refused-to-patch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS fixes Android VPN leak Google refused to patch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 修复 Android VPN 漏洞，Google 拒绝修补</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48075144' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS released a fix for an Android VPN leakage flaw after Google declined to patch it upstream. The bug could allow traffic or DNS leaks that bypass the VPN&#x27;s protections, undermining user privacy. The episode underscores gaps in upstream patching and the value of independent hardening in privacy-focused ROMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 为 Android 的 VPN 泄露漏洞修复提供补丁，而 Google 未在上游修补。该漏洞可能让 VPN 连接中的流量或 DNS 请求暴露，削弱隐私保护。事件凸显上游修补的局限，以及开源自定义系统在提升隐私安全方面的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cyberinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/73173.png' data-full='https://cyberinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/73173.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Hallucinopedia</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038257' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN features Hallucinopedia, a community-curated encyclopedia documenting AI hallucinations with examples and mitigations. It serves as a practical reference for developers and researchers to understand failure modes and design safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示 Hallucinopedia，这是一个由社区维护的关于 AI 幻觉现象的百科全书，收集示例与缓解策略。它为开发者和研究人员提供可操作的参考，帮助他们了解失败模式并设计相应的防护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从语言与文化的视角，揭示意大利叙事中《木偶奇遇记》所具有的独特怪诞与细微差异，关注语言、翻译与民间传说如何塑造这一经典角色。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48047930' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48047930' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dochia.dev/blog/idempotency/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Idempotency is easy until the second request is different</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>幂等性：当第二次请求不同的时候变得困难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48047930' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the subtle pitfalls of idempotent APIs when the second request carries different state or intent. The post argues that ensuring true idempotence requires careful design, such as using immutable operations, deduplication, and compensating actions to keep systems consistent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示幂等性在第二次请求若产生不同结果时的微妙陷阱。强调通过不可变操作、去重和补偿性行为等设计来维持系统的一致性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48038827' data-tags='Programming,Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48038827' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.val.town/better-auth' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Supabase to Clerk to Better Auth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Supabase 到 Clerk，再到 Better Auth：身份认证演进</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038827' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Comparing authentication providers from Supabase to Clerk, the piece advocates a holistic Better Auth approach—emphasizing developer experience, UX, and security defaults. It argues that modern auth is more than components—it&#x27;s flows, tokens, passwordless options, and a seamless experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Supabase、Clerk 等身份认证方案进行比较，文章提出 Better Auth 思路，强调开发者体验、用户体验与默认安全性等一体化认证方式。现代认证不仅是组件，更是完整的认证流程、令牌管理与无密码等现代化选项的结合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.val.town/og-image.png?title=From+Supabase+to+Clerk+to+Better+Auth' data-full='https://blog.val.town/og-image.png?title=From+Supabase+to+Clerk+to+Better+Auth' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48050540' data-tags='Hardware,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48050540' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/motherboards/motherboard-sales-collapse-by-more-than-25-percent-as-chipmakers-strangle-enthusiast-pc-market-to-build-more-ai-chips-asus-projected-to-sell-5-million-fewer-boards-in-2025-gigabyte-msi-and-asrock-also-expected-to-see-reduced-sales-numbers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Motherboard sales &#x27;collapse&#x27; amid unprecedented shortages fueled by AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在AI带动的前所未有短缺中，主板销量崩溃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 293</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 340</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050540' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global motherboard shipments fell sharply due to ongoing chip shortages and surging AI hardware demand, squeezing the enthusiast market. Analysts expect multi-million unit declines in 2025 as vendors recalibrate inventories and shift capacity toward AI-focused components.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>持续的芯片短缺与对AI硬件的强劲需求共同压缩了主板市场，发烧友群体承压。2025 年预计将出现数百万台的销量下滑，厂商也在调整库存并将产能转向AI相关组件。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/JHeDsYJt8RPWBecMPr5u28-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/JHeDsYJt8RPWBecMPr5u28-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PyInfra 3.8.0 adds new modules, improved inventory handling, and quality-of-life enhancements for infrastructure automation. The release tightens API changes and error reporting, helping teams scale automation with fewer surprises.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037009' data-tags='Entertainment,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/05/06/us/ted-turner-death' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ted Turner has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泰德·特纳去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037009' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Media mogul Ted Turner has died, ending a career that fundamentally reshaped television news and entertainment. He founded CNN and built a media empire that popularized 24/7 news and influenced philanthropy, sports, and global media culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>媒体巨头特德·特纳去世，标志着一个改变有线新闻与娱乐格局的时代结束。他创办了 CNN，打造了覆盖全球的媒体帝国，推动了 24/7 新闻的普及，并在慈善、体育和全球媒体领域留下深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/131111212246-16-ted-turner.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/131111212246-16-ted-turner.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48024358' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48024358' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kuber.studio/blog/Reflections/I%27m-Scared-About-Biological-Computing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m scared about biological computing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我对生物计算感到担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48024358' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful piece raises safety and governance concerns as biology-based computing moves closer to mainstream use. It warns about biosecurity, reproducibility, and ethical risk in wetware-enabled computation. The message calls for careful, responsible research and policy framing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇深思熟虑的文章警示生物计算在走向主流计算时的安全、治理与潜在后果。强调生物安全、可重复性与伦理风险，呼吁负责任的研究与政策框架。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/05/rss-feeds-send-me-more-traffic-than-google/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RSS feeds send me more traffic than Google</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RSS 订阅源带来比 Google 更多流量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48043964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48043964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48043964' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blogger reports that RSS feeds drive more visits to their site than Google search, highlighting the enduring value of feed readers and subscription-based readership. The post argues feeds offer stable, engaged readership that resists over-reliance on search algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者表示 RSS 订阅源带来比 Google 还多的流量，凸显订阅制与订阅型读者的价值。该观点强调 RSS 能提供稳定、参与度高的受众，抵御对搜索算法的过度依赖。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48012003' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pv-magazine.com/2026/05/04/heat-pump-sales-rise-17-across-europe-in-q1-as-energy-prices-surge/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Heat pump sales rise across Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲热泵销量上升</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012003' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a 17% rise in heat pump sales in Q1 across Europe amid energy price surges, signaling accelerated adoption of efficient heating technology and implications for energy policy and manufacturers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示欧洲第一季度热泵销量同比上升约 17%，能源价格上涨推动了高效取暖技术的采用，将对能源政策与制造商产生影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clojurescript.org/news/2026-05-07-release' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ClojureScript Gets Async/Await</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ClojureScript 新增 Async/Await 支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48059662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48059662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48059662' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ClojureScript adds async/await to simplify asynchronous code in the browser and Node.js environments. The update brings modern JS-style asynchronous patterns to the language and could broaden frontend adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ClojureScript 新增对 async/await 的支持，使异步代码在浏览器与 Node.js 中更易编写。更新使语言生态更贴近 JavaScript 的异步模式，可能扩大前端应用的采用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zed Editor Theme-Builder lets you craft and preview themes for the Zed Editor, enabling consistent, accessible syntax highlighting. It lowers the barrier to creating polished editor themes and sharing them with the community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zed 编辑器主题生成器可用于设计并预览 Zed 的主题，便于实现一致且易读的语法高亮。降低了创作与分享高质量编辑器主题的门槛。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xogium.me/the-text-mode-lie-why-modern-tuis-are-a-nightmare-for-accessibility' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The text mode lie: why modern TUIs are a nightmare for accessibility</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>文本模式的谎言：为何现代 TUI 对无障碍是一场噩梦</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002938' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that modern text-based user interfaces often fail accessibility due to ambiguous layouts, keyboard traps, and screen-reader inconsistencies, calling for accessibility-first design principles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出现代文本用户界面在无障碍方面的不足，原因包括布局模糊、键盘陷阱和屏幕阅读器不一致，呼吁以无障碍优先的设计原则。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xogium.me/img/avatars/t.png' data-full='https://xogium.me/img/avatars/t.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47998790' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The leak reportedly exposes Metal Gear Solid 2&#x27;s source code, fueling discussion about preservation, modding, and IP risk for classic games. It spotlights how legacy code can reappear and affect fan projects and legal considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称泄露的是《合金装备 2》的源代码，引发关于游戏保留、模组开发以及版权风险的讨论。凸显了遗留代码可能再次曝光对粉丝项目和法律的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/05/08/aws-outage-data-center-fanduel-coinbase.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS North Virginia data center outage – resolved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS 北弗吉尼亚数据中心故障已解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48058197' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outage at AWS&#x27;s North Virginia data center has been resolved after impacting multiple services. The event underscores how regional incidents can ripple across the cloud, reinforcing the importance of multi-region redundancy and robust incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS 北弗吉尼亚数据中心故障已解决，影响了多项服务。这起事件凸显区域性故障对云端的连锁影响，强调多区域冗余和快速事件响应的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48044638' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48044638' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://permacomputing.net/principles/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Permacomputing Principles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永续计算原则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48044638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48044638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48044638' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Permacomputing Principles advocate a sustainable, ethics-first approach to technology. The guidelines stress durability, repairability, energy efficiency, and community governance to align software and infrastructure with long-term ecological and social values.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Permacomputing 原则提出在技术开发中优先考虑对人和环境的长期影响，强调耐用性、可修复性、能效与社区治理等要素，并推动软件与基础设施走向更可持续的方向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48039739' data-tags='Business,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48039739' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/05/05/byd-overtakes-tesla-kia-best-selling-ev-brand-key-overseas-markets/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BYD overtakes Tesla and Kia as the best-selling EV brand in key overseas markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比亚迪超越特斯拉与起亚，成为海外市场最畅销的电动汽车品牌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 444</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48039739' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BYD has overtaken Tesla and Kia to become the best-selling EV brand in key overseas markets, signaling a rapid ascent of Chinese automakers in global electrification. The result underscores BYD&#x27;s scale, diverse model lineup, and export push as traditional automakers face headwinds in price and supply chains. This shift reshapes competitive dynamics and could pressure pricing and dealer strategies worldwide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>比亚迪在关键海外市场超越特斯拉和起亚，成为最畅销的电动汽车品牌。这反映了中国厂商在全球电动化进程中的快速崛起，以及比亚迪的规模、丰富车型与出口策略对全球市场的冲击。该格局变化可能影响全球定价、经销商策略与竞争态势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/06/BYDs-EV-price-cuts-1.jpeg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/06/BYDs-EV-price-cuts-1.jpeg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48029114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48029114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48029114' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigates Polymarket&#x27;s Panama HQ, raising questions about corporate footprint and regulatory compliance for a high-profile prediction market. Implications for crypto and prediction-market oversight are discussed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查 Polymarket 的巴拿马总部地址，质疑其公司实体与监管合规性，涉及对预测市场及加密领域监管的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48066592' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48066592' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/teaching-claude-why' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teaching Claude Why</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>教会 Claude 为什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48066592' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic explores methods to teach Claude to explain its reasoning, enhancing transparency and user trust in large language models. By prompting for justifications and training for verifiable reasoning, the approach aims to reduce hallucinations and improve debuggability in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 探讨让 Claude 解释自己推理过程的方法，以提高透明度和信任度。通过引导性提示和可验证推理的训练，旨在减少幻觉并提升调试性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.anthropic.com/api/opengraph-illustration?name=Hand%20Knot&amp;backgroundColor=cactus' data-full='https://www.anthropic.com/api/opengraph-illustration?name=Hand%20Knot&amp;backgroundColor=cactus' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48031867' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48031867' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://investors.micron.com/news-releases/news-release-details/industry-leading-245tb-micron-6600-ion-data-center-ssd-now' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>245TB Micron 6600 ION Data Center SSD Now Shipping</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>245TB 6600 ION 数据中心 SSD 出货</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031867' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Micron is shipping a 245TB version of the 6600 ION data-center SSD, delivering unprecedented raw capacity for hyperscale storage fleets. The high-density drive targets cold data, archival, and mass-storage workloads, offering efficiency and scalability at a lower cost per byte. As data center demand continues to scale, such density enables bigger pools with fewer devices and less cabling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美光宣布出货245TB 的 6600 ION 数据中心 SSD，面向海量数据中心场景提供前所未有的存储密度。高密度驱动适用于冷数据、归档与大规模存储工作负载，可在单位数据量成本和能效方面带来显著优势。随着数据中心需求持续扩大，此类高密度设计有助于减少设备数量与布线复杂度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48023533' data-tags='AI,Finance,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48023533' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/finance-agents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agents for financial services and insurance</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/05/04/10-lessons-for-agentic-coding.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lessons for Agentic Coding: What should we do when code is cheap?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向代理编码的教训：当代码变得廉价时，我们该怎么做</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48019025' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that as creating code becomes cheap, we must emphasize reliability, security, and auditability when building agentic systems. It presents lessons on provenance, testing, and robust interfaces to avoid brittle, trust-destroying products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张当代码成本低廉时，仍需强调可验证性、模块化和安全性，以避免代理编码下产生不可靠、难以审计的系统。提出关于溯源、测试与鲁棒接口等教训，帮助构建更可信的自动化代码。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48064312' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://btxx.org/posts/memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Serving a website on a Raspberry Pi Zero running in RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 RAM 中运行的 Raspberry Pi Zero 托管网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48064312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48064312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48064312' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduction to serving a site from RAM on a Raspberry Pi Zero, using a memory-based filesystem to achieve fast, wear-free hosting. The approach trades persistence for low latency and reduced SD card wear, making it suitable for ephemeral demos and low-traffic setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在 Raspberry Pi Zero 的内存中运行并托管一个网站，利用内存文件系统实现快速、减少写入磨损的托管。此方法以牺牲持久性换取低延迟和降低 SD 卡磨损，适合短期演示和低流量场景。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that age checks can be bypassed by a drawn-on mustache, illustrating social engineering risk in online safety systems. It underscores the need for more robust, multi-factor or behavior-based age verification approaches.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对1998年《终极 Online》演示服务器的工作原理进行详细分析，揭示其网络协议、世界状态管理与客户端交互方式。此文章为逆向工程遗留在线游戏的实证案例，探讨遗产保护、仿真与维持经典服务器的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsdatascience.com/news/telus-uses-ai-to-alter-call-agent-accents-a3868f63' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Telus Uses AI to Alter Call-Agent Accents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Telus 用 AI 改变呼叫中心客服的口音</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031109' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Telus is piloting AI to adjust call-center agents&#x27; accents to suit regional listening preferences, a move that could improve comprehension but also raises concerns about authenticity, bias, and workforce impact. The experiment highlights the tension between automation for customer experience and preserving human variety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Telus 正试点使用 AI 调整呼叫中心客服的口音，以更好地符合地区受众的聆听习惯，这或提升理解度，但也引发真实性、偏见与对员工的影响等担忧。此举凸显了在提升客户体验与保留人类多样性之间的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/apple-intel-have-reached-preliminary-chip-making-deal-wsj-reports-2026-05-08/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple, Intel have reached preliminary chip-making deal</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48066169' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple and Intel have reportedly reached a preliminary agreement to collaborate on chip manufacturing. If confirmed, the partnership could broaden Apple’s access to additional fabrication capacity and diversify its supply chain, complementing its reliance on TSMC for most chips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称苹果与英特尔已就芯片制造达成初步协议。若成真，可能帮助苹果获得更多代工产能、分散供应风险，并补充其对台积电的现有依赖。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48038307' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48038307' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tbray.org/ongoing/When/202x/2026/05/03/Life-During-Class-Wartime' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Life During Class Wartime</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阶级战争时代的生活</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038307' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tim Bray reflects on life under accelerating class divisions and how technology, work, and policy intersect in ways that feel like wartime conditions. He argues for pragmatic adaptation, stronger social safety nets, and governance that keeps critical digital systems resilient.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蒂姆·布雷在关于阶层分化加剧、科技、工作与治理交错的时代提出反思，生活方式仿佛处于“阶级战争”状态。他倡导务实适应、强化社会保障，并让关键数字系统具备韧性与治理能力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48089091' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jola.dev/posts/running-local-models-on-m4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running local models on an M4 with 24GB memory</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48089091' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical exploration of running local AI models on an M4 with 24GB RAM, detailing the trade-offs between model size, quantization, and CPU inference. It shows how modest hardware can still enable useful local inference without cloud reliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在配备 24GB 内存的 M4 上本地运行 AI 模型的实用探索，讨论模型大小、量化与 CPU 推理之间的权衡。展示了在相对有限的硬件上实现有用的本地推理、减少对云端的依赖。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48012162' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48012162' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.strix.ai/blog/how-strix-found-zero-auth-vulnerability-dod-backed-startup' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Securing a DoD contractor: Finding a multi-tenant authorization vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>保卫国防部承包商：发现多租户授权漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012162' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security research firm identified a multi-tenant authorization vulnerability affecting a DoD-backed startup, showing how access controls across tenants can be misconfigured. Misconfigurations across tenants can grant unintended access to sensitive data. The finding reinforces the importance of zero-trust design and independent security reviews for defense contractors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究公司发现一个影响 DoD 背书创业公司的多租户授权漏洞，暴露了跨租户的访问控制配置错误所带来的风险。错误配置可能让非授权方访问敏感数据。此发现强调在防务承包商中应用零信任设计与独立安全评审的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://strix.ai/blog-schemata-cover.png' data-full='https://strix.ai/blog-schemata-cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reflects on cognitive debt in software work, focusing on mental load from toolchains and complex systems, and proposing strategies like simplifying tooling and reducing toggling to improve developer experience.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48081469' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://g5t.de/articles/20260510-task-paralysis-and-ai/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Task Paralysis and AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>任务瘫痪与人工智能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48081469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48081469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48081469' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes how AI assistants can induce task paralysis by outsourcing cognitive steps, causing users to hesitate and overplan. It offers strategies such as decomposing tasks, incremental prompts, and minimal viable outputs to maintain momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了 AI 助手通过外包认知步骤可能引发的任务瘫痪，使用户犹豫并过度规划。提供将任务拆解、分步提示和产出最小可行结果等策略以保持推进。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hand-drawn QR codes show that with generous error correction and careful design, QR codes can be aesthetically customized without sacrificing scannability. It blends art with encoding, offering practical paths for branding and tactile interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>手绘二维码展示了在保持可扫描性的前提下进行美学设计的可能性，依赖错误更正和排版技巧实现艺术与编码的结合。与此同时为品牌设计和触控界面提供了可行路径。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account of navigating Japan&#x27;s legal system, detailing experiences around arrest or detainment, and offering practical takeaways for travelers or tech workers abroad. It sheds light on procedures, rights, and cultural differences.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037517' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037517' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/what-causes-lightning-the-answer-keeps-getting-more-interesting-20260506/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What causes lightning? The answer keeps getting more interesting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>雷电的成因为何？答案越来越有趣</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037517' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lightning remains a rich puzzle for physics: new observations reveal surprising mechanisms for initiation and energy transfer beyond simple charge separation. The evolving understanding has implications for atmospheric science, weather forecasting, and the design of robust high-voltage systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>雷电的成因比以往想象得更复杂，最新发现揭示了起始机制和能量传输的意外细节。这一认识的演进对大气科学、天气预测以及高压系统的设计都具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/LightningIgnition-crEarthScienceAndRemoteSensingUnit_NASA-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/LightningIgnition-crEarthScienceAndRemoteSensingUnit_NASA-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48027455' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elonlit.com/scrivings/a-theory-of-deep-learning/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Theory of Deep Learning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>深度学习理论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48027455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48027455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48027455' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise theoretical piece outlining a framework for deep learning, touching on why overparameterized models generalize and how optimization dynamics interact with data structure. It provides a high-level lens for researchers seeking intuition beyond empirical results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇提出深度学习理论框架的高层次文章，讨论为何在过参数化的情形下模型仍能泛化，以及优化动力学如何与数据结构交互。为研究者提供直观理解问题的高层视角。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47974682' data-tags='Space,Science,Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47974682' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-usindian-space-mission-extreme-subsidence.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US–Indian space mission maps extreme subsidence in Mexico City</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美印太空任务绘制墨西哥城极端下沉图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47974682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47974682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47974682' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US-Indian space mission mapped extreme subsidence in Mexico City, highlighting rapid ground sinking driven by groundwater extraction and urban growth. The findings underline the vulnerability of infrastructure and the need for sustainable water management and adaptive urban planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美印合作的太空任务绘制出墨西哥城的极端下沉区域，揭示因地下水抽取和快速城市化导致的地面下沉。研究结果凸显基建脆弱性，强调需要加强水资源管理和宜居的城市规划来降低风险。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/offbeat/2026/05/05/noyb-cries-foul-on-linkedin-withholding-profile-visitor-data/5225338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LinkedIn profile visitor lists belong to the people, says Noyb</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NOYB称 LinkedIn 的访问者名单属于个人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48047970' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy group NOYB argues that LinkedIn&#x27;s profile visitor data is personal data and should be accessible to users, challenging LinkedIn&#x27;s withholding practice. The dispute centers on data ownership, consent, and how metadata is monetized.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>隐私倡导组织 NOYB 主张 LinkedIn 的访问者数据属于个人信息，用户应能获取。这场争议涉及数据所有权、用户同意以及元数据的商业化利用。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 270</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48063240' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla recalls its cheaper Cybertruck due to a risk of wheel detachment. The recall involves inspecting and reworking wheel assemblies, reflecting the ongoing demand for vehicle safety and quality control in scaling EV production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉召回价格较低的 Cybertruck，原因是轮子可能脱落的风险。召回将涉及对轮毂/轮组的检查和重新装配，体现了在大规模电动车产能扩张中对安全与质量控制的持续关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48077971' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48077971' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.freebsd.org/security/advisories/FreeBSD-SA-26:13.exec.asc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local privilege escalation via execve()</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FreeBSD：通过 Execve() 实现的本地提权漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48077971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48077971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48077971' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FreeBSD advisory details a local privilege escalation via the execve() syscall, enabling a user with limited privileges to gain higher access. Users should apply patches and review program binaries for potential misuse; it&#x27;s a reminder to enforce least privilege.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeBSD 公告披露了通过 execve() 系统调用实现的本地权限提升漏洞，可能让最低权限用户获得更高权限。应尽快应用修补并检查二进制行为，强调最小权限与进程隔离的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48088151' data-tags='Energy,Politics,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48088151' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/maryland-citizens-slapped-with-usd2-billion-grid-upgrade-bill-for-out-of-state-ai-data-centers-state-complains-to-federal-energy-regulators-says-additional-cost-breaks-ratepayer-protection-pledge-promises' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maryland citizens hit with $2B power grid upgrade for out-of-state AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因外州 AI 数据中心而来的电网升级让马里兰居民承担20亿美元成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48088151' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ratepayers face a multi-billion-dollar upgrade cost to support out-of-state AI data centers; regulators and lawmakers are challenging the billing and seeking protections. The dispute highlights how infrastructure costs for AI can be borne by residents rather than the benefiting entities. The outcome could influence policy on grid investments and fairness for ratepayers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为支持外州 AI 数据中心而进行的电网升级，导致用电者承担高达20亿美元的成本，引发监管机构与立法者的抗议与保护措施的讨论。这一分摊方式凸显了 AI 基础设施投资的公共成本问题，可能影响电网投资政策与对率费使用的公平性评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/KHUetaXQbsmm6z9m5g5Pne-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/KHUetaXQbsmm6z9m5g5Pne-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48067734' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48067734' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ze3tar.github.io/post-zcrx.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You gave me a u32. I gave you root. (io_uring ZCRX freelist LPE)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你给我一个 u32，我就能拿到 root：基于 io_uring freelist 的本地提权漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48067734' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe a local privilege escalation using io_uring&#x27;s freelist, where a crafted u32 value can corrupt kernel state and grant root access. The bug exposes a class of LPE vectors in async I/O subsystems and stresses the importance of robust freelist validation and timely kernel fixes. Practitioners should monitor for upstream patches and consider mitigations in environments exposing io_uring to untrusted inputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者发现借助 io_uring 的 freelist 可以通过伪造的 u32 值实现本地提权，获得 root 权限。该漏洞暴露了异步 I/O 子系统中 freelist 处理的安全漏洞，凸显需要加强对 freelist 的校验与及时的内核修复。企业应关注上游补丁并在受信任输入环境中实施缓解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrouter.ai/announcements/gpt55-cost-analysis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.5 Price Increase: What It Costs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.5 价格上涨：成本有多大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48057209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48057209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48057209' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.5 has increased its pricing, affecting teams and startups that rely on the model at scale. The change pressures buyers to reassess usage patterns and budgets, and could drive interest in cheaper tiers, offloading, or alternative models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.5 提价将直接影响大规模使用该模型的团队与初创企业，迫使重新评估用量和预算。此举可能推动向更低价层、离线部署或其他模型的替代方案的兴趣。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://productonboarding.com/articles/why-product-tours-get-skipped' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why most product tours get skipped</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何大多数产品引导被跳过</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48028546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48028546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48028546' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Product tours often fail to engage because they interrupt flow, misalign with users&#x27; goals, or overwhelm with features. The article argues for lightweight, contextual onboarding, progressive disclosure, and task-based guidance that starts with value-delivery steps rather than feature lists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>产品引导往往因为打断使用流程、与用户目标错位、或信息量过大而被跳过。文章主张采用轻量、情境化的引导与渐进式披露，优先通过任务驱动的价值呈现来提升留存与转化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48085095' data-tags='Open Source,Business,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48085095' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dbushell.com/2026/04/29/github-is-sinking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub is sinking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 正在走下坡路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48085095' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece arguing GitHub is losing momentum due to pricing shifts, outages, and policy moves that push developers toward self-hosted options or alternatives. It reflects concerns about platform centralization and resilience for open-source workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇观点文章认为 GitHub 因定价变动、故障与政策调整而失去势头，促使开发者转向自托管或替代工具。文章反映了对平台集中化与开源工作流韧性的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48002607' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jdgr.net/the-hidden-costs-of-great-abstractions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &#x27;Hidden&#x27; Costs of Great Abstractions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伟大抽象背后的隐藏成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002607' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turns a critical eye to high-level abstractions, showing how they can hide costs like performance overhead, debugging complexity, and maintenance burdens. Encourages mindful design choices that expose true costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批判性地审视高层抽象，揭示它们可能隐藏的成本，如性能开销、调试复杂性和维护负担。倡导在设计时对真实成本有清晰认识并做出谨慎选择。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48045012' data-tags='DevOps,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48045012' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aniket.foo/posts/20260505-netboot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Diskless Linux boot using ZFS, iSCSI and PXE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 ZFS、iSCSI 和 PXE 的无磁盘 Linux 启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48045012' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY guide demonstrates diskless Linux boot using ZFS over iSCSI via PXE, enabling servers to boot from network storage. The approach can reduce hardware costs and simplify management, but relies on robust network and storage performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>教程展示如何通过 PXE 和 iSCSI 使用 ZFS 实现无磁盘网络启动，可将服务器从网络存储加载。该做法能降低硬件成本、简化运维，但对网络与存储性能要求较高。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uchicagomedicine.org/forefront/research-and-discoveries-articles/nonprofit-hospitals-spend-billions-on-management-consultants' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nonprofit hospitals spend billions on consultants with no clear effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>非营利医院在管理顾问上花费数十亿美元却未见明显成效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056158' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nonprofit hospitals spend billions on management consultants with little measurable impact on outcomes, prompting questions about the value of external advice in healthcare systems. The finding suggests rethinking procurement and internal capability-building.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>非营利医院在管理咨询上的支出达到数十亿美元，但对结果的可量化影响甚微，引发对外部咨询在医疗体系中的价值的讨论。研究或促使重新审视采购与内部能力建设的策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037223' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037223' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2025/12/04/we-see-something-that-works-and-then-we-understand-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We see something that works, and then we understand it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>先看到有用的东西再去理解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037223' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037223' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037223' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that practical success often precedes theory: we adopt working solutions first and only then invest in understanding why they work. This pragmatic approach accelerates learning, while cautioning against premature overgeneralization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张实用性先于理论：先采用能用的解决方案，再去理解其背后的原理。通过具体例子说明让有用的方法先行，有助于快速迭代与学习，但也提醒避免过早给出普遍性的结论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Capture-decran-le-2025-12-04-a-10.40.38-999x1024.png' data-full='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Capture-decran-le-2025-12-04-a-10.40.38-999x1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球性研究揭示隐蔽监控者反复利用电信基础设施进行监控，威胁隐私与网络安全。报告梳理了攻击手段和目标，并呼吁加强防护与问责。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48017813' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dragonsreach.it/2026/05/04/cve-2026-31431-copy-fail-rootless-containers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CVE-2026-31431: Copy Fail vs. rootless containers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CVE-2026-31431：无根容器中的拷贝失败问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48017813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48017813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48017813' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-31431 highlights a bug in rootless container environments related to copy operations, where failure semantics can lead to unexpected behavior or security implications. The issue underscores how nuanced container security is when user namespaces and file operations interact. Patching and careful error handling are likely needed to close the vulnerability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-31431 指出在无根容器环境中与拷贝操作相关的一个缺陷，拷贝失败的语义可能导致意外行为或安全影响。该问题凸显了用户命名空间与文件操作交互时，容器安全的复杂性。修补和谨慎的错误处理可能是解决该漏洞的关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coe.gatech.edu/news/2026/04/batteries-not-included-or-required-these-smart-home-sensors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Batteries Not Included, or Required, for These Smart Home Sensors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>这些智能家居传感器无需电池也可工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47998638' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>These smart home sensors operate without regular batteries, using energy harvesting or low-power design to draw power from the environment. The approach enables maintenance-free deployments, especially for remote or hard-to-reach locations, but raises questions about reliability and cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这些智能家居传感器通过能量收集或低功耗设计实现不需要定期更换电池就能工作。此举可实现对偏远或难以维护位置的免维护部署，但也带来可靠性和成本方面的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A self-cancelling subscription model ends automatically after a defined period or condition, removing long-term commitment friction. The concept prompts rethinking retention strategies and value delivery for subscription products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自我取消订阅模型在达到设定条件或时间后自动结束，消除了长期绑定的摩擦。该思路促使人们重新审视订阅产品的留存策略与价值交付。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47997486' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47997486' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mobeigi.com/blog/security/security-through-obscurity-is-not-bad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Security through obscurity is not bad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安全性并非来自暴露：隐藏策略也有价值</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47997486' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that obscurity, used judiciously, can complement strong security practices rather than replace them. It discusses how limited visibility can reduce attack surfaces, protect IP, and buy time against adversaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张在恰当时机下的隐藏/保密可以与强安全措施互补，而不是取代它们。文中指出有限的可见性可降低攻击面、保护知识产权，并为防御赢得时间。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN post demonstrates Apple’s Sharp running in the browser via ONNX Runtime Web. It showcases how browser-based inference can be feasible for certain ML workloads without server round-trips.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ratex.lites.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RaTeX: KaTeX-compatible LaTeX rendering engine in pure Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RaTeX：用纯 Rust 编写、兼容 KaTeX 的 LaTeX 渲染引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48010729' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RaTeX is a LaTeX rendering engine written in Rust that aims to be KaTeX-compatible and fast, safe, and embeddable in web apps. It offers Rust developers a high-performance math rendering option without relying on C++ bindings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RaTeX 是用 Rust 编写的 LaTeX 渲染引擎，目标是兼容 KaTeX API、提供高性能和安全性，便于嵌入到 Web 应用中，为 Rust 开发者提供纯粹的数学渲染方案。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathoverflow.net/questions/43690/whats-a-mathematician-to-do' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What&#x27;s a mathematician to do? (2010)</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48083007' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MathOverflow hosts a question about what a mathematician should do to maximize impact and career prospects. Responses emphasize focusing on clear, addressable problems, cultivating collaborations, and communicating ideas beyond academia. They also stress balancing deep theoretical work with opportunities in industry or applied research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个关于数学家的职业路径的 MathOverflow 问题引发广泛讨论。回复强调聚焦清晰的问题、建立合作关系、将研究成果更广泛地传播，以及在理论深度与潜在应用之间寻求平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48060918' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48060918' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geojson.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GeoJSON</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GeoJSON</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48060918' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GeoJSON is a lightweight format for encoding geographic features as JSON. It standardizes points, lines, polygons, and collections, enabling simple sharing and mapping in web apps and GIS workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GeoJSON 是一种用 JSON 编码地理要素的轻量格式，支持点、线、面及集合，便于在网页地图和 GIS 工作流中进行简单的共享与可视化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48026021' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48026021' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5V-Turbo introduces a native foundation model tailored for multimodal agents, likely featuring integrated vision capabilities with language reasoning, enabling more capable AI assistants. The work contributes to building efficient, open, and deployable models that run closer to end-user hardware, accelerating experimentation and practical deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5V-Turbo 是一个面向多模态代理的原生基础模型，整合文本与视觉能力，提升AI 助手的推理与交互能力。这一工作推动了更高效、可部署的开源基础模型的发展，使在本地或边缘硬件上进行试验与落地更加可行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
