<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.yahoo.com/vpn/article/its-official-utah-is-the-us-state-closest-to-banning-vpns-153556814.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s official: Utah is the U.S. state closest to banning VPNs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>官方消息：犹他州是最接近全面禁止 VPN 的美国家州</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48023473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48023473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48023473' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Utah is moving toward stricter VPN restrictions, positioning itself as the state closest to banning consumer VPNs amid ongoing debates over privacy, encryption, and circumvention. The policy trajectory signals rising state-level efforts to regulate or limit access to privacy tools, with potential implications for remote work and digital rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>犹他州正推动更严格的 VPN 限制，成为美国在禁止或限制消费者 VPN 方面最接近的州。此类趋势反映出各州在隐私、加密与规避工具方面的日益活跃的监管行动，可能影响远程办公与数字权利。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.yimg.com/ny/api/res/1.2/XQn9gSTq5dxldGx4qxSuqQ--/YXBwaWQ9aGlnaGxhbmRlcjt3PTEyMDA7aD04MDA-/https://d29szjachogqwa.cloudfront.net/images/2026-05/d678f101-f319-4657-ba19-1316a752dd24' data-full='https://s.yimg.com/ny/api/res/1.2/XQn9gSTq5dxldGx4qxSuqQ--/YXBwaWQ9aGlnaGxhbmRlcjt3PTEyMDA7aD04MDA-/https://d29szjachogqwa.cloudfront.net/images/2026-05/d678f101-f319-4657-ba19-1316a752dd24' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://janrosenow.substack.com/p/spain-just-became-one-of-europes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain has become one of Europe’s cheapest power markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙成为欧洲最便宜的电力市场之一</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48085330' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain has emerged as one of Europe&#x27;s cheapest power markets, driven by ample renewables and favorable pricing dynamics. The trend could attract data centers and energy-intensive industries, but may also raise concerns about price volatility and grid investments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙凭借丰富的可再生能源和有利的定价结构，成为欧洲最便宜的电力市场之一。这一趋势可能吸引数据中心与高耗能行业，但也可能带来价格波动与电网投资方面的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用一个 10 MB 的有限状态机转写器二进制取代 3GB SQLite 数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48082676' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer replaces a 3 GB SQLite database with a compact finite-state transducer binary, slashing storage from gigabytes to megabytes and likely improving startup and lookup speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者用一个紧凑的有限状态机转写器二进制取代了 3 GB 的 SQLite 数据库，将存储规模从 GB 级降至 MB 级，并可能提升启动与查找速度。文章探讨了可变性与数据更新工作流等权衡，以及在读密集型场景下何时适合使用 FST。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chapter 1 surveys the origins and ambitions of Visual Basic, tracing how it shaped beginner-friendly programming, rapid app development, and the broader software ecosystem. It sets up the narrative for how VB influenced tooling and education.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47970497' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Proton 推出 Proton Meet，一款强调端到端加密与数据主权的隐私型视频会议服务，面向注重安全性的团队与机构。力求在主流市场中提供更强的隐私保护选项。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48045174' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ProgramBench: Can language models rebuild programs from scratch?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ProgramBench：语言模型能否从零重新构建程序？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48045174' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers evaluate whether large language models can reconstruct or rewrite programs from scratch using a benchmark called ProgramBench. The findings reveal current limitations and suggest integrating formal methods with AI-assisted code generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员利用 ProgramBench 基准评估大型语言模型在给定规格下从零重建复杂程序的能力。结果揭示现有局限性，并暗示将形式化方法与 AI 生成代码结合的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://placesjournal.org/article/the-disappearance-of-the-public-bench/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Disappearance of the Public Bench</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48042817' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The public bench is vanishing from modern urban spaces, prompting a critique of design and policy that affect social life, accessibility, and city planning. The piece connects benches to civic space and democracy.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cyber.netsecops.io/articles/obsidian-plugin-abused-in-campaign-to-deploy-phantom-pulse-rat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Obsidian plugin was abused to deploy a remote access trojan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Obsidian 插件被滥用部署远程访问木马</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48088576' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security incident shows how a malicious Obsidian plugin can be used to deploy a remote access trojan, underscoring the risk surface in extensible apps and the need for safer plugin ecosystems and vetting processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全事件揭示恶意 Obsidian 插件如何被用于部署远程访问木马，凸显可扩展应用的风险点，以及建立更安全的插件生态和审核机制的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48008074' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48008074' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/newton-s-law-gravity-passes-its-biggest-test-ever' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Newton&#x27;s law of gravity passes its biggest test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>牛顿引力定律经受史上最大规模测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48008074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48008074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48008074' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New tests confirm Newtonian gravity&#x27;s inverse-square law across scales or contexts, reinforcing classical gravity as a robust baseline. The result tightens constraints on alternative theories and helps calibrate models used in astronomy and engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的观测结果在史上最大的测试规模中验证了牛顿引力定律的逆平方关系，强化了经典引力作为基线的有效性。该发现对替代理论的约束和天体物理、工程建模具有重要意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48056268' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48056268' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bcm.edu/news/researchers-discover-advanced-language-processing-in-the-unconscious-human-brain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plasticity and language in the anaesthetized human hippocampus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>麻醉状态下人类海马体的可塑性与语言处理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056268' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research shows language processing can occur in the anaesthetized hippocampus, revealing surprising neural plasticity during unconscious states. The finding prompts rethinking how anesthesia affects memory and language networks and could influence future neuroimaging and clinical assessments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现麻醉状态下海马体仍具语言处理能力，揭示昏迷中神经可塑性，挑战了对麻醉对记忆与语言网络影响的直觉。此发现可能影响对麻醉下记忆和语言功能的评估以及未来的神经影像研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bcm.edu/sites/default/files/2024-07/brain-ocd.jpg' data-full='https://www.bcm.edu/sites/default/files/2024-07/brain-ocd.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48054386' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48054386' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/instructure-hacker-claims-data-theft-from-8-800-schools-universities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ShinyHunters claims data theft from 8,800 schools (Instructure/Canvas)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ShinyHunters 声称窃取 8800 所学校数据（Instructure/Canvas）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48054386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48054386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48054386' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ShinyHunters claims to have stolen data from 8,800 schools using Instructure&#x27;s Canvas, highlighting ongoing security risks in edtech platforms. The incident underscores the need for sector-specific safeguards and rapid incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ShinyHunters 宣称从 8800 所学校的 Canvas 平台窃取数据，暴露教育科技平台的持续安全风险。事件凸显对教育行业特有的安全防护与快速响应的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2025/01/22/school-hacker.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2025/01/22/school-hacker.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48080702' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48080702' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/expanded-gemini-api-file-search-multimodal-rag/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini API File Search is now multimodal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini API 文件搜索现已支持多模态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48080702' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Gemini API file search now supports multimodal inputs, enabling richer queries over documents and media with retrieval-augmented generation. This broadens how developers build search interfaces on Gemini and signals a trend toward multimodal tooling in AI APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini API 文件搜索现已支持多模态输入，允许对文档和多媒体内容进行更丰富的查询，并结合检索增强的生成能力。此举拓展了开发者在 Gemini 上构建搜索界面的方式，体现了 AI API 多模态工具的发展趋势。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48067790' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48067790' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.status.io/pages/incident/55957a99e800baa4470002da/69fe2d6698ca07050eb4b1b3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let’s Encrypt: Stopping Issuance for Potential Incident – Resolved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let’s Encrypt：潜在事件中止签发证书—现已解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48067790' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt halted new certificate issuance during a potential incident and has since resolved the issue. The incident underscores the resilience and governance of automated CAs and the importance of prompt incident response to maintain TLS trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let’s Encrypt 在潜在事件期间暂停了证书签发，现已解决。这一事件强调了自动化证书颁发机构在维护 TLS 信任方面的韧性与治理，以及快速应对能力的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the PC Engine&#x27;s CPU, the HuC6280, an enhanced 8-bit processor derived from 6502 architecture. The post shows how this CPU design influenced the console&#x27;s performance and programming model, contributing to its distinctive era of games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文聚焦 PC Engine 的处理器 HuC6280，这是一颗基于 6502 架构的增强型 8 位处理器。该设计影响了主机的性能与编程模型，成为其独特游戏时代背后的硬件基础。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.citizen.co.za/news/home-affairs-officials-suspended-ai-hallucinations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two Home Affairs officials suspended after AI &#x27;hallucinations&#x27; found</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两名内政部官员因 AI &#x27;幻觉&#x27; 被停职</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48053842' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigation found that an AI system produced hallucinations affecting decisions, leading to the suspension of two Home Affairs officials. The case underscores governance and reliability challenges when government workflows depend on AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查发现某 AI 系统出现幻觉式输出，影响决策，因此两名内政部官员被停职。此案凸显政府工作流程对 AI 工具的治理与可靠性挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.citizen.co.za/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/AI.jpg' data-full='https://media.citizen.co.za/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/AI.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47983467' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47983467' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reiner.org/neural-net-ciphers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are neural networks and cryptographic ciphers so similar? (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么神经网络和密码学密码如此相似？（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47983467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47983467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47983467' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues structural parallels between neural networks and cryptographic ciphers, touching on layered transformations, diffusion/propagation, and potential cross-domain insights. It suggests that insights from ML and crypto can inform each other.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文指出神经网络与密码密码学之间存在结构性相似之处，涉及分层转换、扩散/混淆等概念，并探讨两者在理论与应用层面的潜在互相启发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://reiner.org/static/banner-medium.471c8add.png' data-full='https://reiner.org/static/banner-medium.471c8add.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48076465' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48076465' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.copahost.com/blog/cpanels-black-week-three-new-vulnerabilities-patched-after-ransomware-attack-on-44000-servers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CPanel&#x27;s Black Week: 3 New Vulnerabilities Patched After Attack on 44k Servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>cPanel 的黑色周：对 4.4 万台服务器的攻击后修补三处新漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48076465' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Following a ransomware incident that hit tens of thousands of cPanel servers, three new vulnerabilities were patched. The quick fix underscores how exposed control panels remain and why rapid patching and monitoring are crucial after breaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在针对数万台服务器的勒索攻击后，cPanel 修补了三处新漏洞。此次快速修复凸显了远程管理面板仍是关键攻击面，强调在漏洞披露后要加速打补丁和监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.copahost.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/9d592da9-48e6-4f09-b972-a73a543c754a.png' data-full='https://www.copahost.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/9d592da9-48e6-4f09-b972-a73a543c754a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48048679' data-tags='Hardware,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48048679' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://learn.sparkfun.com/tutorials/how-leds-are-made/all' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How LEDs are made (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LED 的制造过程（2014）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48048679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48048679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48048679' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LEDs are built by growing multiple semiconductor layers on a substrate, forming diodes that emit light. White LEDs rely on blue emission plus a phosphor layer that converts color, and packaging and thermal management are essential for efficiency. The 2014 tutorial walks through chip fabrication, epitaxial growth, die bonding, phosphor application, and final assembly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LED 的制造涉及在衬底上生长多层半导体材料，形成发光二极管。白光 LED 依赖蓝光发射并通过荧光粉层实现颜色转换，封装和热管理对于效率至关重要。该教程从芯片制造、外延生长、芯片键合、荧光粉应用到最终组装，逐步讲解全过程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sparkfun.com/avatar/1ff6ad39b8c242a14296a76845e116cd?d=retro&amp;s=20&amp;r=pg' data-full='https://cdn.sparkfun.com/avatar/1ff6ad39b8c242a14296a76845e116cd?d=retro&amp;s=20&amp;r=pg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48036289' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48036289' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/man-finds-1-million-worth-of-yu-gi-oh-cards-in-a-dumpster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man finds $1M worth of Yu-Gi-Oh cards in a dumpster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>男子在垃圾桶发现价值 100 万美元的 Yu-Gi-Oh 卡牌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48036289' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A man stumbled upon a discarded bag of Yu-Gi-Oh cards valued at up to $1 million. The find underscores the hidden value of collectibles and how storage conditions, rarity, and demand can dramatically affect resale potential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名男子在垃圾桶中发现价值高达百万美元的 Yu-Gi-Oh 卡牌收藏。该发现凸显收藏品的潜在价值，以及保存条件、稀有度和市场需求如何影响转售潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/2026/05/Yug1-1.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/2026/05/Yug1-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48011077' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.happydaze.se/wolf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wolfenstein 3D for Gameboy Color on custom cartridge (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Game Boy Color 上的 Wolfenstein 3D：用自制卡带实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48011077' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A homebrew cartridge lets you play Wolfenstein 3D on Game Boy Color, showcasing retro hardware hacking and the challenges of squeezing PC-era games into handheld hardware. It demonstrates how enthusiasts push limits of memory, CPU speed, and cartridge tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过自制卡带在 Game Boy Color 上运行 Wolfenstein 3D，展示了复古硬件黑客技艺以及将 PC 时代游戏塞入掌机的挑战。此项工作体现爱好者在内存、CPU 速度与卡带工具方面的极限探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.happydaze.se/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/rev_c_and_d.jpg' data-full='http://www.happydaze.se/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/rev_c_and_d.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48074087' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dillo-browser.org/lab/web-fork/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Forking the Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 Web 的分叉实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48074087' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers experiment with &#x27;forking the Web&#x27; by building a lab-oriented browser environment to test alternate security models, policies, and performance trade-offs. The effort highlights how modular, forkable architectures can reveal assumptions baked into modern browsers and the web platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在实验室环境中通过构建面向实验的浏览器环境，测试替代的安全模型、策略与性能权衡，以实现对 Web 的分叉实验。此举强调模块化、可分叉架构如何暴露现代浏览器与网络平台中的隐性假设。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48067786' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48067786' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=zR9PpXWsKFQ' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Production engineering when trading billions of dollars a day [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日交易额达数十亿美元时的生产工程实践 [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48067786' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A production engineer describes the live-in production practices behind billions of dollars in daily trades, covering latency budgets, incident response, and deployment discipline. The talk reveals how reliability engineering adapts to ultra-low latency, high-stakes environments where downtime is costly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位生产工程师介绍在日交易额达数十亿美元的金融系统中，可靠性实践如何落地，涵盖延迟预算、事件响应及部署规范。演讲揭示了在超低时延、高风险环境下，SRE 方法如何进行适应性调整，以避免昂贵的停机。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pomiferous.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pomiferous: The most extensive apples (pommes) database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pomiferous：最全面的苹果品种数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48009441' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pomiferous compiles a wide range of apple varieties with details such as flavor notes, origins, and imagery. It serves as a comprehensive reference for orchardists, researchers, and enthusiasts looking to compare cultivars.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pomiferous 汇集了大量苹果品种，附带风味、来源和图片等信息，成为种植者、研究者与爱好者的全面参考。该数据库还帮助比较品种特性、选育与园艺决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48085055' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当地人不知道的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48085055' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues that local communities are often unaware of how tech or policy changes affect them, underscoring the need for better communication and inclusive planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章揭示当地社区往往对影响他们的技术或政策毫不知情，强调需要更好的沟通与包容性规划。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bumbershootsoft.wordpress.com/2026/05/02/comparing-the-z80-and-6502-to-their-relatives/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Comparing the Z80 and 6502 to Their Relatives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Z80 与 6502 与它们的亲缘处理器对比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47993240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47993240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Comparisons of classic microprocessors with related designs illuminate tradeoffs in instruction sets, performance, and hardware constraints. The piece helps readers appreciate historical engineering decisions that shape modern CPUs and computing culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对经典微处理器 Z80 与 6502 及其同族设计的对比，揭示指令集、性能和硬件约束等权衡。帮助读者理解塑造现代 CPU 与计算文化的历史性工程决策。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47972922' data-tags='Science,Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47972922' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hpcwire.com/2026/02/05/what-is-z-angle-memory-and-why-is-intel-developing-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is Z-Angle Memory and why is Intel developing it?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么是 Z-Angle Memory？英特尔为何在开发它？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972922' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Z-Angle Memory is a novel memory concept being explored by Intel to improve data access patterns for HPC workloads. It hints at potential gains in bandwidth, latency, and energy efficiency, while outlining engineering challenges to bring it to production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Z-Angle Memory 是英特尔在探索的新型内存理念，旨在改善高性能计算工作负载的数据访问模式。其潜在收益包括带宽、延迟和能效的提升，同时也面临实现上的技术挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hpcwire.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/ZAM_memory_shutterstock_MeshCube.jpg' data-full='https://www.hpcwire.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/ZAM_memory_shutterstock_MeshCube.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48048902' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48048902' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/posts/making-your-own-programming-language.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making your own programming language is easier than you think (but also harder)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自己写一门编程语言比你想的容易，但也更难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48048902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48048902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48048902' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blog argues that with existing tooling and clear goals, creating a language is approachable, yet achieving real usability and performance remains hard. It provides practical steps and mental models for hobbyists and teams considering a language project.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博文认为在现有工具和明确目标的帮助下，编写一种语言并不如想象中那样困难，但要达到实用性与性能仍具挑战。文中给出面向爱好者和团队的实际步骤与思路。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002089' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Provides a primer on Atom, the open-source text editor, covering core features, package ecosystem, and typical workflows, while noting its evolving status in the editor landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍开源文本编辑器 Atom 的核心特性、插件生态与常见工作流，并讨论其在编辑器生态中的定位与演变。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47999460' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47999460' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8jNQDcpHc68' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I recreated the Apple Lisa computer inside an FPGA [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 FPGA 中重现 Apple Lisa 计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999460' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on project recreates the Apple Lisa architecture on an FPGA, offering a tangible tour of its hardware quirks and OS interactions. It provides nostalgia plus a practical view into how legacy systems can be prototyped today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过在 FPGA 上复现 Apple Lisa 的架构，展示了其硬件特点与与操作系统的交互。既有怀旧情怀，也展现了用现代现场可编程硬件原型化旧系统的可行性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47969142' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47969142' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/04/brain-scans-individual-versus-group.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Group averages obscure how an individual&#x27;s brain controls behavior: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>群体平均掩盖个人大脑对行为的控制方式：研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47969142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47969142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47969142' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study finds that averaging brain data across groups hides how an individual brain maps to behavior. Individual-level analyses reveal patterns that group means miss, challenging one-size-fits-all interpretations. The work supports personalized brain models and cautions against over-reliance on group averages in neuroscience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项研究发现，将大脑数据按组平均会掩盖个体脑部如何映射到行为的方式。个体级分析揭示了群体均值所忽略的模式，挑战了“一个方案适用于所有人”的解读。研究强调需要建立个性化的大脑模型，并警示不要过度依赖群体平均数据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/04/brain-scans-individual-versus-group/_jcr_content/cq:socialfeaturedimage.coreimg.jpeg/1777295241330/Menon-brain-scans.jpeg' data-full='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/04/brain-scans-individual-versus-group/_jcr_content/cq:socialfeaturedimage.coreimg.jpeg/1777295241330/Menon-brain-scans.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47986331' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47986331' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.docker.com/engine/storage/containerd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Docker 29 has changed its default image store for new installs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Docker 29 已改变新安装的默认镜像存储</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47986331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47986331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47986331' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docker 29 now uses containerd&#x27;s content store as the default image store for new installs, changing how images are stored and managed. Operators should plan migrations and update CI/CD pipelines accordingly. It may affect performance characteristics and deduplication behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docker 29 将新安装的默认镜像存储改为 containerd 的内容存储，改变镜像的存放与管理方式。运维需规划迁移并相应调整 CI/CD 流程，可能影响性能与去重等特性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://docs.docker.com/images/thumbnail.webp' data-full='https://docs.docker.com/images/thumbnail.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48067197' data-tags='Programming,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48067197' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.poppastring.com/blog/what-we-lost-the-last-time-code-got-cheap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What we lost the last time code got cheap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>上一次代码变便宜时我们失去了什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48067197' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A meditation on what was lost when software development grew cheaper: increased tech debt, brittle systems, and reduced emphasis on long-term maintainability. The piece argues for sustainable engineering practices, thoughtful trade-offs, and valuing developer time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章审视当软件开发成本下降时带来的负面影响：技术负债上升、系统脆弱，以及对长期可维护性的忽视。提出坚持可持续的工程实践、理性权衡并重视开发者时间的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.poppastring.com/blog/content/binary/cricket-ball-made-in-india.jpg' data-full='https://www.poppastring.com/blog/content/binary/cricket-ball-made-in-india.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48065254' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48065254' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sigops.org/2026/can-llms-model-real-world-systems-in-tla/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can LLMs model real-world systems in TLA+?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLMs 能否用 TLA+ 对现实世界系统建模？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48065254' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It discusses the risks of inaccuracies in formal analysis and the potential for hybrid workflows where LLMs assist human experts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨大型语言模型是否能够用 TLA+ 为现实系统生成准确的形式化规范，并分析语言模型在严格建模中的局限性与风险。也提出将 LLM 作为辅助工具，与人类专家共同工作以提高建模质量与效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sigops.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/sysmobench-1672x941.jpg' data-full='https://www.sigops.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/sysmobench-1672x941.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48056749' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48056749' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/robertdfrench/ifuncd-up' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GNU IFUNC is the real culprit behind CVE-2024-3094</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GNU IFUNC是CVE-2024-3094真正罪魁祸首</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056749' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical analysis argues that GNU IFUNC is the true driver behind CVE-2024-3094, highlighting how dynamic symbol resolution can create attack surfaces in software. The piece serves as a cautionary note about low-level kernel and libc vulnerabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>技术分析声称GNU IFUNC才是CVE-2024-3094的真正根因，强调动态符号解析如何在软件中暴露攻击面。这一结论提醒开发者关注底层内核与C库的潜在漏洞。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/824707729/6c1c05bd-59f2-4874-af77-31faee285f2a' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/824707729/6c1c05bd-59f2-4874-af77-31faee285f2a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47997915' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47997915' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://academy.dair.ai/blog/wiki-builder-claude-code-plugin' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wiki Builder: Skill to Build LLM Knowledge Bases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wiki Builder：构建LLM知识库的技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47997915' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wiki Builder helps tailor LLMs by assembling and curating knowledge bases, enabling scalable, searchable references for AI systems. It promotes structured knowledge integration to reduce hallucinations and improve factual accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wiki Builder 能通过整合和整理知识库，帮助大语言模型更好地获取可信参考。这种结构化的知识整合有助于降低幻觉、提升事实准确性，并支持大规模知识管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://academy.dair.ai/blog/wiki-builder-claude-code-plugin/cover.png' data-full='https://academy.dair.ai/blog/wiki-builder-claude-code-plugin/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48063548' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48063548' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/regent-vcs/re_gent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Git for AI Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：面向 AI 代理的 Git</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48063548' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces a Git-inspired tool tailored for AI-agent workflows, enabling reproducible traces of actions and state management across agents. This project demonstrates how version-control concepts can be extended to cognitive processes and collaborative AI tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 介绍了一款面向 AI 代理工作流的 Git 风格工具，支持跨代理的可重复行动追踪与状态管理。该项目展示了将版本控制概念扩展到认知过程与协作型 AI 任务的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7a37b97e0c56aa938fa59c19efe36fd85f5c63c7e05d4fffd222f81ed55b3158/regent-vcs/re_gent' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7a37b97e0c56aa938fa59c19efe36fd85f5c63c7e05d4fffd222f81ed55b3158/regent-vcs/re_gent' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48004380' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48004380' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.firgelli.com/pages/humanoid-robot-actuators' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Humanoid Robot Actuators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人形机器人执行器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48004380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48004380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48004380' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of actuator options suitable for humanoid robots, focusing on electric and servo-based joints. It highlights how choices differ in force, speed, precision, and power efficiency, guiding designers to balance payload with compact, modular packaging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述适用于人形机器人的一系列执行器，聚焦电动和伺服关节的选型。讨论了力矩、速度、精度与能效等因素，帮助设计者在承载能力与紧凑、模块化结构之间做权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48015218' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/may/04/new-orleans-sea-levels-relocation-climate-crisis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Point of no return&#x27;: New Orleans relocation must start now due to sea level</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>‘不可回头点’：海平面上升推动新奥尔良迁移必须现在就开始</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48015218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48015218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48015218' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Climate risk places New Orleans at a critical junction where relocation may become inevitable. The piece argues planning now—policy, funding, and infrastructure—will determine whether a managed retreat can be orderly rather than chaotic, with implications for coastal cities globally.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>气候风险将新奥尔良推向一个关键节点，迁移可能成为不可避免的选项。文章强调要现在就开展规划，包括政策、资金与基础设施建设，以确保后续的可控、有序的退迁，而这对全球沿海城市也具重要启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/8b57e9379868c36a7d4ef87c011431873c0099ad/283_0_1751_1400/master/1751.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=25ffafad98aae9965497c4f9ae0c8e78' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/8b57e9379868c36a7d4ef87c011431873c0099ad/283_0_1751_1400/master/1751.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=25ffafad98aae9965497c4f9ae0c8e78' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47999520' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47999520' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lelanthran.com/chap15/content.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs Are Not a Higher Level of Abstraction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLMs 不是更高层次的抽象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999520' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Contends that large language models are not simply a new abstraction layer; they introduce novel failure modes, data leakage risks, and demand new cost, latency, and reliability considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张大型语言模型并非只是新的一层抽象，它们带来新的故障模式、数据泄露风险，并需要在成本、延迟与可靠性方面采取新策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47997056' data-tags='DevOps,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47997056' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simpleobservability.com/docs/alert-driven-monitoring' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alert-driven monitoring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于告警驱动的监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47997056' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alert-Driven Monitoring describes a monitoring approach where alerts guide the instrumentation and response workflows. It argues for designing actionable alerts with clear runbooks and automation to reduce noise and speed incident containment. The concept complements existing metrics dashboards by focusing on what actually requires action.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>告警驱动的监控将告警作为驱动，将监控的编排和响应流程引导。主张以可操作的告警、清晰的处置手册与自动化为设计核心，以减少噪声并提升事件处置速度。该理念与现有的指标仪表板互补，聚焦于真正需要采取行动的场景。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48040306' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48040306' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mceglowski.substack.com/p/bitter-lessons-from-the-isspresso' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitter Lessons from the ISSpresso</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自 ISSpresso 的苦涩教训</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48040306' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shares hard-won lessons from ISSpresso, focusing on reliability engineering in extreme environments and how small design or testing gaps become costly in space. The takeaway: design for robustness, rigorous testing, and clear failure modes to avoid expensive, deterministic failures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述 ISSpresso 这类极端环境下的工程教训，聚焦可靠性设计、充分测试和清晰的故障模式。结论是要以稳健性思维来避免昂贵的失败。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/are-prediction-markets-good-for-anything' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Looking at the data behind prediction markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>洞察预测市场背后的数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48049014' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An examination of the data generated by prediction markets reveals how liquidity, participation biases, and event framing affect accuracy and informativeness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对预测市场产生的数据进行分析，揭示流动性、参与偏差和事件框架如何影响预测准确性与信息含量。数据所呈现的价格信号在哪些情形下可能误导决策者，并据此提出在使用此类市场进行预测时的注意事项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theparisreview.org/blog/2026/05/06/rotten-dot-com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rotten Dot Com</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rotten Dot Com（腐点网）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48082039' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece revisits the infamous Rotten Dot Com, tracing its place in early internet culture and its enduring influence on web aesthetics, humor, and user-generated-content boundaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇回顾性文章回顾了臭名昭著的 Rotten Dot Com，梳理其在早期互联网文化中的地位及对网页美学、幽默感和用户生成内容边界的长久影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.theparisreview.org/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/default.jpg' data-full='https://www.theparisreview.org/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/default.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48000507' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48000507' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ambrook.com/offrange/environment/a-forest-in-your-pocket' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Make your own microforest (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在家自造微森林</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48000507' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to creating a microforest in a small urban space, outlining species selection, planting density, and care using approaches like Miyawaki. It shows how individuals can boost biodiversity and urban cooling with tiny forests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在小型城市空间内自制微森林的要点，涵盖物种选择、密植与养护，采用宫崎式等方法。强调个人也能通过小小森林提升生物多样性和城市降温效果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/h6kk644c/production/97e738f9bc64d3cef96f0f3cc50e758edf052af0-2000x1050.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/h6kk644c/production/97e738f9bc64d3cef96f0f3cc50e758edf052af0-2000x1050.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48064789' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48064789' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.databricks.com/blog/how-lakebase-architecture-delivers-5x-faster-postgres-writes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lakebase architecture delivers faster Postgres writes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lakebase 架构让 Postgres 写入速度提升 5 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48064789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48064789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48064789' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Databricks’ Lakebase architecture promises a fivefold improvement in PostgreSQL write throughput by combining lakehouse data management with optimized storage and compute separation. This could reshape data pipelines for analytics workloads, but adoption will hinge on compatibility and operational complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lakebase 架构通过整合数据湖管理、存储与计算分离等技术，承诺将 PostgreSQL 的写入吞吐提升约五倍。这可能重塑分析型工作负载的数据管线，但实际落地取决于与现有系统的兼容性与运维复杂性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2026-05/2025-10-blog-lakebase-fully-managed-postgres-database-integrated-with-databricks-og-1200x628-2x.png' data-full='https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2026-05/2025-10-blog-lakebase-fully-managed-postgres-database-integrated-with-databricks-og-1200x628-2x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48010208' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48010208' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://globalnews.ca/news/11828244/alberta-voter-list-leak-public-safety-disaster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alberta voter list leak is a potential public safety disaster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔伯塔省选民名单泄露可能带来公共安全隐患</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48010208' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alberta&#x27;s voter list leak poses privacy and safety risks, enabling potential doxxing and manipulation. The incident highlights the stakes of safeguarding voter data and the need for rapid remediation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿尔伯塔省选民名单泄露带来隐私和公共安全风险，可能被用于定向攻击、骚扰或误导。事件暴露了保护选民数据的紧迫性和修复工作的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://globalnews.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/44a3eb6eb1d9781bef3d1812468415ff2f9414368429229e6baf73bf7fdb6613.jpg?quality=65&amp;strip=all&amp;w=720&amp;h=379&amp;crop=1' data-full='https://globalnews.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/44a3eb6eb1d9781bef3d1812468415ff2f9414368429229e6baf73bf7fdb6613.jpg?quality=65&amp;strip=all&amp;w=720&amp;h=379&amp;crop=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48010266' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sierra.ai/blog/better-customer-experiences-built-on-sierra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sierra Raises $950M at $15B Valuation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sierra 融资9.5亿美元，估值150亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48010266' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sierra raises a $950M round at a $15B valuation to boost its AI-powered customer experience platform. The funding signals ongoing appetite for enterprise software that blends automation and analytics with customer workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sierra 完成9.5亿美元融资，估值达150亿美元，计划扩展其基于 AI 的客户体验平台。此次融资体现了市场对将自动化和分析能力嵌入客户工作流的企业软件的持续需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sierra.ai/-/cdn/image?src=https%3A%2F%2Fcdn.sanity.io%2Fimages%2Fca4jck6w%2Fproduction%2Fe6ba33e842c9c6b82954a73d3b9b464770b5e8c4-2400x1260.png&amp;width=1200&amp;quality=75' data-full='https://sierra.ai/-/cdn/image?src=https%3A%2F%2Fcdn.sanity.io%2Fimages%2Fca4jck6w%2Fproduction%2Fe6ba33e842c9c6b82954a73d3b9b464770b5e8c4-2400x1260.png&amp;width=1200&amp;quality=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48009879' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ikesau.co/blog/dithering-with-css/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dithering with CSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS 抖动技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48009879' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSS dithering combines techniques like gradients and color quantization to simulate more colors or smooth gradients on limited palettes. The piece offers practical patterns and cautions about performance and visual artifacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSS 抖动通过渐变、颜色量化等方法，在受限调色板中模拟更多颜色或平滑渐变。文中给出实用做法与在性能与视觉伪影之间的权衡注意点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ikesau.co/images/squidward-future.png' data-full='https://ikesau.co/images/squidward-future.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48010774' data-tags='AI,Education,Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48010774' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/literacy-in-future-technologies-artificial-intelligence-act-adam-schiff-mike-rounds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI, Google, and Microsoft Back Bill to Fund &#x27;AI Literacy&#x27; in Schools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI、谷歌和微软支持资助学校的 AI 素养教育法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48010774' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI, Google, and Microsoft back a bill to fund AI literacy in schools, signaling a push to integrate AI education into K-12. The move suggests public-private collaboration to prepare students for an AI-driven economy, with debates over curriculum, privacy, and equity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI、Google 和微软支持资助学校 AI 素养教育的法案，标志着将 AI 教育纳入 K12 的公共与私营协作。此举旨在为学生应对 AI 驱动的经济做好准备，同时在课程设置、隐私与教育公平等方面引发讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1598032604570-d65c6680d246?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDR8fGNoaWxkJTIwb24lMjBhJTIwY29tcHV0ZXIlMjBpbiUyMHNjaG9vbHxlbnwwfHx8fDE3Nzc5MDQ2NDd8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1598032604570-d65c6680d246?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDR8fGNoaWxkJTIwb24lMjBhJTIwY29tcHV0ZXIlMjBpbiUyMHNjaG9vbHxlbnwwfHx8fDE3Nzc5MDQ2NDd8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48089289' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48089289' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jamesshore.com/v2/blog/2026/you-need-ai-that-reduces-your-maintenance-costs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI coding agent, used to write code, needs to reduce your maintenance costs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于编写代码的 AI 编码代理需要降低你的维护成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48089289' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI coding agents should focus on reducing maintenance costs, not just code generation speed. It emphasizes durable interfaces, testability, and thoughtful architecture as drivers of long-term productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出 AI 编码代理应以降低维护成本为目标，而不仅仅追求代码产出速度，强调稳定接口、可测试性与良好架构对长期生产力的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jamesshore.com/images/site/title-696x290.png' data-full='https://www.jamesshore.com/images/site/title-696x290.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48075429' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48075429' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/health/wellness/hearing-loss-walking-speed-iphone-study-c53c482a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Walking slower? Your ears, not your knees, might be the problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>走路变慢？耳朵可能是关键，而非膝盖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48075429' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study links age-related hearing loss to slower walking speed, suggesting auditory health impacts mobility in older adults. Using data from a large-scale smartphone-based study, researchers show a notable association that could motivate early hearing interventions to preserve independence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究将与年龄相关的听力损失与步速减慢联系起来，表明听力健康会影响老年人的行动能力。基于大规模手机数据的研究显示出显著关联，可能推动及早听力干预以维持独立性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48047082' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48047082' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://firethering.com/zaya1-8b-open-source-math-coding-model/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ZAYA1-8B matches DeepSeek-R1 on math with less than 1B active parameters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ZAYA1-8B 以不到 1B 激活参数的规模，在数学推理任务中达到与 DeepSeek-R1 相当的水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48047082' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-source LLM ZAYA1-8B achieves math performance on par with DeepSeek-R1 with under 1B active parameters. The result highlights progress toward compact, on-device capable models and the potential for accessible high-quality math reasoning in smaller footprints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ZAYA1-8B 以不到 1B 激活参数的规模，在数学推理任务中达到与 DeepSeek-R1 相当的水平。此进展推动了在边缘设备上运行高效、可访问的高质量推理的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://firethering.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/zaya1-8B-Ai-model.jpg' data-full='https://firethering.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/zaya1-8B-Ai-model.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
