<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48035617' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/shrinkflation-is-quietly-making-all-gadgets-worse-2000754565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RAM prices are forcing companies to choose higher prices, worse specs, or both</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RAM 价格上涨迫使厂商在提高价格与降低规格之间做出权衡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48035617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48035617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48035617' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RAM price hikes are forcing companies to either raise prices, compromise on specs, or both, leading to shrinkflation in gadgets and affecting consumer and enterprise hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAM 价格上涨迫使厂商在提高价格与降低规格之间做出权衡，导致消费电子与企业硬件出现‘收缩性’现象。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48052333' data-tags='Programming,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48052333' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/trevin/status/2051316002730991795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Principles for agent-native CLIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向代理的原生 CLI 原则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48052333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48052333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48052333' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Designing CLIs that work with AI agents requires clear guidance on reliability, observability, and security. The principles outline how to keep interactions predictable, support reproducible results, and avoid leaking prompts or secret data. These guidelines help engineers build safer, more maintainable AI-powered tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为 AI 代理设计原生 CLI 需要强调可靠性、可观测性与安全性。原则强调保持交互可预测、结果可复现，并防止提示或敏感数据泄露，帮助工程师构建更安全、易维护的 AI 工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHN5QmAaYAA-lJO.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHN5QmAaYAA-lJO.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48077231' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun was ported to Rust in six days, illustrating rapid cross-language porting and potential gains in safety and performance. The anecdote reflects trends in rewriting performance-critical runtimes in Rust and what it means for the JavaScript ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 在六天内完成对 Rust 的移植，展示了快速跨语言移植的可行性以及在安全性和性能方面的潜在收益。此事反映出用 Rust 重写性能关键运行时组件的趋势及其对 JavaScript 生态的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48061602' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joachimschipper.nl/Stop%20MITM%20on%20the%20first%20SSH%20connection,%20on%20any%20VPS%20or%20cloud%20provider.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop MitM on the first SSH connection, on any VPS or cloud provider</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在首次 SSH 连接时阻止 MITM 攻击：适用于任意 VPS/云提供商</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48061602' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to preventing MITM on the very first SSH connection across clouds and VPS providers. It emphasizes out-of-band host key verification, pinning, and robust initial trust strategies to reduce supply-chain-like risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供在首次 SSH 连接时防止 MITM 攻击的实用指南，适用于任意云与 VPS 提供商。强调带外验证、主机密钥固定以及稳健的初始信任策略，以降低类似供应链的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48011781' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/features/2026-05-03/trillions-in-us-retirement-dollars-flow-into-opaque-trusts-that-rival-etfs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trillions in Retirement Dollars Flow into Opaque Trusts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>退休资金流入不透明信托基金的规模达万亿级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48011781' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trillions of retirement dollars flow into opaque trusts that rival ETFs, raising questions about transparency and governance. The trend could affect market efficiency and investor protection, calling for clearer reporting and oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数万亿退休资金流入不透明信托基金，规模甚至可与 ETF 相媲美，引发对透明度、治理与投资者保护的担忧。此趋势可能影响市场效率，需要更明确的披露和监管。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48066038' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48066038' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/05/08/court-to-doge-bros-asking-chatgpt-yo-is-this-dei-is-not-proper-legal-process-also-a-first-amendment-violation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court to DOGE: Asking ChatGPT &#x27;Is This DEI?&#x27; Is Not Proper Legal Process</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院对 DOGE：让 ChatGPT 判断“这是否属于 DEI？”不是合适的法律程序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48066038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48066038' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court admonishes a legal filing where an argument relies on ChatGPT outputs to assess DEI compliance, clarifying that AI-generated conclusions are not a substitute for evidence or legal reasoning. The ruling also touches on First Amendment concerns around AI assistance in legal processes and underscores the need for human oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院警告，使用 ChatGPT 判断“这是否属于 DEI”的做法并非正当的法律程序，强调 AI 产出不能替代证据与法律推理。判决还涉及与 AI 辅助相关的第一修正案问题，强调需要人为监督与校验法律材料。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48026412' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48026412' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/PySimpleGUI/PySimpleGUI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PySimpleGUI 6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PySimpleGUI 6 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48026412' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PySimpleGUI 6 brings further simplifications to building cross-platform GUIs with minimal code. The update focuses on a stable API, easier layout handling, and broader widget options, lowering the barrier for developers to ship quick desktop tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PySimpleGUI 6 在跨平台 GUI 的开发上继续简化，强调稳定 API、简化布局处理与扩展的小部件选项，降低开发者快速交付桌面工具的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/140614233/b1327c00-8320-11eb-8e4d-25dff36db261' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/140614233/b1327c00-8320-11eb-8e4d-25dff36db261' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48036190' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48036190' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mendelgreenberg.com/posts/ourcar/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OurCar: What I learned making an app for my family</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>给家人做一个应用学到的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48036190' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on building a family-focused app, with lessons on simplicity, accessibility, and privacy. It shows how designing for non-technical users exposes trade-offs between feature richness and ease of use, maintenance, and data governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享为家庭开发应用的经验教训，聚焦简洁性、可访问性与隐私等要点。设计面向非技术用户的产品时，需在功能丰富性与易用性、维护成本与数据治理之间做权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mendelgreenberg.com/_assets/Simulator%20Screenshot%20-%20iPhone%2016e%20-%202026-05-01%20at%2016.51.22.56podho6_2eCe9G.webp' data-full='https://mendelgreenberg.com/_assets/Simulator%20Screenshot%20-%20iPhone%2016e%20-%202026-05-01%20at%2016.51.22.56podho6_2eCe9G.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48040470' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48040470' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.colorado.edu/today/2026/05/06/light-without-electricity-glowing-algae-could-make-it-possible' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Light without electricity? Glowing algae could make it possible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无电照明？发光藻类或可实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48040470' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes progress toward lighting without grid power using bioluminescent algae, exploring sustainable, low-energy illumination tech. Notes the engineering challenges and potential niche applications where glowing algae could complement or replace electrical lighting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍通过发光藻类实现无电照明的最新进展，探讨其在低功耗、可持续照明方面的潜力。也提到实现现实世界应用需要面对的工程挑战和适用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.colorado.edu/today/sites/default/files/2026-05/Buffalo_Acid%20triggered_HighRes.png' data-full='https://www.colorado.edu/today/sites/default/files/2026-05/Buffalo_Acid%20triggered_HighRes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48026773' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48026773' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cadence.moe/blog/2026-05-05-what-makes-a-good-smartphone-camera' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What makes a good smartphone camera?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>优秀手机摄像头的关键是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48026773' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyzes the factors behind a great smartphone camera, including sensor size, optics, ISP, and software processing. It emphasizes that best results emerge from optimizing the entire stack, not just hardware specs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析决定手机摄像头质量的关键因素，如传感器尺寸、镜头、图像信号处理和算法软件。强调最佳效果来自硬件与软件的全面协同，而不仅仅是硬件规格。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cadence.moe/static/img/Censor_PGb.gif?statichash=604c5a028209f01a20239e9f5ffa69768b0279b87115c1345f84c743aaaacd05' data-full='https://cadence.moe/static/img/Censor_PGb.gif?statichash=604c5a028209f01a20239e9f5ffa69768b0279b87115c1345f84c743aaaacd05' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47988781' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wr9YLGbhxng' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>World&#x27;s biggest RC A380 [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球最大的遥控 A380 模型 [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47988781' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video showcases a colossal RC Airbus A380, illustrating hobbyist scale modeling at extreme size. It highlights the engineering challenges, flight dynamics, and community around giant model aircraft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段视频展现了全球最大的遥控 A380 机模型，展示极大尺寸模型的工程挑战与飞行物理。社区对巨型模型飞机的热情及相关技术也得到展现。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47976081' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47976081' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/a-treasure-trove-of-cambrian-fossils-rewrites-the-story-of-early-life-20260501/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A treasure trove of fossils rewrites the story of early life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一批化石宝藏改写早期生命故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47976081' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Cambrian fossils reveal more about early multicellular life and ancient ecosystems, suggesting complexity and ecological interactions appeared earlier than previously thought. The finds fill gaps in the record and refine our understanding of life&#x27;s beginnings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一批罕见且保存完好的寒武纪化石揭示了早期多细胞生物和古代生态系统的更多细节，显示比此前预期更早出现的复杂性与生态互动。这些发现填补了化石记录的空白，推动对生命起源的认识。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/New-Canbrian-Fossils-cr.Han-Zeng-et.al-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/New-Canbrian-Fossils-cr.Han-Zeng-et.al-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48017641' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48017641' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/yattsu/biscuit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Biscuit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Biscuit 项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48017641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48017641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48017641' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biscuit is a new token/authorization framework emphasizing expressive, capability-based access control with more flexible delegation and policy composition beyond traditional tokens.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Biscuit 是一个新兴的代币/授权框架，强调表达能力更强的能力型访问控制，支持更灵活的权限委托与策略管理。旨在超越传统张贴式令牌，提升细粒度访问控制的可组合性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c99ceb642f2336bbd0c04aa23af8b5b93672bb028f95804477e1573f30946cb5/yattsu/biscuit' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c99ceb642f2336bbd0c04aa23af8b5b93672bb028f95804477e1573f30946cb5/yattsu/biscuit' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48012477' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48012477' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.taipeitimes.com/News/front/archives/2026/04/28/2003856358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Offenders sentenced up to 10 years for spying on TSMC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对窃取台积电情报的罪犯判刑最高可达十年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012477' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Five individuals were sentenced for spying on Taiwan&#x27;s TSMC, underscoring the importance of IP protection in global semiconductors. The case shows how espionage threats can ripple through supply chains and national security, prompting stronger deterrence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五名涉案人员因窃取台积电情报被判刑，凸显全球半导体产业对知识产权保护的高度重视。此案揭示间谍活动可能对供应链与国家安全产生影响，强化了威慑力度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.taipeitimes.com/images/2026/04/28/P01-260428-601.jpg' data-full='https://img.taipeitimes.com/images/2026/04/28/P01-260428-601.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48049828' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48049828' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kohlschuetter.github.io/blog/posts/2026/05/07/serial-ttl-connector/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Serial TTL connector we deserve</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们应得的 Serial TTL 连接器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48049828' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A design-focused post presenting a better Serial TTL connector, addressing common pain points like level shifting, wiring simplicity, and reliability. It argues for a more user-friendly interface to streamline hardware prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出一种更友好的 Serial TTL 连接器设计，聚焦电平转换、接线简易性和长期可靠性等痛点。目标是让硬件原型开发更顺畅、降低接线错误率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kohlschuetter.github.io/blog/assets/posts/2026/05/07/serial-ttl-connector/before.jpg' data-full='https://kohlschuetter.github.io/blog/assets/posts/2026/05/07/serial-ttl-connector/before.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48058644' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48058644' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/graemeg/blaise' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blaise – A modern self-hosting zero-legacy Object Pascal compiler targeting QBE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Blaise：面向 QBE 的现代自托管、零遗留 Object Pascal 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48058644' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blaise is a modern self-hosting Object Pascal compiler that aims to shed legacy baggage and target the QBE backend. The project emphasizes self-hosting capabilities and a clean, minimal toolchain, enabling Pascal development with a contemporary stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Blaise 是一个现代的自托管 Object Pascal 编译器，目标是抛弃遗留负担并对接 QBE 后端。该项目强调自托管能力和简洁的工具链，为 Pascal 开发提供现代化的堆栈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/22e7eeb378c4c52b8aebce40a606f3daf2c0ecc02cb480c33d30c068920071aa/graemeg/blaise' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/22e7eeb378c4c52b8aebce40a606f3daf2c0ecc02cb480c33d30c068920071aa/graemeg/blaise' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48021023' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48021023' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ivanpleshkov.dev/blog/polynomial-autoencoder/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A polynomial autoencoder beats PCA on transformer embeddings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多项式自编码器在 Transformer 嵌入上超越 PCA</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48021023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48021023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48021023' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A polynomial autoencoder—leveraging nonlinear polynomial interactions—outperforms PCA at compressing transformer embeddings, capturing more structure in a smaller latent space. This points to more efficient representations for large models, with potential savings in storage and downstream compute.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种利用非线性多项式交互的多项式自编码器，在压缩 Transformer 嵌入时优于 PCA，能够在更小的潜在空间中保留更多结构信息。这为大型模型提供了更高效的表示，可能降低存储与下游计算成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ivanpleshkov.dev/og/blog/en/polynomial-autoencoder.png' data-full='https://ivanpleshkov.dev/og/blog/en/polynomial-autoencoder.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48043184' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48043184' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/travel/article/20260506-what-british-people-really-mean-when-they-say-sorry' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What British people mean when they say &#x27;sorry&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国人说对不起时的真实含义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48043184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48043184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48043184' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Arguably, saying sorry in Britain often serves as social lubrication rather than an admission of fault.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在英国，说‘对不起’往往是社交润滑剂，而非承认错误。文章解析不同场景下的语用差异，帮助读者理解这一礼貌用法背后的社会规范。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0njlkyj.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0njlkyj.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Apple Network Server 2.0 ROM 上测试 macOS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47998158' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Documents experiments running macOS on Apple Network Server hardware ROMs, a retro computing exploration illustrating hardware-software compatibility challenges. It highlights the quirks of vintage Apple hardware and the ingenuity of hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>记录在 Apple Network Server 2.0 的 ROM 上测试 macOS 的实验，展示了软硬件兼容性挑战的复古计算探索，突显老苹果硬件的怪癖与爱好者的创造力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48056359' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48056359' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cam.ac.uk/research/news/gambling-ads-on-social-media-reach-more-than-twice-as-many-men-as-women' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gambling ads on social media reach more than twice as many men as women: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>社交媒体赌博广告对男性覆盖量是女性的两倍以上：研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056359' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Study finds gambling ads on social platforms reach twice as many men as women, signaling gendered exposure and potential risk, particularly for vulnerable groups. The result has implications for platform advertising policies and public health considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现社交媒体上的博彩广告对男性的覆盖量明显高于女性，超过两倍，揭示性别化曝光与潜在风险，尤其对易受影响群体。该结果对平台广告政策与公共卫生具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cam.ac.uk/sites/default/files/news/news/gettyimages-1426353191-dp.jpg' data-full='https://www.cam.ac.uk/sites/default/files/news/news/gettyimages-1426353191-dp.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47996668' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47996668' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/mit-engineers-virtual-violin-produces-realistic-sounds-0429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Virtual violin produces realistic sounds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>虚拟小提琴发出逼真声音</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47996668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47996668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47996668' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MIT researchers have created a virtual violin capable of producing highly realistic sounds, blending physical modeling and DSP/AI to capture expressive nuances. This could boost digital instrument libraries, VR/AR experiences, and remote collaborations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>麻省理工研究人员开发的虚拟小提琴可产生极其逼真的音色，结合物理建模与数字信号处理，捕捉细腻的情感表达。将推动数字乐器库、VR/AR 体验和远程协作的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202604/MIT-Strad-Model-01-press.jpg' data-full='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202604/MIT-Strad-Model-01-press.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48014868' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.os2museum.com/wp/when-networking-doesnt-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When networking doesn&#x27;t work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网络不工作时的反思</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48014868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48014868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48014868' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>When Networking Doesn&#x27;t Work reviews historical network failures and the lessons they offer for modern infrastructure, from protocol quirks to firmware bugs. It emphasizes designing for resilience, observability, and easier debugging. A reminder that even decades-old issues still inform today’s best practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《当网络不工作时》回顾了历史上的网络故障及其对现代基础设施的启示，涵盖协议缺陷、固件问题等方面。强调在设计中考虑韧性、可观测性和更易排错的能力。提醒我们即使是多年前的问题也能指引今天的最佳实践。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48084710' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48084710' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/924933/google-chrome-4gb-gemini-nano-ai-features' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome&#x27;s AI features may be hogging 4GB of your computer storage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 的 AI 功能可能占用你电脑 4GB 存储</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48084710' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome&#x27;s AI features may store sizable model data locally, potentially consuming up to 4GB of disk space. This matters for devices with limited storage and prompts considerations about managing features, storage impact, and user control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 的 AI 功能可能在本地存储大量模型数据，甚至占用多达 4GB 的磁盘空间。这对存储容量有限的设备影响较大，提醒用户在功能开启与存储管理之间做出权衡与取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/24418650/STK114_Google_Chrome_01.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/24418650/STK114_Google_Chrome_01.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.engadget.com/big-tech/someone-allegedly-used-a-hairdryer-to-rig-polymarket-weather-bets-155312411.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone allegedly used a hairdryer to rig Polymarket weather bets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>据称有人用吹风机篡改 Polymarket 天气赌注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48008326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48008326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48008326' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alleged incident where a hairdryer was used to influence weather data feeding Polymarket bets raises concerns about oracle integrity and platform safeguards. The episode underscores the fragility of real-world data inputs in prediction markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称有人成使用吹风机干预 Polymarket 的天气赌注，暴露了预言市场对真实世界数据输入的脆弱性及平台防护的挑战。此事件凸显了数据源可信度在市场设计中的关键性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.engadget.com/engadget/someone-allegedly-used-a-hairdryer-to-rig-polymarket-weather-bets-155312411/30e17760-252b-11f1-83c9-0a94a50264da.jpg' data-full='https://www.engadget.com/engadget/someone-allegedly-used-a-hairdryer-to-rig-polymarket-weather-bets-155312411/30e17760-252b-11f1-83c9-0a94a50264da.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48017251' data-tags='Privacy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48017251' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nobodyaskedforthis.lol/posts/connected-car/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Car That Watches You Back: The Advertising Infrastructure of Modern Cars</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>会回看你的车：现代汽车的广告基础设施</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48017251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48017251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48017251' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern connected cars collect data via sensors and telemetry to support personalized advertising and content. The piece maps data flows from sensors to third-party networks, raising privacy concerns and urging stronger user controls and policy guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代汽车通过传感器和遥测数据收集乘客与车辆信息，用于定向广告和个性化内容，形成车载广告技术栈。文中梳理了从数据采集到与第三方合作伙伴的数据流，引发隐私与同意方面的担忧，并呼吁更强的用户控制和政策约束。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look back at Guy Goma&#x27;s accidental BBC interview and how the moment shaped his life and media culture, reflecting on the fragility of live TV and the long-tail of viral fame.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Guy Goma 在 BBC 的意外采访如何塑造了他的生活与媒体文化，反映直播时代身份暴露的脆弱性以及病毒式传播的长期影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48029261' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://singleride.nyc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SingleRide: Longest route on NYC Subway without visiting the same station twice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SingleRide：纽约地铁中尽量不重复经过车站的最长路线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48029261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48029261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48029261' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SingleRide documents attempts to find the longest route through the NYC Subway without repeating stations, illustrating an interesting graph-traversal puzzle in a real-world network. It highlights the algorithmic limits of longest simple paths and how network structure constrains exploration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SingleRide 记录了在纽约地铁网络中尽量不重复经过任意车站的最长路线，揭示了现实网络中的图遍历难题。该项目体现了在大规模网络中寻找最长简单路径的理论极限与实际限制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48065916' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48065916' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.stroustrup.com/bs_faq2.html#memory-leaks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do I deal with memory leaks? (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bjarne Stroustrup：如何处理内存泄漏？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48065916' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bjarne Stroustrup discusses memory leaks in a 2022 FAQ, recommending modern C++ practices like RAII, smart pointers, and careful resource management. He emphasizes designing code to avoid leaks from the start and using tooling to detect leaks early.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴兰·斯特鲁斯特鲁普在 2022 年的问答中谈及内存泄漏，推荐现代 C++ 实践如 RAII、智能指针和谨慎的资源管理，强调从设计阶段就避免泄漏，并使用工具尽早发现问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare details its response to the Copy Fail Linux vulnerability, outlining mitigation steps, traffic rules, and deployment strategies to limit risk. The incident highlights how edge networks must rapidly adapt to kernel flaws that could affect TLS, caching, and DDoS protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 介绍了对 Copy Fail Linux 漏洞的应对策略，包括缓解步骤、流量控制和部署策略，以降低风险。此事件凸显边缘网络需要快速应对可能影响 TLS、缓存和抗 DDoS 能力的内核漏洞。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/ml/all/20260507070547.2268452-1-sashal@kernel.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Killswitch: Per-function short-circuit mitigation primitive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Killswitch：按函数粒度的短路缓解原语</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Killswitch proposes a per-function short-circuit primitive to quickly disable misbehaving code paths, limiting damage from bugs or exploits. The mechanism balances safety and performance, enabling rapid containment without global exits. The write-up covers design points, potential integration points, and trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Killswitch 提出一个按函数粒度的短路缓解原语，用于快速禁用异常函数路径，从而将漏洞或错误的影响控制在最小范围内。该机制在安全性与性能之间寻求折中，方便快速收容问题而不影响整体系统。文章讨论了设计要点、潜在的集成点与权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>野生动物保护工作强调河狸的显著回升，得益于栖息地修复、污染治理和保护走廊的建设。文章讨论了对生态系统的连锁效应，以及河狸在城市与野生生物共存中的案例意义。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48045778' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The journey to generate PDFs in the browser with selectable text exposes intricate challenges around fonts, encoding, and cross-browser behavior. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器中生成可选中文本的 PDF 面临字体嵌入、编码及跨浏览器行为等复杂挑战。内容介绍了多种实现策略、权衡，以及实现可靠、可访问的客户端 PDF 生成为何仍然困难。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/finance/investing/polymarket-kalshi-betting-profits-prediction-markets-eb23ac11' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Almost Everyone Loses–Except a Few Sharks–On Prediction Markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在预测市场中几乎所有人都会亏损——只有少数鲨鱼盈利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48007503' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48007503' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48007503' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Most participants lose in prediction markets due to fees, slippage, and information asymmetries, while a small group captures outsized profits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预测市场的现实是多数参与者亏损，只有少数“鲨鱼”能从中获利，原因包括交易成本、滑点和信息不对称。文章讨论对市场设计与参与者风险的启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037655' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037655' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yosefk.com/blog/all-means-are-fair-except-solving-the-problem.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All means are fair except solving the problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>除了解决问题，所有手段都算正当</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037655' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post critiques over-engineered processes that stall real solutions by chasing process, rules, and convoluted debates instead of addressing the core problem. It urges pragmatism and actionable work, warning that software and system design suffer when &#x27;solving the problem&#x27; is deprioritized.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评为了避免直面核心问题而过度追求流程与规章，导致拖延解决方案。它主张务实、落地的行动，警惕在设计与实现中把‘解决问题’置于次要位置。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Instagram 将在 5 月 8 日结束加密消息功能，影响用户的隐私工作流与支持数据访问。用户应备份信息并考虑替代方案，这再次凸显隐私功能与平台策略之间的矛盾。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48042204' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48042204' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flow.byu.edu/posts/julia-c++' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Julia as Fast as C++ (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 Julia 的速度赶上 C++（2019）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48042204' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that Julia can reach C++-level speeds in hot code paths with strategies like type-stable code, careful inlining, and aggressive use of the LLVM backend. It emphasizes that parity isn’t automatic and requires careful algorithmic design and memory management. Practical takeaways include profiling workflows and when to trust Julia for systems-level tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为通过保持类型稳定、对内联的精细控制以及借助 LLVM 的优化，Julia 在热点路径上可以达到与 C++ 相近的性能。实现这种对等需要精心的算法设计、内存管理和代码风格。实用要点包括性能分析流程以及何时应信任 Julia 用于系统级任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://flow.byu.edu/images/logo.png' data-full='https://flow.byu.edu/images/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>费姆恩贝尔特隧道首个隧道单元下沉完成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050666' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The first tunnel element of the Fehmarnbelt fixed link has been lowered into place, marking a milestone in one of Europe’s largest infrastructure projects. The operation demonstrates precision engineering at scale and advances a cross‑Baltic rail and road corridor expected to boost regional freight and mobility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>费姆恩贝尔特海底隧道的首个隧道单元已成功下沉，标志着全球最大规模基础设施项目之一的关键里程碑。这一作业展示了大规模精密工程，并将推动横跨波罗的海的铁路与公路走廊，提升区域货运与出行能力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 介绍了 PHP-fts——完全用 PHP 实现的全文检索引擎，无需任何扩展。它适合在无法安装本地扩展的环境中快速嵌入到 PHP 应用，但需权衡性能等取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeutopia.net/blog/2026/05/09/you-should-read-programming-as-theory-building/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Read Programming as Theory Building</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把编程读作理论构建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48074829' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reframes programming reading as a process of building theory about computation rather than merely absorbing techniques. It suggests combining reading with hands-on experimentation and reflective critique to advance underlying concepts. The takeaway is to treat code as a vehicle for theory and to design learning around that interplay.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文将编程相关的读物视作构建计算理论的过程，而非仅仅学习技巧。它建议将阅读与动手实验和反思性批评相结合，以推动潜在概念的发展。核心观点是把代码作为理论的载体，并围绕这一关系设计学习方法。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLMorphism：当人们把自己视作语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48082021' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concept paper exploring how humans anthropomorphize language models, shaping self-perception and behavior. It discusses implications for UI design, trust, and the risk of mistaking language pattern for cognition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本论文探讨人们如何拟人化语言模型，从而影响自我认知与行为；讨论对界面设计、信任建立，以及将语言模式错当成认知的风险的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD/zaurus is highlighted in a light-hearted story about the quirks and charm of OpenBSD on the Zaurus handheld. It underscores OpenBSD’s culture of portability and tinkering, often celebrated in niche hardware communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇轻松的故事聚焦于在 Zaurus 掌上设备上运行 OpenBSD 的趣味与挑战，彰显 OpenBSD 的可移植性与折腾文化。社区对这类小众硬件的热情也体现了自由与安全优先的精神。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yibo-fu.com/SoundOff-Low-cost-Passive-Ultrasound-Tags-for-Non-invasive-and-Non' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SoundOff: Low-Cost Passive Ultrasound Tags</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038750' hidden>

<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260504-00/?p=112296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do I inform Windows that I&#x27;m writing a binary file?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何让 Windows 知道我正在写二进制文件？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48018715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48018715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48018715' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the difference between text and binary file handling on Windows and how to open files in binary mode to avoid newline translation and text processing. The post offers practical tips for ensuring binary data is written exactly as intended.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释 Windows 如何处理文本与二进制文件，以及如何以二进制模式打开文件以避免意外的换行符转译和文本处理。给出确保二进制数据按预期写入和读取的实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48019414' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48019414' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/the-hidden-mathematical-dance-inside-plant-cells-20260504/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Mathematical Dance Inside Plant Cells</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>植物细胞里的数学之舞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48019414' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quanta Magazine explains how plant cells use geometry and signaling to regulate growth and pattern formation, supported by mathematical models of cell wall mechanics and turgor pressure. The article showcases how mathematical constraints and symmetry shape cellular behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《植物细胞里的数学之舞》介绍植物细胞如何借助几何与信号调控生长与模式形成，并辅以细胞壁力学和膨压等数学模型。文章突出约束与对称性在生物学中的作用，展示数学如何指引细胞行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/ChloroplastPacking-crBenediktPleyer_NomadicNostoc_SciencePhotoLibrary-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/ChloroplastPacking-crBenediktPleyer_NomadicNostoc_SciencePhotoLibrary-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031707' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In commit messages, a discussion about whether to include a &#x27;Co-authored-by: Copilot&#x27; tag reflects evolving attribution practices for AI-assisted code, affecting licensing, credit, and contributor workflows in IDEs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于在提交信息中是否保留 ‘Co-authored-by: Copilot’ 标签的讨论，折射出对 AI 辅助代码的归属与署名的新实践，影响许可、署名与协作流程。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2024/02/the-first-microcomputer-transfluxor.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The first microcomputer: The transfluxor-powered Arma Micro Computer from 1962</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038573' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical look at the Arma Micro Computer (1962), powered by transfluxor technology, highlighting early experimentation with microcomputer concepts and the push toward smaller, more accessible computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾1962年的 Arma 微型计算机——由转流器驱动，代表早期微型计算机在器件创新与小型化方面的探索。展示了当时的设计约束和对后续计算机架构的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47988439' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(359) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thebuild.com/blog/2026/05/01/pgxbackup-continuity-support-for-pgbackrest/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>pgxbackup: Continuity Support for pgBackRest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>pgxbackup：pgBackRest 的连续性支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47988439' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>pgxbackup adds continuity support to pgBackRest, ensuring backup compatibility across PostgreSQL upgrades and patches. This reduces admin risk and improves predictability of data protection and recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目为 pgBackRest 提供连续性/兼容性支持，确保在 PostgreSQL 的升级与修补过程中备份保持可用。此举降低管理员风险，提升数据保护和恢复的可预测性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thebuild.com/media/media/noun-armchair-129971.png' data-full='https://thebuild.com/media/media/noun-armchair-129971.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kutilek.de/selectric/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reviving the IBM Selectric Composer Fonts (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>复兴 IBM Selectric Composer 字体（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48040412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48040412' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece documents reviving the IBM Selectric Composer fonts for modern use, blending historical typography with contemporary tooling. It reveals how digitized outlines and hinting capture the distinctive typesetting charm of the original.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文记录了将 IBM Selectric Composer 字体用于现代应用的复兴工作，融合了历史排版与当代工具。报道揭示了数字化轮廓和提示效果如何保留原始字体的独特排版魅力。文章强调在数字化形式中保留复古印刷美学的关注扩展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://juretriglav.si/surfel-based-global-illumination-on-the-web/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surfel-based global illumination on the web</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48077395' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates surfels-based global illumination running in the browser, enabling more realistic lighting for web apps without heavy offline rendering. This signals progress in WebGL/WebGPU and real-time graphics research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器中演示基于 Surfels 的全局光照方法，使网页应用能够实现更真实的光照效果，避免离线渲染的高成本。这体现了 WebGL/WebGPU 在实时图形领域的进展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/information-technology/2026/05/mozilla-says-271-vulnerabilities-found-by-mythos-have-almost-no-false-positives/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla says 271 vulnerabilities found by Mythos and &quot;almost no false positives&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 称 Mythos 发现 271 个漏洞几乎没有误报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48053816' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla reports that Mythos found 271 vulnerabilities with almost no false positives, highlighting the tool&#x27;s effectiveness for security testing. The claim signals confidence in automated vulnerability discovery and could influence how teams adopt AI-assisted testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 指出 Mythos 在安全测试中发现了 271 个漏洞，几乎没有误报，凸显该工具在自动化漏洞发现方面的有效性。这一说法可能影响团队对 AI 辅助测试的采用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wordtrak introduces a new word game with distinctive mechanics and an approachable online release. The post shares design decisions, core loop, and how it performs in practice, illustrating how indie developers ship tight, focused games.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47994946' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47994946' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://physicsworld.com/a/researchers-print-structural-colour-with-an-inkjet-printer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Researchers print structural colour with an inkjet printer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究人员用喷墨打印机实现结构色</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47994946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47994946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47994946' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate printing structural color—colors arising from microstructure rather than pigments—using a standard inkjet printer. This could enable cheap, scalable color engineering for displays and cosmetics, with potential for environmentally friendly pigments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示了用普通喷墨打印机实现结构色——颜色来自微结构而非颜料。此技术有望降低成本、提升尺度化颜色工程的应用，且可能更环保。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://physicsworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/1-05-26-sugimoto-inkjet-display-featured.jpg' data-full='https://physicsworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/1-05-26-sugimoto-inkjet-display-featured.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48084547' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Animated visualization of the Shunting Yard algorithm helps learners grasp how rail cars are rearranged efficiently. It&#x27;s a neat educational piece that could inspire more algorithm visualization projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对 Shunting-Yard 算法的动画可视化，帮助学习者理解列车在轨道上的高效编排过程。这是一个有趣的教育作品，或许会促使更多算法可视化项目的开展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48046023' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48046023' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/darkrishabh/agent-skills-eval' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Agent-skills-eval – Test whether Agent Skills improve outputs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Agent-skills-eval——评估技能是否提升输出</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48046023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48046023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48046023' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent-skills-eval provides a framework to test whether adding specific agent skills actually improves output quality across tasks. It benchmarks skill sets with controlled experiments and metrics, helping practitioners avoid assuming improvements without evidence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agent-skills-eval 提供一个框架，用以在受控实验中检验新增的代理技能是否确实提高输出质量。通过对比不同技能组的结果，帮助从业者避免凭直觉而非证据来判断改进效果。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zknill.io/posts/everyone-said-sse-token-streaming-was-easy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to make SSE token streams resumable, cancellable, and multi-device</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何让 SSE 令牌流具备可恢复、可取消和多设备支持</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece outlines practical patterns to make Server-Sent Events streams resumable, cancellable, and usable across multiple devices. It covers state management, signaling, and synchronization strategies that enable robust real-time streaming in web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文概述了使 Server-Sent Events 流具备可恢复、可取消以及跨多设备使用的实用模式，涵盖状态管理、信号传输与同步策略，帮助 Web 应用实现更强的实时流能力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The collection highlights quirky, impractical gadgets from Japanese culture; each item embodies the spirit of Chindogu—ingenious but not useful in daily life—and offers cultural and design insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这组收藏展示日本文化中的怪异、实用性很低的小发明；每个珍道具都体现了珍道具的精神——巧思但日常实用性有限，并提供文化与设计层面的洞见。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/environment/underwater-robot-tracks-sperm-whale-conversations-real-time-2026-05-01/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Underwater robot tracks sperm whale conversations in real time</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47998664' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An underwater robot tracks sperm whale conversations in real time, enabling researchers to analyze vocalization patterns and social interactions. The project advances marine bioacoustics and demonstrates sensor fusion in challenging environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>水下机器人实时记录抹香鲸的交流，帮助研究者分析声纳模式和社交互动。该项目推动海洋生物声学研究，并展示在极端环境下的传感器融合能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48032617' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48032617' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://microsoft.github.io/bocpy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Behavior-Oriented Concurrency for Python</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 的面向行为并发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48032617' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces Behavior-Oriented Concurrency for Python (BocPy), a model that focuses on observable behaviors to structure parallel work rather than low-level threading. The approach aims to simplify Python concurrency and improve maintainability for real-world workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 BocPy——一种以行为为核心来组织并发的 Python 框架或概念，意在简化并发编程并提升可维护性。探讨如何让实际工作负载受益于这种模型。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/29/arts/roger-sweet-dead-he-man.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roger Sweet, Creator of the He-Man Action Figure, Dies at 91</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>He-Man 动作人偶设计师 Roger Sweet 去世，享年91岁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977510' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Obituary honors Roger Sweet, who helped shape the iconic He-Man action figures and influenced 1980s toy design with his eye for character aesthetics and market appeal. His work contributed to the branding and licensing strategies that defined the era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讣告缅怀 Roger Sweet，他参与塑造了标志性的 He-Man 动作人偶，并在80年代玩具设计中以对角色美学与市场吸引力的敏锐洞察著称。其作品影响了当时的品牌与特许经营策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48047280' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48047280' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fractalbits.com/blog/remove-fsync/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Removing fsync from our local storage engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从本地存储引擎中移除 fsync</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48047280' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Removing fsync from their storage engine yielded noticeable latency and throughput gains by avoiding synchronous flushes. The move trades some durability guarantees for performance, relying on crash-consistency strategies like write-ahead logs and careful recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过移除存储引擎中的 fsync，显著降低了延迟并提升了吞吐量，避免了同步刷新带来的开销。这一改动在性能与数据耐久性之间作出权衡，依赖写前日志等崩溃一致性策略和细致的恢复流程。文中讨论了设计决策、风险以及在何种场景下该优化才有意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fractalbits.com/logo.png' data-full='https://www.fractalbits.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-gregoire-moq-msfts-00.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MPEG-2 Transport Stream Packaging for Media over QUIC Transport</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 QUIC 传输的 MPEG-2 运输流打包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48049963' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Draft proposes packaging MPEG-2 Transport Streams over QUIC to streamline media delivery with lower latency and better security over HTTP/3, useful for streaming services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>草案提出将 MPEG-2 运输流打包并通过 QUIC 传输，以在 HTTP/3 上实现更低时延和更好的安全性，适用于流媒体服务。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48068254' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48068254' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://0xkrt26.github.io/math_behind_security/2026/05/08/birthday-problem.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When is your birthday? The math behind hash collisions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你生日是哪天？哈希冲突背后的数学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48068254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48068254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48068254' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines the birthday paradox as it applies to hash collisions, explaining how collision probability grows with the number of samples and the size of the hash space. Keeps math intuitive, with practical implications for choosing hash sizes and understanding cryptographic risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以生日悖论为线索解释哈希冲突的数学原理，讲清冲突概率随样本量和哈希空间大小的关系。给出对选择哈希位数和理解密码风险的直观提示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48084350' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
