<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.taxpayeradvocate.irs.gov/news/nta-blog/tens-of-millions-of-taxpayers-may-be-eligible-for-significant-tax-refunds/2026/04/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taxpayers May Be Eligible for Significant Tax Refunds – If They Act by July 10</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>7月10日前行动，纳税人可能获得大额退税</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48084350' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Millions of taxpayers may be eligible for significant refunds due to IRS changes, but they need to act by July 10 to claim them. The post outlines who qualifies and practical steps to file or adjust returns. It also cautions about common pitfalls and scams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>许多纳税人可能因为 IRS 调整而获得可观的退税，但需在7月10日前完成申报或调整。文章列出符合条件的人群及提交或修改申报的实用步骤，并警示常见风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48041953' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48041953' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/apr/28/disneyland-entrance-facial-recognition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mickey Mouse is watching you: Disneyland deploys facial recognition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>米奇正在盯着你：迪士尼乐园部署人脸识别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48041953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48041953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48041953' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disneyland deploys facial recognition for park entry, raising privacy, consent, and data-use questions. The move highlights the tension between security and civil liberties in public spaces and may influence policy debates and guest expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迪士尼乐园在入园时部署人脸识别，带来隐私、同意与数据使用方面的问题。此举凸显公共空间安保与公民自由之间的张力，可能影响相关政策讨论和游客预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7462bde6518a5d70751171c6c3dc99b92c5c0f87/688_443_3711_2971/master/3711.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0e19476b2d747e21c6672778c233315d' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7462bde6518a5d70751171c6c3dc99b92c5c0f87/688_443_3711_2971/master/3711.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0e19476b2d747e21c6672778c233315d' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48008104' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s43016-025-01173-4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gaps in national food production, worldwide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球粮食产量的缺口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48008104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48008104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48008104' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global gaps between national food production and demand reveal vulnerabilities to climate shocks, trade disruptions, and population growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球范围内的粮食产量与需求之间存在缺口，受气候变化、贸易中断与人口增长等因素影响，暴露出全球粮食安全的脆弱性。文章讨论通过数据、政策和农业科技投资来缩小产量差距、提升韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs43016-025-01173-4/MediaObjects/43016_2025_1173_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs43016-025-01173-4/MediaObjects/43016_2025_1173_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47999391' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://marijnhaverbeke.nl/blog/collaborative-editing-cm.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Collaborative Editing in CodeMirror (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CodeMirror 的协作编辑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999391' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CodeMirror&#x27;s collaborative editing approach explores real-time co-editing in a lightweight editor, tackling conflict resolution and latency without heavy backend infrastructure. It offers practical patterns for embedding collaborative features in web IDEs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CodeMirror 的协作编辑方案探讨在轻量编辑器中实现实时协作的可行性，聚焦冲突解决与低延迟等挑战。为在浏览器中嵌入协同编辑提供了可操作的架构模式与 UX 启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48014197' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48014197' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Transformers Are Inherently Succinct (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transformer 本质上是简洁的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48014197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48014197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48014197' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New work argues that transformers encode information succinctly, with implications for model efficiency, pruning, and interpretability. The findings suggest that more compact representations could enable faster inference and easier analysis without sacrificing performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究认为 Transformer 本质上以简洁表征信息，对模型效率、剪枝与可解释性具有潜在影响。研究结果暗示更紧凑的表征或许让推理更快、分析更易进行，同时不显著降低性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It illustrates the leap from early photographic processes to modern image-centric journalism and information sharing. The piece situates this moment in the broader history of visual communication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于第一张在报纸上刊登的照片，这一历史性时刻标志着媒体技术的里程碑。它展现了从早期摄影工艺到如今以图像为核心的新闻与信息传播的跃迁，成为视觉传播史的重要节点。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多项式与线性代数的乐趣；或者，些许抽象的无厘头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47975166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47975166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以幽默笔触将多项式与线性代数的关系揭示出来，强调直观理解胜过繁琐推导。文章通过轻松风格展示代数结构在求解方程中的应用，提升对计算中数学直觉的理解。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47985932' data-tags='Startups,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47985932' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/05/startup-says-sound-waves-can-replace-fire-sprinklers-experts-arent-so-sure/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Infrasound waves stop kitchen fires, but can they replace sprinklers?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>低频声波能否替代喷淋系统？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47985932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47985932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47985932' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A startup claims infrasound waves can suppress kitchen fires, potentially replacing sprinklers; experts express skepticism about effectiveness, safety, and scalability. The topic underscores the long-standing challenge of reliable fire suppression tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家初创公司声称低频声波可扑灭厨房火灾，理论上或可替代喷淋系统；专家对效果、安全性与普适性持怀疑态度。此话题凸显了长期以来对可靠火灾抑制技术的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-1472054212-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-1472054212-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Daisy-DAG is a graph-based workflow engine for orchestrating tasks with dependencies, enabling parallel execution and pluggable backends. It targets scalable automation pipelines for data and infrastructure workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Daisy-DAG 是一个基于有向无环图的工作流引擎，用于按依赖关系编排任务，支持并行执行和可替换的后端。目标是在数据与基础设施工作负载中提供可扩展的自动化管线。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48058340' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The policy expands passport revocation for nonpayment of child support, aiming to enforce financial obligations. It raises questions about due process, potential hardship, and the data systems needed to enforce it.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a lightweight meta-harness on Islo.dev for orchestrating experiments and workflows with minimal boilerplate. The approach emphasizes reproducibility, portability, and easier experimentation in ML or data pipelines. It&#x27;s pitched as a simple, practical tool rather than a full framework.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Islo.dev 上的一个简易元控件，用于最小化样板代码地编排实验与工作流。该方法强调可重复性、可移植性，方便在机器学习或数据管道中的实验迭代，定位为实用而非完整框架。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48068947' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48068947' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flox.dev/blog/achieving-rapid-cve-remediation-in-an-era-of-escalating-vulnerabilities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Non-determinism is an issue with patching CVEs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>修补 CVE 时的非确定性问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48068947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48068947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48068947' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Non-deterministic behavior in patch deployment can cause inconsistent CVE remediation across systems, complicating verification and rollback. The piece suggests deterministic patching pipelines, reproducible builds, and thorough testing to improve remediation consistency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在打补丁的过程中，非确定性会导致不同系统的修补效果不一致，增加验证和回滚的难度。文章建议建立可重复的补丁流程、使用可重现构建和严格测试来提升修复的一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://flox.dev/images/blog/desk-with-magnifying-glass.webp' data-full='https://flox.dev/images/blog/desk-with-magnifying-glass.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48012342' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alikamp/Parks-KPBM-Scaling' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speedup in Lattice Boltzmann Cylinder Flow</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在圆柱流动中的格子玻尔兹曼法实现37倍加速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012342' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report a 37x speedup for lattice Boltzmann simulations of flow around a cylinder, enabling faster computational fluid dynamics studies. The work showcases advances in parallelization and solver efficiency for CFD workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在圆柱绕流的格子玻尔兹曼法数值模拟中实现了约37倍加速，使计算流体力学研究更高效。这项工作体现了并行化和求解器效率方面的显著进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ae4c566bbca6491741ff2ae6fdcd7fd3e01245de363840564ccdf2a2265a44a2/alikamp/Parks-KPBM-Scaling' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ae4c566bbca6491741ff2ae6fdcd7fd3e01245de363840564ccdf2a2265a44a2/alikamp/Parks-KPBM-Scaling' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48025828' data-tags='Security,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48025828' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nsa.gov/Cybersecurity/Quantum-Key-Distribution-QKD-and-Quantum-Cryptography-QC/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) and Quantum Cryptography (QC)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>量子密钥分发与量子密码学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48025828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48025828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48025828' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise overview clarifies what QKD and QC each offer. QKD enables information-theoretically secure key exchange, while QC uses quantum algorithms to strengthen cryptography. The distinction helps planners map post-quantum security strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段简要说明区分 QKD 与 QC 的要点：QKD 提供信息论层面的安全密钥交换，而 QC 则用量子算法强化密码学。这个区分帮助决策者规划后量子时代的安全策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48032393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article traces the origins of the Cyrillic script in the medieval Slavic world, highlighting its Bulgarian roots and Greek influences that enabled widespread literacy in Eastern Europe. It explains how Cyrillic shaped liturgy, culture, and modern languages across the region.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文追溯西里尔字母在中世纪斯拉夫世界的起源，强调其在保加利亚的根源以及希腊影响，推动了东欧的广泛读写能力。西里尔字母的形成深刻影响了祈祷仪式、文化及现代区域语言。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lanl.gov/media/publications/1663/from-ghost-particle-to-cosmic-messenger' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Los Alamos and the long path to detecting neutrinos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>洛斯阿拉莫斯与探测中微子之漫长征程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48036622' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece chronicles Los Alamos’ long journey in neutrino detection, illustrating how detecting these elusive particles required decades of ingenuity and large-scale experiments. Neutrinos offer deep insights into astrophysics and fundamental physics, underpinning many modern discoveries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾洛斯阿拉莫斯在中微子探测方面漫长的探索，展示了通过数十年的创新和大规模实验来捕捉这类微粒的挑战。中微子为天体物理与基础物理提供深刻见解，是现代诸多发现的基础。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/922877/homebridge-2-0-matter-update-robot-vacuums' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Homebridge 2.0 is here, and it speaks Matter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Homebridge 2.0 来了，它支持 Matter</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48010597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48010597' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Homebridge 2.0 adds Matter compatibility, enabling non-Matter devices to work with Matter ecosystems through bridging. It expands smart home interoperability and reduces vendor lock-in, while raising security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Homebridge 2.0 增添 Matter 兼容性，通过桥接让非 Matter 设备进入 Matter 生态，提升智能家居的互操作性并降低厂商锁定，但也带来安全性方面的新考量。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reidatcheson.com/sparse/linear/cholesky/2026/04/09/etree.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sparse Cholesky Elimination Tree</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48080221' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores elimination trees for sparse Cholesky factorization, detailing data structures and algorithmic ideas to reduce fill-in and memory usage in sparse linear solvers. A practical read for numerical linear algebra practitioners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍稀疏 Cholesky 的消去树（ETree），讲解用于减少填充和内存用量的数据结构与算法思想，面向稀疏线性求解器的数值线代工作者。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://therecord.media/ADT-data-breach-cyberattack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ADT says customer data stolen in cyber intrusion</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48043487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48043487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48043487' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ADT confirms a cyber intrusion with customer data theft, highlighting ongoing security risks faced by consumer-security providers. The breach underlines the need for strong encryption, credential protection, and prompt incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ADT 确认发生网络入侵，部分客户数据被窃取，凸显家居安防等领域的数据安全风险。事件凸显需要强加密、凭证保护与快速响应能力来提升信任。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47948338' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cordouan_Lighthouse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cordouan Lighthouse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科杜昂灯塔</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47948338' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cordouan Lighthouse stands at the mouth of the Gironde estuary as one of France’s oldest and architecturally distinctive lighthouses. Its construction and upkeep illustrate the evolution of maritime signaling and state power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科杜昂灯塔位于吉伦特河口，是法国最古老且建筑风格独特的灯塔之一。其建造与维护反映了航海信号技术与国家权力的演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b6/Cordouan.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b6/Cordouan.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48057842' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48057842' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-vp62-r36r-9xqp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code CVE-2026-39861:sandbox escape via symlink</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code CVE-2026-39861：通过符号链接实现的沙箱逃逸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48057842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48057842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48057842' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vulnerability in Claude Code allows sandbox escape via a symlink, exposing potential bypass of sandboxed execution. The advisory lists affected versions and mitigations; users should patch or disable affected features until fixed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 存在通过符号链接实现沙箱逃逸的漏洞（CVE-2026-39861），可能使受限执行环境被突破。公告列出受影响版本及缓解措施，建议尽快打补丁或在修复前禁用相关功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/advisory-database-show-f32c8207dfb2.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/advisory-database-show-f32c8207dfb2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48013706' data-tags='Business,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48013706' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/Google-is-discontinuing-its-free-web-search-index-for-developers-11152411.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google is discontinuing its free web search index for developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌停止向开发者提供免费网页搜索索引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48013706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48013706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48013706' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is ending free access to its web search index for developers, pushing developers toward paid APIs or alternative indexing solutions. The move affects tooling, SEO, and app features that relied on free indexing, potentially driving developers to consolidating services or reevaluating data strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌停止向开发者提供免费网页搜索索引，促使开发者转向付费 API 或其他索引方案。这一举措影响依赖免费索引的工具、SEO 与应用功能，可能促使开发者调整数据策略和服务整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://heise.cloudimg.io/bound/1200x1200/q85.png-lossy-85.webp-lossy-85.foil1/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/0/1/5/5/0/0/oss_google_analytics-45d45366348919c9.png' data-full='https://heise.cloudimg.io/bound/1200x1200/q85.png-lossy-85.webp-lossy-85.foil1/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/0/1/5/5/0/0/oss_google_analytics-45d45366348919c9.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48004734' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48004734' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/small-html-pages/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stitch together lots of little HTML pages with navigations for interactions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用大量小型 HTML 页面拼接出带导航的交互体验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48004734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48004734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48004734' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses assembling complex interactions by linking many small HTML pages rather than a single monolith. Emphasizes modularity, maintainability, and predictable behavior for simple demos or learning projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将复杂交互拆分为多张小型 HTML 页面并设置导航来实现，强调模块化、可维护性和可预测性，适用于简单演示或学习原型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/blog-llm-navigation.png' data-full='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/blog-llm-navigation.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chosker.github.io/blog/colored-shadow-penumbra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Colored Shadow Penumbra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>彩色阴影半影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48053435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48053435' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A design-focused exploration of colored shadow penumbra, blending optics, rendering, and perhaps creative coding. The piece invites readers to think about how colored lighting and layered shadows impact perception and UI aesthetics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇以彩色阴影为主题的设计论述，结合光学与渲染及创意编码的思考，讨论有色光照与分层阴影如何影响视觉感知与界面美感。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48012681' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48012681' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/05/02/local_ai_coding_agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Usage-based pricing killing your vibe, here&#x27;s how to roll your own local AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于使用量定价毁坏体验，教你搭建本地 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012681' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that usage-based pricing for cloud AI hurts user experience and costs, offering a DIY path to run local AI agents. It covers practical steps, hardware considerations, and privacy advantages of keeping models on premises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出云端 AI 的基于使用量定价会损害用户体验与增加成本，提出自建本地 AI 代理的可行路径。内容包括实际实现步骤、硬件考量以及在本地运行模型带来的隐私优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/06/28/code_shutterstock.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/06/28/code_shutterstock.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48075645' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48075645' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenewstack.io/subquadratic-12-million-context-window/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The context window has been shattered: Subquadratic debuts a 12M token window</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Subquadratic 公布 12M token 的上下文窗口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48075645' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Subquadratic debuts a 12M token context window, enabling long-context processing beyond standard transformer limits. The development could impact training efficiency and long-document understanding in future LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Subquadratic 公布了 12M token 的上下文窗口，突破了标准 Transformer 的上下文容量限制。此进展对未来大模型在长文本处理、训练效率和延展能力方面具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.thenewstack.io/media/2026/05/d2090430-screenshot-2026-05-05-at-10.02.53.png' data-full='https://cdn.thenewstack.io/media/2026/05/d2090430-screenshot-2026-05-05-at-10.02.53.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48084131' data-tags='Energy,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48084131' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/oil-price-bets-ahead-iran-war-news-totalled-7-billion-reporting-shows-2026-05-07/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oil-price bets ahead of Iran war news totalled $7B, reporting shows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在伊朗战争消息面前的油价赌注高达七亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48084131' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oil price bets ahead of potential Iran war news totaled around $7 billion, signaling traders&#x27; expectations of supply risk and geopolitical risk premiums. The figure underlines how geopolitics can rapidly influence energy markets and hedging strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在伊朗可能爆发冲突前，油价赌注总额大约为70亿美元，反映交易员对供应风险和地缘风险溢价的预期。这一数字凸显地缘政治如何迅速影响能源市场和对冲策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48073978' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48073978' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/25/health/pepfar-hiv-aids-zambia.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aids Creeps Back in Parts of Zambia, a Year After U.S. Cuts to HIV Assistance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>赞比亚在美国 HIV 援助削减后部分地区艾滋病再度蔓延</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073978' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After US cuts to HIV aid, certain regions of Zambia see a relapse in AIDS-related health challenges, impacting treatment access and health outcomes. The report highlights disruptions in treatment access, supply chains, and health data systems amid funding shifts. It underscores how international policy changes directly affect on-the-ground health tech and data systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国削减 HIV 援助后，赞比亚部分地区再度出现艾滋病相关健康挑战，影响治疗获取与健康结局。报道指出治疗获取、物资供应链及健康数据系统因资金变动而受影响，凸显国际政策变化对本地健康科技与数据系统的直接影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48038322' data-tags='Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48038322' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://portalvr.io/motion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PortalVR Motion – use any VR content in 2D with 3D tracked Joy-Cons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PortalVR Motion：用追踪 Joy-Cons 将任意 VR 内容在 2D 显示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038322' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PortalVR Motion lets you play VR content in 2D by using 3D-tracked Joy-Cons, enabling broader accessibility and quick prototyping of VR experiences. It demonstrates how motion-tracking peripherals can unlock unconventional display formats and testing workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PortalVR Motion 通过 3D 跟踪的 Joy-Cons 让任意 VR 内容在 2D 显示，带来更广泛的可访问性和快速原型测试的可能。此类外设与追踪技术的组合，展示了在显示格式与测试工作流上的创新空间。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://portalvr.io/assets/images/motion-og.jpg' data-full='https://portalvr.io/assets/images/motion-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48039910' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48039910' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/science/story/2026-05-06/rfk-hands-major-win-to-indoor-tanning-industry-to-dermatologists-dismay' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFK Jr. clears path for minors to use tanning beds, much to dermatologist dismay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFK Jr. 为未成年人使用日晒浴床铺平道路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48039910' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFK Jr. advocates loosening restrictions on tanning bed use for minors, drawing strong disapproval from dermatologists who warn about skin cancer risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFK Jr. 公布一项主张，试图放宽未成年人使用日晒浴床的限制，皮肤科医生对此表示强烈反对，警示皮肤癌风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/784201e/2147483647/strip/true/crop/6720x3528+0+476/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F2c%2F45%2F0d14ff5f4d6889fb8637ee932b53%2F1426446-me-0330-rfk-cesar-chavez-gem-004.jpg' data-full='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/784201e/2147483647/strip/true/crop/6720x3528+0+476/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F2c%2F45%2F0d14ff5f4d6889fb8637ee932b53%2F1426446-me-0330-rfk-cesar-chavez-gem-004.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48021240' data-tags='Security'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48065203' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GETadb.com is a tiny service that records every HTTP GET request as a database row, enabling instant, auditable traces of visited routes. The idea showcases how simple endpoints can enable powerful visibility into web traffic and can inspire lightweight observability tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GETadb.com 是一个小型服务，通过将每次 HTTP GET 请求写入数据库实现对访问路径的可审计追踪，展示了简单端点也能带来强大的可观测性，并可能成为轻量级观测工具的灵感。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Martin-Löf 类型理论中的扩展可预测 Mahlo 宇（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47996395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47996395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47996395' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The extended predicative Mahlo universe in Martin-Löf type theory surveys a sophisticated universe construction and its implications for foundations of mathematics and constructive verification. The work informs how type-theoretic universes can encode large-cardinal-like structures and influence formal proof systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Martin-Löf 型理论中扩展的可预测 Mahlo 宇进行讨论，涉及基础数学与构造性验证的影响。该研究揭示类型理论宇宙如何编码类似大基数的结构并影响形式化证明系统。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48088343' data-tags='Health,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48088343' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260508-parents-in-ancient-times-felt-less-sleep-deprived-what-our-ancestors-did-differently-on-baby-sleep' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why modern parents feel more sleep deprived than our ancestors did</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代父母为何比祖辈更易感到睡眠不足</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48088343' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern parenting comes with around-the-clock connectivity, unpredictable infant sleep patterns, and heightened social expectations, all of which erode nightly rest. Researchers point to circadian disruption from screens, work culture, and sleep debt carried across generations as a driver. The story matters for developers of parenting tech and designers of workplaces that support caregivers, as sleep shapes cognitive function, mood, and long-term health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代育儿伴随着24小时连接、不可预测的婴儿睡眠模式和更高的社会期望，导致夜间休息被侵蚀。研究指出屏幕引发的昼夜节律干扰、工作文化和跨代累积的睡眠债务，是睡眠不足的关键因素。这一议题对育儿科技开发者和为照护者设计工作场所的人尤为重要，因为睡眠状况直接影响认知功能、情绪与长期健康。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nk42d7.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nk42d7.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adaptivesoftware.substack.com/p/the-wrapper-and-the-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple is enforcing an old App Store rule against a new kind of software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果对新型软件执行老规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48042099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48042099' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple is applying an old App Store rule to a new software model, constraining apps that rely on wrappers or interpreters to download code at runtime. The move suggests Apple maintains strict controls on how code can be loaded and executed within apps, impacting developers exploring new distribution patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果对新型软件模型执行长期存在的 App Store 规则，针对依赖包装器或运行时下载代码的应用。此举表明苹果对应用内代码加载与执行的规则保持严格，或对探索新型分发模式的开发者带来影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/06/some-kids-are-bypassing-age-verification-checks-with-a-fake-mustache/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Some kids are bypassing age verification checks with a fake mustache</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一些孩子用假胡子绕过年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037465' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some kids are bypassing age gates by using a fake mustache to trick visual checks. The report highlights how superficial verification methods can be easily fooled and underscores the need for stronger, multi-factor safeguards on platforms that restrict age-restricted content. The takeaway: age verification remains a vulnerability and requires more robust, privacy-conscious design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些孩子用假胡子骗过年龄验证，暴露了基于视觉的简单检查的薄弱。报道强调此类验证的易被欺骗，并呼吁在限制年龄内容的平台引入更强的多因素防护。核心要点是：年龄识别仍是个漏洞，需要更稳健且注重隐私的设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/08/business/europe-solar-panels-iran-war.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Energy Prices Are Driving Demand for Solar Panels and Heat Pumps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>能源价格推动太阳能与热泵需求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48089641' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rising energy prices are fueling demand for solar panels and heat pumps, as households and businesses seek energy independence and efficiency, with implications for markets and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>能源价格上涨推动家庭与企业对太阳能板和热泵的需求增长，推动能源独立与高效系统的采用，同时影响市场与相关政策走向。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48041009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/05/mits-virtual-violin-offers-luthiers-a-new-design-tool/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MIT’s virtual violin offers luthiers a new design tool</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MIT 的虚拟小提琴为制琴师提供新设计工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48041009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48041009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48041009' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MIT unveils a virtual violin design tool that lets luthiers explore geometry and acoustics before building physical prototypes. The tool accelerates iteration, helps compare wood choices and resonant cavity shapes, and illustrates how digital modeling can transform traditional craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MIT 推出一款虛擬小提琴設計工具，讓制琴師在動手製作前就能測試音色與共鳴特性。透過數位建模，設計迭代速度更快，能比較木材、共鳴箱與琴橋等變化對音色的影響，顯示數字設計在傳統工藝中的應用前景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/stradTOP-1152x648-1777837031.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/stradTOP-1152x648-1777837031.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48073769' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48073769' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pv-magazine.com/2026/05/04/solar-on-canals-reduces-water-evaporation-by-70-and-algae-growth-by-85/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solar on canals reduces water evaporation by 70% and algae growth by 85%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>运河上太阳能板降低蒸发和藻类生长</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073769' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar canopies installed on canal networks deliver renewable power while reducing water evaporation by about 70% and algae growth by about 85%, providing a dual-use infrastructure solution. The approach demonstrates scalable benefits for climate resilience and water management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在运河网络上安装的太阳能顶篷在提供清洁能源的同时，将水蒸发量约降低70%、藻类生长约减少85%，实现了双用途基础设施。此做法展示了在气候韧性与水资源管理方面的可扩展收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Screenshot-2026-05-04-122108.png' data-full='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Screenshot-2026-05-04-122108.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0022316626001902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Egg Intake and the Incidence of Alzheimer&#x27;s Disease in Adventist Health Study-2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安息日健康研究2中的蛋类摄入与阿尔茨海默病发病率关系</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038873' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Report from Adventist Health Study-2 on egg consumption and Alzheimer&#x27;s disease incidence, noting associations and the caveats about causality and confounding dietary patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自安息日健康研究2的研究报告显示蛋类摄入与阿尔茨海默病发病率之间存在相关性，但强调相关性不等于因果关系，并需考虑饮食模式的混杂因素。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theassemblync.com/news/business/american-efficient-ferc-durham-fine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feds Fine Durham Energy Efficiency Co $722M</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦监管机构对 Durham 能效公司罚款 7.22 亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48004992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48004992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48004992' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Federal regulators fined Durham Energy Efficiency $722 million over alleged compliance issues in an energy efficiency program. The case underscores the importance of accurate efficiency claims and the regulatory risk faced program implementers, with implications for future incentives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦监管机构对 Durham 能效公司处以 7.22 亿美元罚款，源于对能效项目合规性与声明真实性的质疑。此案凸显准确宣称效果的重要性，以及项目实施方在未来激励措施中的监管风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.theassemblync.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/AP25111623944517-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.theassemblync.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/AP25111623944517-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48062606' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pankajpipada.com/posts/2026-05-08-human-habits-tokens/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human typing habits and token counts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类打字习惯与令牌数量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48062606' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyzes how human typing habits map to token counts in prompts and model inputs, offering practical guidance on prompting efficiency and cost control. Highlights the gap between perception of length and actual token usage in LLM workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析人类打字习惯与提示、输入中的令牌数量之间的关系，为提升提示效率和成本控制提供实用建议。指出感知长度与实际令牌数量之间的差异，以及在 LLM 工作流中的应用要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48045067' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(464) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smbc-comics.com/comic/2011-09-08' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We programmed a program to program new programs (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们编程了一个程序去编程新程序（2011）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48045067' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nostalgic webcomic illustrating a meta-programming joke: a program that writes programs, which in turn write other programs. Highlights how recursion and self-reference appear in programming humor and thought experiments about automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这幅漫画以元编程为梗，描绘一个程序用来写新的程序，形成自指与递归的幽默情景。体现了自动化、自我改写在程序设计领域的长期趣味与思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.smbc-comics.com/comics/20110908.gif' data-full='https://www.smbc-comics.com/comics/20110908.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48035677' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/sqliteai/adam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Adam – An embeddable cross-platform AI agent library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Adam — 一个可嵌入的跨平台 AI 代理库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48035677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48035677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48035677' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adam is an embeddable AI agent library designed for cross-platform use, enabling developers to ship autonomous agents inside their apps. It emphasizes lightweight integration, tooling for agents, and a plugin-friendly architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Adam 是一个可嵌入的跨平台人工智能代理库，便于开发者在应用中集成自主代理。强调轻量级集成、代理工具链和插件式架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2dd1593acd4aef14049a7934f6bb94bae6473a3a35cc766e9e8c7ab86e2173bd/sqliteai/adam' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2dd1593acd4aef14049a7934f6bb94bae6473a3a35cc766e9e8c7ab86e2173bd/sqliteai/adam' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48076009' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48076009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stoffelmpc.com/stoffel-blog/beaver-triples-tuples' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introduction to Beaver Triples</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Beaver 三元组入门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48076009' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An introductory guide to Beaver triples, a cryptographic primitive used for secure multiparty computation to enable efficient multiplication of secret shares. The post explains concept, construction, and why Beaver triples matter for privacy-preserving computations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Beaver 三元组是一种用于安全多方计算的加密原语，可高效实现秘密共享下的乘法运算。文中讲解其基本原理、构造方法以及在隐私计算中的应用意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/iQ8PDzJzneZ3WrZuvf0AH1ODJVg.jpg?width=1280&amp;height=719' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/iQ8PDzJzneZ3WrZuvf0AH1ODJVg.jpg?width=1280&amp;height=719' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48036356' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48036356' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tieknots.how/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Random tie knots (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2014 年的随机领带结集合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48036356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cs.stanford.edu/people/eroberts/cs181/projects/productivity-paradox/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The IT Productivity Paradox</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056740' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This exploration delves into the IT productivity paradox—the disconnect between IT spending and measured productivity gains. It covers measurement challenges, evolving IT value, and how to better quantify impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨 IT 生产力悖论，即 IT 投入与生产力提升之间的错配，分析衡量挑战、IT 价值的演变以及如何更准确地量化影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48031436' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rMi1dIqMwNw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Defeating Works by Design&#x27;s Unpickable Lock [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>破解 Works by Design 的“不可解锁”锁 [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48031436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48031436' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video demonstration challenges a puzzle-like lock marketed as unpickable, illustrating how design choices can be bypassed with clever reasoning or tooling. The content offers lessons on security-by-obscurity vs. verifiable robustness and the importance of rigorous testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频演示挑战一个被标榜为“不可解锁”的锁，展示通过巧妙推理或工具绕过设计选择的可能性。内容强调“以花招掩盖安全性”与可验证鲁棒性之间的取舍，以及严格测试的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48049343' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mbmccoy.dev/posts/buffons-noodle/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Buffon&#x27;s Needle to Buffon&#x27;s Noodle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从布丰针到布丰面条</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48049343' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Expands Buffon’s needle idea into an approachable noodle-based experiment, showing how simple probabilistic reasoning can yield elegant estimates for pi. The piece blends math history with hands-on exploration to teach geometry and randomness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将布丰针问题的思路用于用面条进行的直观实验，展示简单的概率推理如何给出对圆周率的优美估算。文章将数学史与动手探究结合，帮助读者理解几何与随机性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mbmccoy.dev/images/buffons-noodle/noodle-drop.png' data-full='https://mbmccoy.dev/images/buffons-noodle/noodle-drop.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48068475' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48068475' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/V4bel/dirtyfrag' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dirty Frag: Universal Linux LPE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dirty Frag：通用的 Linux 本地提权漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48068475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48068475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48068475' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing Dirty Frag, a demonstration of a universal Linux local privilege escalation technique, illustrating how flexible bug chaining can yield root access from unprivileged code. The work underlines persistent kernel vulnerabilities and the need for hardened kernel configs, prompt patches, and defense-in-depth in Linux deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dirty Frag 展示了一种通用的 Linux 本地提权技术，演示了如何通过漏洞链从普通用户获得 root。该工作凸显了内核层漏洞的持续风险，强调在 Linux 系统中需要更强的内核加固、及时修补与防御深度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/507935b60863da955d448e19887e4208dd36e0dc196adc137d3c70b0b911d6d4/V4bel/dirtyfrag' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/507935b60863da955d448e19887e4208dd36e0dc196adc137d3c70b0b911d6d4/V4bel/dirtyfrag' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48045205' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48045205' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/hackernoon/introducing-immer-immutability-the-easy-way-9d73d8f71cb3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Immer: Immutability the easy way (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Immer：让不可变性更简单（2018）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48045205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48045205' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Immer provides a simple way to work with immutable state in JavaScript by letting you write code that mutates a draft state, while producing an immutable next state automatically. This reduces boilerplate in state management (e.g., Redux) and improves DX, but introduces some runtime cost and caveats about end-state stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Immer 通过在草稿状态上进行变更并自动生成不可变的下一状态，为 JavaScript 的不可变数据提供了简便路径。它能显著减少状态管理中的样板代码、提升开发体验，但也带来一定的运行时成本与对最终状态稳定性的注意事项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*bZ2J4iIpsm2lMG4ZoXcj3A.png' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*bZ2J4iIpsm2lMG4ZoXcj3A.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48069354' data-tags='Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48069354' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/trump-doge-humanities-funding-cuts-dda1383436c41be08da3bbf7cc08818e' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge rules DOGE cancellation of humanities grants was unconstitutional</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官裁定 DOGE 取消人文类拨款被判违宪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48069354' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48069354' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48069354' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court ruled that the DOGE&#x27;s cancellation of humanities grants was unconstitutional, potentially requiring restoration of funding and setting a precedent on public or quasi-public funding decisions. The decision invites scrutiny of political interference in arts and humanities funding and how such actions can face legal challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定 DOGE 取消人文类拨款的行为违宪，可能要求恢复拨款并对公共资金决策设立先例。此判决引发对政治干预艺术与人文资助的审视，以及此类举动如何面临法律挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/1c00acc/2147483647/strip/true/crop/6000x3998+0+1/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fd9%2Fbf%2Fdc0647cb38ff6c3016b3c75b79f2%2F5d3e88baec4c4878b1272b7977101d65' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/1c00acc/2147483647/strip/true/crop/6000x3998+0+1/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fd9%2Fbf%2Fdc0647cb38ff6c3016b3c75b79f2%2F5d3e88baec4c4878b1272b7977101d65' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48063601' data-tags='Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48063601' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1071775/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Four stable kernels with partial fixes for Dirty Frag</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>四个稳定内核对 Dirty Frag 的部分修复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48063601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48063601' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The kernel maintainers released four stable builds with partial fixes for a vulnerability nicknamed Dirty Frag. The patches reduce exposure, but a full fix may come in later updates; users should stay current.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内核维护者推出四个稳定版本，包含对名为 Dirty Frag 的漏洞的部分修复。这些修补降低了风险，但完整修复可能在后续更新中发布，用户应保持更新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48039150' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48039150' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://modal.com/blog/boosting-multimodal-inference-performance-by-greater-than-10-with-a-single-python-dictionary' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Boosting multimodal inference performance by &gt;10% with a single Python dict</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仅用一个 Python 字典提升多模态推理性能超 10%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48039150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48039150' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A single Python dictionary can yield more than 10% gains in multimodal inference, illustrating how lightweight data organization and caching strategies can unlock meaningful efficiency. The takeaway for ML engineers is that small, clever data-structuring tweaks can complement hardware and model optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用一个 Python 字典就能让多模态推理性能提升超过 10%，体现了数据组织与缓存策略对性能的直接影响。对 ML 工程师而言，微小但巧妙的数据结构优化可与硬件及模型优化形成协同效应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://modal.com/docs/social-image.png?title=Boosting+multimodal+inference+performance+by+%3E10%25+with+a+single+Python+dictionary&amp;socialType=blog' data-full='https://modal.com/docs/social-image.png?title=Boosting+multimodal+inference+performance+by+%3E10%25+with+a+single+Python+dictionary&amp;socialType=blog' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48078009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(482) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.goto10retro.com/p/inside-the-atari-800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Atari 800 – By Paul Lefebvre</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Atari 800 – 作者 Paul Lefebvre</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48078009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48078009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48078009' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Lefebvre&#x27;s piece on the Atari 800 delves into its hardware, design decisions, and historical significance in early home computing. It offers retro insights that resonate with modern hardware enthusiasts and software preservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Paul Lefebvre 的文章带你回顾 Atari 800 的硬件结构、设计选择及其在早期家庭计算中的重要性。内容结合怀旧与对现代硬件爱好者及软件保护的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!zyYy!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1f9e90ce-30e1-4e95-94f3-655c48986cf8.heic' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!zyYy!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1f9e90ce-30e1-4e95-94f3-655c48986cf8.heic' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48072315' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48072315' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Poka-yoke' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Poka-Yoke</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Poka‑Yoke：防错设计的艺术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48072315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48072315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48072315' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Poka-yoke introduces simple, domain-specific safeguards to prevent human error in manufacturing and design. In software and UX, the principle translates to lockouts, validation, and fail-safes that reduce mistakes before they happen.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Poka‑Yoke 强调通过简单的、领域特定的防错设计来防止人为错误。在软件与用户体验中，这一原则被转化为锁定、校验与容错等手段，以在错误发生前降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' data-full='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
